    const now = new Date();
    const h = String(now.getHours()).padStart(2, '0');
    const m = String(now.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0');
    const ts = h + ':' + m;
    document.querySelectorAll('.status-bar .time').forEach(function(el) {
      el.textContent = ts;
    });
  }
  _updateStatusBarTime();
  setInterval(_updateStatusBarTime, 30000);
})();

  const dot = document.createElement('div');
  dot.className = 'screen-dot' + (i === 0 ? ' active' : '');

  dot.onclick = () => gotoIndex(i);
  list.appendChild(dot);
});

function goto(id) {
  // 2026-05嚗BTI ?撌脣??剁?隞颱? goto('screen-mbti') 銝敺?撠???行郭閮剖???  if (id === 'screen-mbti') id = 'screen-connect';
  // 瘥活???恍?賢撥?嗆??文?賣??? overlay嚗甇?Android 皜脫? bug 撠??憭望?
  _forceCloseAllOverlays();
  // ?脣?餃?恍???憛思?甈∠?亦????Ⅳ
  if (id === 'screen-login') {
    try {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem('saved_login_phone');
      if (saved) {
        const el = document.getElementById('login-phone-input');
        if (el && !el.value) el.value = saved;
      }
    } catch (e) {}
  }
  const idx = screens.findIndex(s => s.id === id);
  if (idx >= 0) gotoIndex(idx);
}

function gotoIndex(idx) {
  document.getElementById(screens[current].id).classList.remove('active');
  document.querySelectorAll('.screen-dot')[current].classList.remove('active');
  current = idx;
  document.getElementById(screens[current].id).classList.add('active');
  document.querySelectorAll('.screen-dot')[current].classList.add('active');

  // ?脣?炎皜祉?????
  //   1. ?甈⊥???行郭憛恍脣? chip / bw-row嚗?隞?神甇餌? demo ?詨?嚗?  //   2. ?芸???摰?勗? AI ??嚗??臭遙??+ SSE ?脣漲嚗?  //   ?? 雿瘝????瘜Ｚ???敹???銝阡＊蝷箝??唳炎皜研?閰望?
  if (screens[current].id === 'screen-results') {
    try {
      if (typeof _renderResultsFromEeg === 'function') _renderResultsFromEeg();
    } catch (e) { console.error('憛怠?行郭憭望?', e); }

    if (!_rgJobId) {
      const bwCheck = _validateBrainwaveCapture(window._lastEegCapture);
      if (!bwCheck.ok) {
        // 銝??????頝喳?Ⅱ?航炊撠店獢?        _showRedetectModal(bwCheck.reason);
        return;  // ?? ?餅?敺?瘚?
      }
      if (typeof startFullReportGeneration === 'function') {
        try {
          startFullReportGeneration({
            brainwaveData: window._lastEegCapture,
          });
        } catch (e) { console.error('???勗???憭望?', e); }
      }
    }
  }
  const _t = document.getElementById('screen-title'); if(_t) _t.textContent = screens[current].label;

  // ?脣?孵??恍??????hook
  const id = screens[current].id;
  if (id === 'screen-mbti' && typeof _resetMBTI === 'function') {
    _resetMBTI();
  }
  if (id === 'screen-subject') {
    if (typeof updateSubjectAge === 'function') updateSubjectAge();
    if (typeof refreshSubjectsDropdown === 'function') refreshSubjectsDropdown();
  }
  if (id === 'screen-home'     && typeof refreshHomeData     === 'function') refreshHomeData();
  if (id === 'screen-settings' && typeof refreshSettingsData === 'function') refreshSettingsData();
  if (id === 'screen-history'  && typeof refreshHistoryData  === 'function') refreshHistoryData();
  if (id === 'screen-connect'  && typeof _populateConnectScreen === 'function') _populateConnectScreen();
  if (id === 'screen-detect'   && typeof _startEegLiveCapture   === 'function') _startEegLiveCapture();
  if (id === 'screen-reports'  && typeof _applyRolePricing      === 'function') _applyRolePricing();
}

// ??? 擐? / 閮剖???靘?亥??＊蝷?????????????????????????????????????????
async function refreshHomeData() {
  const nameEl = document.getElementById('home-name-display');
  const valEl  = document.getElementById('home-stat-value');
  const listEl = document.getElementById('home-recent-list');

  const user = _currentUser || _getAuth();
  if (nameEl) {
    if (user && user.name) {
      const role = (user.role === 'admin') ? ' 繚 蝞∠??? : ' 憿批?';
      nameEl.textContent = `${user.name}${role}`;
    } else {
      nameEl.textContent = '??憿批?';
    }
  }

  // ?撌梧????admin嚗? subjects
  if (!user || !user.token) {
    if (valEl) valEl.textContent = '隢??餃';
    if (listEl) listEl.innerHTML = '';
    return;
  }
  try {
    const r = await apiFetch('/subjects');
    if (!r.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${r.status}`);
    const subs = await r.json();
    if (valEl) valEl.textContent = `${subs.length} 雿;

    if (listEl) {
      if (subs.length === 0) {
        listEl.innerHTML = `<div class="history-item" style="text-align:center;color:#888;">
          撠?葫??暺??嫘?憪瑼Ｘ葫?遣蝡洵銝蝑???
        </div>`;
      } else {
        listEl.innerHTML = subs.slice(0, 6).map(s => {
          const age = _calcAgeStr(s.birth_date);
          const created = s.created_at ? s.created_at.replace('T', ' ').slice(5, 16) : '';
          return `<div class="history-item">
            <div class="hi-top">
              <span class="hi-name">${s.name}</span>
              <span class="tag-status" style="background:#e8f5e9;color:#2e7d32;padding:4px 10px;border-radius:10px;font-size:11px;font-weight:600;">${age||''}甇?${s.gender||''}</span>
            </div>
            ${s.occupation ? `<div class="hi-tags"><span class="tag" style="background:#e3f2fd;color:#1565c0;">? ${s.occupation}</span></div>` : ''}
            <div style="font-size:11px;color:#bbb;margin-top:6px;">撱箸? ${created}</div>
          </div>`;
        }).join('');
      }
    }
  } catch (e) {
    if (valEl) valEl.textContent = '??;
    if (listEl) listEl.innerHTML = `<div class="history-item" style="color:#c62828;">頛憭望?嚗?{e.message}</div>`;
  }
}

// ??? 隞狡蝝???亦?蝝啁?嚗???頛 payments/my ??????????????????????????
async function refreshEarningsData() {
  const listEl  = document.getElementById('earnings-list');
  const totalEl = document.getElementById('earnings-total');
  const mCntEl  = document.getElementById('earnings-month-count');
  const tCntEl  = document.getElementById('earnings-total-count');
  if (!listEl) return;

  listEl.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:#bbb;font-size:13px;">頛銝凌?/div>';

  const user = _currentUser || _getAuth();
  if (!user || !user.token) {
    listEl.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align:center;padding:40px;color:#bbb;">隢??餃</div>';
    return;
  }

  let payments = [];
  try {
    const r = await apiFetch('/payments/my?limit=100');
    if (r.ok) payments = (await r.json()).payments || [];
  } catch (e) {
    listEl.innerHTML = `<div style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:#c62828;font-size:13px;">頛憭望?嚗?{e.message}</div>`;
    return;
  }

  // ?? 蝯梯? ??
  const paidList = payments.filter(p => p.status === 'paid');
  const nowMs = Date.now();
  const monthStart = new Date(); monthStart.setDate(1); monthStart.setHours(0,0,0,0);
  const monthPaid = paidList.filter(p => (p.paid_at || 0) * 1000 >= monthStart.getTime());
  const monthTotal = monthPaid.reduce((s, p) => s + (p.amount || 0), 0);

  if (totalEl) totalEl.textContent = `NT$${monthTotal.toLocaleString()}`;
  if (mCntEl)  mCntEl.textContent  = `${monthPaid.length} 隞跆;
  if (tCntEl)  tCntEl.textContent  = `${paidList.length} 蝑;

  // ?? ?∟?????
  if (payments.length === 0) {
    listEl.innerHTML = `
      <div style="text-align:center;padding:50px 16px;color:#bbb;">
        <div style="font-size:48px;margin-bottom:10px;">?</div>
        <div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:600;color:#666;">撠隞狡蝝??/div>
        <div style="font-size:12px;margin-top:6px;line-height:1.6;">摰?擐??行郭瑼Ｘ葫隞狡敺?<br>蝝???芸?憿舐內?券ㄐ</div>
      </div>`;
    return;
  }

  // ?? ?勗?憿?璅惜 ??
  const _rtTag = (rt) => {
    const m = {
      life_trial: { label:'鈭箇??繚擃???, bg:'#f3e5f5', color:'#6a1b9a' },
      life_full:  { label:'鈭箇??繚摰??, bg:'#ede7f6', color:'#4527a0' },
      life_vip:   { label:'鈭箇??繚VIP??,  bg:'#f3e5f5', color:'#6a1b9a' },
      child_trial:{ label:'?咱憭抵釵繚擃???, bg:'#e3f2fd', color:'#1565c0' },
      child_full: { label:'?咱憭抵釵繚摰??, bg:'#e3f2fd', color:'#1565c0' },
      child_vip:  { label:'?咱憭抵釵繚VIP??,  bg:'#e3f2fd', color:'#1565c0' },
      test_1:     { label:'NT$1?皜祈岫',     bg:'#fff3e0', color:'#e65100' },
    };
    const t = m[rt] || { label: rt || '??, bg:'#f5f5f5', color:'#666' };
    return `<span style="background:${t.bg};color:${t.color};padding:2px 8px;border-radius:8px;font-size:11px;">${t.label}</span>`;
  };

  const _statusTag = (s) => {
    const m = {
      paid:    { label:'??撌脖?甈?, bg:'#e8f5e9', color:'#2e7d32' },
      pending: { label:'??敺?甈?, bg:'#fff8e1', color:'#f57c00' },
      expired: { label:'??撌脤暹?', bg:'#f5f5f5', color:'#999'    },
      failed:  { label:'??憭望?',   bg:'#ffebee', color:'#c62828' },
      refunded:{ label:'?抬? ?甈?,  bg:'#f3e5f5', color:'#6a1b9a' },
    };
    const t = m[s] || { label: s, bg:'#f5f5f5', color:'#666' };
    return `<span style="background:${t.bg};color:${t.color};padding:2px 8px;border-radius:8px;font-size:11px;font-weight:600;">${t.label}</span>`;
  };

  // ?? 皜脫? ??
  let html = `<div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:600;color:#333;margin-bottom:12px;">瘥?隞狡蝝??/div>`;
  payments.forEach(p => {
    const dateStr = _formatTs(p.created_at);
    const paidStr = p.paid_at ? _formatTs(p.paid_at) : null;
    const invoiceLine = p.invoice_no
      ? `<div style="font-size:11px;color:#888;margin-top:2px;">?潛巨嚗?{p.invoice_no}</div>` : '';
    html += `
      <div style="background:white;border-radius:12px;padding:14px;box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.06);margin-bottom:10px;${p.status!=='paid'?'opacity:0.75;':''}">
        <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:flex-start;">
          <div style="flex:1;min-width:0;">
            <div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:600;color:#1a1a2e;">${p.subject_name || '??}</div>
            <div style="font-size:11px;color:#aaa;margin-top:2px;">${dateStr}${paidStr && paidStr !== dateStr ? ' 繚 隞狡 ' + paidStr : ''}</div>
            ${invoiceLine}
          </div>
          <div style="text-align:right;margin-left:10px;">
            <div style="font-size:16px;font-weight:700;color:${p.status==='paid'?'#2e7d32':'#bbb'};">NT$${(p.amount||0).toLocaleString()}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div style="margin-top:8px;height:1px;background:#f5f5f5;"></div>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;margin-top:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;">
          ${_rtTag(p.report_type)}
          ${_statusTag(p.status)}
        </div>
      </div>`;
  });
  listEl.innerHTML = html;
}

// ??? 甇瑕蝝????頛閰脣董??瑼Ｘ葫?湔活 ?????????????????????????????????
let _historyFilter = 'all';     // all / adult / child / relation / week
let _historyCache  = [];        // 敺?蝡臬?????鞈?

const _HISTORY_PRICE_MAP = {
  // session.report_type ???摯??嚗＊蝷箇嚗鞈???憿舐內嚗?  // 瘜冽?嚗B ?芸? adult/child嚗蝝啣? trial/full/vip嚗?甇斗銝＊蝷粹?憿?};

function _formatTs(epochSec) {
  if (!epochSec) return '??;
  const d = new Date(epochSec * 1000);
  const pad = n => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${d.getFullYear()}/${pad(d.getMonth()+1)}/${pad(d.getDate())} ${pad(d.getHours())}:${pad(d.getMinutes())}`;
}

function _historyReportLabel(rt) {
  switch (rt) {
    case 'adult':    return { name: '?行郭??鈭箇??', cls: 'tag-purple', icon: '?' };
    case 'child':    return { name: '?咱?行郭憭抵釵閫?Ⅳ', cls: 'tag-blue',   icon: '??' };
    case 'relation': return { name: '?行郭?望???勗?', cls: 'tag-green',  icon: '??' };
    default:         return { name: rt || '瑼Ｘ葫', cls: 'tag-purple', icon: '??' };
  }
}

function setHistoryFilter(filter, btn) {
  _historyFilter = filter;
  document.querySelectorAll('#screen-history .filter-chip').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
  if (btn) btn.classList.add('active');
  _renderHistoryList();
}

function _renderHistoryList() {
  const c = document.getElementById('history-list-container');
  if (!c) return;

  // 憟蝭拚
  const nowSec = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  const weekAgo = nowSec - 7 * 86400;
  const filtered = _historyCache.filter(s => {
    if (_historyFilter === 'all')   return true;
    if (_historyFilter === 'week')  return (s.created_at || 0) >= weekAgo;
    return (s.report_type || 'adult') === _historyFilter;
  });

  if (filtered.length === 0) {
    c.innerHTML = `
      <div style="text-align:center;padding:60px 16px;color:#bbb;">
        <div style="font-size:48px;margin-bottom:10px;">?</div>
        <div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:600;">撠瑼Ｘ葫蝝??/div>
        <div style="font-size:12px;margin-top:6px;line-height:1.5;">摰?銝蝑瘜Ｘ炎皜砍?嚗?br>閮???＊蝷箏?ㄐ</div>
      </div>`;
    return;
  }

  // 靘僑??蝯?  const groups = {};
  filtered.forEach(s => {
    const d = s.created_at ? new Date(s.created_at * 1000) : new Date();
    const key = `${d.getFullYear()}撟?{d.getMonth()+1}?;
    (groups[key] = groups[key] || []).push(s);
  });

  let html = '';
  Object.keys(groups).forEach(monthKey => {
    html += `<div style="font-size:12px;color:#888;margin:8px 0 8px;font-weight:500;">${monthKey}</div>`;
    groups[monthKey].forEach(s => {
      const lbl = _historyReportLabel(s.report_type);
      const isSuccess = s.status === 1;
      const reportDone = s.report_status === 'completed' && s.report_url;
      let statusTag, extraNote = '', reportTagsHtml = '';
      if (!isSuccess) {
        statusTag = `<span class="tag-status" style="background:#fce4ec;color:#c62828;padding:4px 10px;border-radius:10px;font-size:11px;font-weight:600;">??憭望?</span>`;
        if (s.failure_reason) extraNote = `<div style="font-size:12px;color:#aaa;margin-top:4px;">${s.failure_reason}</div>`;
      } else if (reportDone) {
        statusTag = `<span class="tag-status tag-done">???勗?摰?</span>`;
      } else if (s.report_status === 'processing') {
        statusTag = `<span class="tag-status" style="background:#fff8e1;color:#f57c00;padding:4px 10px;border-radius:10px;font-size:11px;font-weight:600;">????銝?/span>`;
      } else {
        statusTag = `<span class="tag-status tag-done">??瑼Ｘ葫摰?</span>`;
      }

      reportTagsHtml = `<div class="hi-tags"><span class="tag ${lbl.cls}">${lbl.icon} ${lbl.name}</span></div>`;

      const subjAge = s.subject_age ? ` 繚 ${s.subject_age}甇深 : '';
      const dateStr = _formatTs(s.created_at);
      const captures = s.total_captures ? ` 繚 ${s.total_captures} 蝑 : '';

      const bwBtn = isSuccess
        ? `<button onclick="_viewHistorySession(${s.session_id})" style="background:#e8f0fe;color:#1a3a6e;border:none;font-size:12px;padding:5px 10px;border-radius:8px;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;">?? ?亦??行郭</button>`
        : '';

      html += `
        <div class="history-item" style="margin:0 0 10px;${isSuccess ? '' : 'opacity:0.7;'}">
          <div class="hi-top">
            <span class="hi-name">${s.subject_name || '??}${subjAge}</span>
            ${statusTag}
          </div>
          ${reportTagsHtml}
          ${extraNote}
          <div style="font-size:11px;color:#bbb;margin-top:6px;">${dateStr}${captures}</div>
          <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;margin-top:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;">
            ${bwBtn}
          </div>
          <!-- ?行郭撅??嚗?閮剝??-->
          <div id="bw-detail-${s.session_id}" style="display:none;margin-top:10px;padding:10px 12px;background:#f0f4f8;border-radius:10px;font-size:12px;"></div>
        </div>`;
    });
  });
  c.innerHTML = html;
}

// 暺??亦??行郭??敺?蝡臬?閰?Session ?瘜Ｙ絞閮?撅??典????// ????window._lastEegCapture 閮剔??嚗靘踴??蝯???憿舐內甇?Ⅱ?行郭
async function _viewHistorySession(sessionId) {
  const detailEl = document.getElementById(`bw-detail-${sessionId}`);
  if (!detailEl) return;

  // toggle嚗歇憿舐內撠望韏?  if (detailEl.style.display !== 'none') {
    detailEl.style.display = 'none';
    return;
  }

  detailEl.style.display = 'block';
  detailEl.innerHTML = '<span style="color:#888;">??頛?行郭鞈?銝凌?/span>';

  try {
    const r = await apiFetch(`/eeg/sessions/${sessionId}/stats`);
    if (!r.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${r.status}`);
    const data = await r.json();

    if (!data.eeg_stats) {
      detailEl.innerHTML = '<span style="color:#aaa;">?? 甇文甈∠?行郭鞈?</span>';
      return;
    }

    const s = data.eeg_stats;
    const b = s.bands_avg || {};
    const clamp = v => Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round(Number(v) || 0)));

    const metrics = [
      { name: '撠釣',     val: clamp(s.attention_percentage) },
      { name: '?暸?',     val: clamp(s.meditation_percentage) },
      { name: '?渲死(庛)', val: clamp(b.theta) },
      { name: '瘞??(帢)', val: clamp(b.alpha) },
      { name: '?摩(帣)', val: clamp(b.beta) },
      { name: '閫撖?帠)', val: clamp(b.gamma) },
    ];

    const bars = metrics.map(m => `
      <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;margin-bottom:5px;">
        <div style="width:64px;font-size:11px;color:#555;flex-shrink:0;">${m.name}</div>
        <div style="flex:1;height:8px;background:#dde4ef;border-radius:4px;overflow:hidden;">
          <div style="width:${m.val}%;height:100%;background:#2D3561;border-radius:4px;"></div>
        </div>
        <div style="width:34px;text-align:right;font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:#1a3a6e;">${m.val}%</div>
      </div>`).join('');

    const createdStr = data.created_at
      ? new Date(data.created_at * 1000).toLocaleString('zh-TW', {month:'2-digit',day:'2-digit',hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'})
      : '';

    detailEl.innerHTML = `
      <div style="font-weight:700;color:#1a3a6e;margin-bottom:8px;">
        ?? ${data.subject_name || '?葫??}${data.subject_age ? ` 繚 ${data.subject_age}甇深 : ''} ${createdStr ? '(' + createdStr + ')' : ''} 繚 ${s.sample_count} 蝑?      </div>
      ${bars}
      `;

    // ?? session ?瘜Ｗ?韏瑚??
    window._historyEegCache = window._historyEegCache || {};
    window._historyEegCache[sessionId] = {
      stats: s,
      meta:  { subject_name: data.subject_name, subject_age: data.subject_age, created_at: data.created_at },
    };
  } catch (e) {
    detailEl.innerHTML = `<span style="color:#c00;">??頛憭望?嚗?{e.message}</span>`;
  }
}

// 暺???函???憿舐內???風??session ?瘜Ｗ‵?亦???銝西歲頧?function _loadHistoryToResultsPage(sessionId) {
  const cached = (window._historyEegCache || {})[sessionId];
  if (!cached) return;

  // ?風?脰瘜Ｚ?撖怠 _lastEegCapture嚗????霈????質??堆?
  window._lastEegCapture = cached.stats;

  // 閮剖?撠???皜祈?祈?閮?  const meta = cached.meta || {};
  if (meta.subject_name) _currentSubjectName = meta.subject_name;
  if (meta.subject_age != null) _currentSubjectAge = meta.subject_age;

  // 頝喳蝯???gotoIndex ????_renderResultsFromEeg
  goto('screen-results');
}

async function refreshHistoryData() {
  const c = document.getElementById('history-list-container');
  if (!c) return;
  c.innerHTML = `<div style="text-align:center;padding:40px 12px;color:#bbb;font-size:13px;">頛銝凌?/div>`;

  const user = _currentUser || _getAuth();
  if (!user || !user.token) {
    c.innerHTML = `
      <div style="text-align:center;padding:60px 16px;color:#bbb;">
        <div style="font-size:48px;margin-bottom:10px;">??</div>
        <div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:600;">隢??餃</div>
      </div>`;
    return;
  }

  try {
    const r = await apiFetch('/eeg/sessions?limit=100');
    if (!r.ok) {
      c.innerHTML = `<div style="text-align:center;padding:40px 12px;color:#c62828;font-size:13px;">頛憭望?嚗TTP ${r.status}</div>`;
      return;
    }
    const data = await r.json();
    _historyCache = (data && data.sessions) || [];
    _renderHistoryList();
  } catch (e) {
    c.innerHTML = `<div style="text-align:center;padding:40px 12px;color:#c62828;font-size:13px;">???憭望?嚗?{e.message}</div>`;
  }
}

async function refreshSettingsData() {
  const nameEl  = document.getElementById('settings-name-display');
  const phoneEl = document.getElementById('settings-phone-display');
  const roleEl  = document.getElementById('settings-role-badge');
  const valEl   = document.getElementById('settings-stat-value');

  const user = _currentUser || _getAuth();
  if (!user) return;

  if (nameEl)  nameEl.textContent  = `${user.name || '??} ${user.role === 'admin' ? '蝞∠??? : '憿批?'}`;
  if (phoneEl) phoneEl.textContent = user.phone || '??;
  if (roleEl) {
    const labels = {
      admin: '?? 蝞∠???,
      consultant: (user.org_type || '憿批?') + (user.org ? ' 繚 ' + user.org : ''),
    };
    roleEl.textContent = labels[user.role] || labels.consultant;
  }

  try {
    const r = await apiFetch('/subjects');
    if (r.ok) {
      const subs = await r.json();
      if (valEl) valEl.textContent = `${subs.length} 雿;
    }
  } catch (e) { /* 撌脰◤ apiFetch 401 ?? */ }

  // ???憿舐內
  try {
    const notifEl = document.getElementById('settings-notify-state');
    if (notifEl) {
      const on = _getNotifPref();
      notifEl.textContent = on ? '??' : '??';
      notifEl.style.color = on ? '#2D3561' : '#888';
    }
  } catch (e) {}

  // 撌脤?撠身?嚗誑 localStorage + ?嗅? BLE ????其摯嚗?  try {
    const devEl = document.getElementById('settings-device-count');
    if (devEl) {
      let count = 0;
      try {
        const raw = localStorage.getItem('paired_devices');
        if (raw) count += (JSON.parse(raw) || []).length;
      } catch (e) {}
      if (window._btDevice && window._btDevice.name) count = Math.max(count, 1);
      devEl.textContent = `${count} ?躬;
    }
  } catch (e) {}
}

function toggleReport(el) {
  el.classList.toggle('selected');
  const cb = el.querySelector('.custom-checkbox');
  cb.textContent = el.classList.contains('selected') ? '?? : '';
  // Update total
  const selected = document.querySelectorAll('#screen-reports .report-item.selected').length;
  document.getElementById('total-points').textContent = (selected * 1 + (el.querySelector('.report-price').textContent.includes('2') ? 1 : 0)) + ' 暺?;
}

function selectRadio(el) {
  const group = el.parentElement;
  group.querySelectorAll('.radio-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('selected'));
  el.classList.add('selected');
}

// ??? ?葫?蜓瑼?Postgres CRUD + 銵典憛怠 ?????????????????????????????????
let _currentSubjectId = null;          // ??銝甇乓摮???subject_id
let _cachedSubjects   = [];            // 銝??典翰??
function _getSubjectFormValues() {
  const get = (id) => (document.getElementById(id)?.value || '').trim();
  const genderEl = document.querySelector('#subject-gender-group .radio-btn.selected');
  return {
    name:            get('subject-name-input'),
    email:           get('subject-email-input'),
    phone:           get('subject-phone-input'),
    birth_date:      get('subject-birth-input'),
    gender:          genderEl ? genderEl.dataset.gender || genderEl.textContent.trim() : '??,
    occupation:      get('subject-occupation-input'),
    medical_history: get('subject-history-input'),
    medications:     get('subject-meds-input'),
  };
}

function _setSubjectFormValues(s) {
  const set = (id, v) => { const el = document.getElementById(id); if (el) el.value = v || ''; };
  set('subject-name-input',       s.name);
  set('subject-email-input',      s.email);
  set('subject-phone-input',      s.phone);
  set('subject-birth-input',      s.birth_date);
  set('subject-occupation-input', s.occupation);
  set('subject-history-input',    s.medical_history);
  set('subject-meds-input',       s.medications);

  // ?批 radio
  document.querySelectorAll('#subject-gender-group .radio-btn').forEach(b => {
    b.classList.toggle('selected', (b.dataset.gender || b.textContent.trim()) === s.gender);
  });

  if (typeof updateSubjectAge === 'function') updateSubjectAge();
}

async function refreshSubjectsDropdown() {
  const sel = document.getElementById('subject-existing-select');
  if (!sel) return;
  try {
    const r = await apiFetch('/subjects');
    if (!r.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${r.status}`);
    _cachedSubjects = await r.json();
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn('頛?葫??銵典仃??', e);
    _cachedSubjects = [];
  }
  // ?遣銝??賊?
  sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">???豢?撌脫??葫????/option>' +
    _cachedSubjects.map(s => {
      const age = _calcAgeStr(s.birth_date);
      const label = `${s.name}${age ? `嚗?{age}甇莎?` : ''}${s.occupation ? '?? + s.occupation : ''}`;
      return `<option value="${s.subject_id}">${label}</option>`;
    }).join('');
}

function _calcAgeStr(birthDate) {
  if (!birthDate) return '';
  const m = birthDate.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
  if (!m) return '';
  const now = new Date();
  let age = now.getFullYear() - parseInt(m[1], 10);
  const bm = parseInt(m[2], 10), bd = parseInt(m[3], 10);
  if ((now.getMonth() + 1) < bm || ((now.getMonth() + 1) === bm && now.getDate() < bd)) age--;
  return age >= 0 ? String(age) : '';
}

function onSelectExistingSubject(sel) {
  const id = sel.value;
  const hint = document.getElementById('subject-existing-hint');
  if (!id) {
    if (hint) hint.style.display = 'none';
    _currentSubjectId = null;
    return;
  }
  const s = _cachedSubjects.find(x => String(x.subject_id) === String(id));
  if (!s) return;
  _setSubjectFormValues(s);
  _currentSubjectId     = s.subject_id;
  _currentSubjectName   = s.name || _currentSubjectName;
  _currentSubjectEmail  = s.email || _currentSubjectEmail;
  _currentSubjectGender = s.gender || '';
  const ageStr2 = _calcAgeStr(s.birth_date);
  _currentSubjectAge = ageStr2 ? parseInt(ageStr2, 10) : null;
  if (hint) hint.style.display = 'block';
}

// ??? 蝞∠??∪??堆??葫?恣???” + 蝺刻摩 + ?芷嚗?????????????????????????
let _adminSubjects = [];           // 摰皜
let _adminSubjectEditingId = null; // ?桀?甇?蝺刻摩??subject_id

function _escapeHtml(s) {
  if (s == null) return '';
  return String(s)
    .replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
    .replace(/"/g, '&quot;').replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
}

async function renderAdminSubjects() {
  const list = document.getElementById('admin-subject-list');
  const cnt  = document.getElementById('admin-subject-count');
  if (!list) return;

  // 蝚砌?甈⊥????圈?tab ??鞈?嚗雿輻???冽?撠???嚗窒?典翰??  if (!Array.isArray(_adminSubjects) || _adminSubjects.length === 0 || !document.activeElement || document.activeElement.id !== 'admin-subject-search') {
    list.innerHTML = `<div class="admin-empty">頛銝凌?/div>`;
    try {
      const r = await apiFetch('/subjects');
      if (!r.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${r.status}`);
      _adminSubjects = await r.json();
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn('頛?葫??銵典仃??', e);
      list.innerHTML = `<div class="admin-empty">?? 頛憭望?嚗?{_escapeHtml(e.message)}</div>`;
      if (cnt) cnt.textContent = '??0 蝑?;
      return;
    }
  }

  const q = ((document.getElementById('admin-subject-search')?.value) || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  const filtered = !q ? _adminSubjects : _adminSubjects.filter(s =>
    (s.name || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
    (s.email || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
    (s.phone || '').includes(q)
  );

  if (cnt) cnt.textContent = `??${filtered.length} 蝑?{q ? ` / ?券 ${_adminSubjects.length}` : ''}`;

  if (filtered.length === 0) {
    list.innerHTML = `<div class="admin-empty">${q ? '瘝?蝚血?璇辣??皜祈? : '撠?葫????}</div>`;
    return;
  }

  list.innerHTML = filtered.map(s => {
    if (s.subject_id === _adminSubjectEditingId) return _renderSubjectEditCard(s);
    return _renderSubjectViewCard(s);
  }).join('');
}

function _renderSubjectViewCard(s) {
  const age = _calcAgeStr(s.birth_date);
  const created = s.created_at ? s.created_at.replace('T', ' ').slice(0, 16) : '';
  return `<div class="admin-card">
    <div class="name">${_escapeHtml(s.name)}
      ${age ? `<span style="background:#e3f2fd;color:#1565c0;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:8px;font-size:11px;margin-left:6px;">${age} 甇?/span>` : ''}
      ${s.gender ? `<span style="background:#f3e5f5;color:#6a1b9a;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:8px;font-size:11px;margin-left:4px;">${_escapeHtml(s.gender)}</span>` : ''}
      <span style="font-size:11px;color:#aaa;font-weight:400;margin-left:6px;">#${s.subject_id}</span>
    </div>
    ${s.occupation ? `<div class="meta">? ${_escapeHtml(s.occupation)}</div>` : ''}
    <div class="row">? ${_escapeHtml(s.email)}</div>
    <div class="row">? ${_escapeHtml(s.phone)}</div>
    <div class="row" style="color:#888;">?? ${_escapeHtml(s.birth_date)}</div>
    ${s.medical_history ? `<div class="row" style="color:#c62828;">?? ?嚗?{_escapeHtml(s.medical_history)}</div>` : ''}
    ${s.medications ? `<div class="row" style="color:#ef6c00;">?? ?亦嚗?{_escapeHtml(s.medications)}</div>` : ''}
    <div class="meta" style="color:#bbb;">撱箇?嚗?{created}</div>
    <div class="actions" style="margin-top:8px;">
      <button class="admin-btn approve" onclick="adminSubjectEdit(${s.subject_id})">?? 蝺刻摩</button>
      <button class="admin-btn reject"  onclick="adminSubjectDelete(${s.subject_id})">??儭??芷</button>
    </div>
  </div>`;
}

function _renderSubjectEditCard(s) {
  const fid = (k) => `admin-sub-edit-${k}-${s.subject_id}`;
  return `<div class="admin-card" style="border:2px solid #1565c0;">
    <div class="name">?? 蝺刻摩銝?#${s.subject_id}</div>
    <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px;margin-top:8px;">
      <input id="${fid('name')}"       class="form-input" placeholder="憪? *"          value="${_escapeHtml(s.name)}">
      <input id="${fid('email')}"      class="form-input" placeholder="Email *"        value="${_escapeHtml(s.email)}">
      <input id="${fid('phone')}"      class="form-input" placeholder="?? *"          value="${_escapeHtml(s.phone)}">
      <input id="${fid('birth')}"      class="form-input" type="date"                  value="${_escapeHtml(s.birth_date)}">
      <select id="${fid('gender')}"    class="form-input">
        <option value="?? ${s.gender==='???'selected':''}>??/option>
        <option value="憟? ${s.gender==='憟??'selected':''}>憟?/option>
        <option value="?嗡?" ${s.gender==='?嗡?'?'selected':''}>?嗡?</option>
      </select>
      <input id="${fid('occupation')}" class="form-input" placeholder="?瑟平 / 蝔梯?"     value="${_escapeHtml(s.occupation||'')}">
      <input id="${fid('history')}"    class="form-input" placeholder="??寞??"     value="${_escapeHtml(s.medical_history||'')}">
      <input id="${fid('meds')}"       class="form-input" placeholder="?桀?雿輻?亦"     value="${_escapeHtml(s.medications||'')}">
    </div>
    <div class="actions" style="margin-top:10px;">
      <button class="admin-btn approve" onclick="adminSubjectSave(${s.subject_id})">? ?脣?</button>
      <button class="admin-btn reject"  onclick="adminSubjectCancelEdit()">??</button>
    </div>
  </div>`;
}

function adminSubjectEdit(id) {
  _adminSubjectEditingId = id;
  renderAdminSubjects();
}

function adminSubjectCancelEdit() {
  _adminSubjectEditingId = null;
  renderAdminSubjects();
}

async function adminSubjectSave(id) {
  const fid = (k) => `admin-sub-edit-${k}-${id}`;
  const get = (k) => (document.getElementById(fid(k))?.value || '').trim();
  const payload = {
    name:            get('name'),
    email:           get('email'),
    phone:           get('phone'),
    birth_date:      get('birth'),
    gender:          get('gender'),
    occupation:      get('occupation'),
    medical_history: get('history'),
    medications:     get('meds'),
  };
  if (!payload.name || !payload.email.includes('@') || !payload.phone || !payload.birth_date) {
    alert('憪? / Email / ?? / ?箇??交??敹‵');
    return;
  }
  try {
    const r = await apiFetch(`/subjects/${id}`, {
      method: 'PUT',
      body: JSON.stringify(payload),
    });
    if (!r.ok) {
      const txt = await r.text();
      alert(`?脣?憭望?嚗?{r.status}嚗?${txt}`);
      return;
    }
    _adminSubjectEditingId = null;
    _adminSubjects = [];      // 撘瑕??
    await renderAdminSubjects();
  } catch (e) {
    alert(`?脣?憭望?嚗?{e.message}`);
  }
}

async function adminSubjectDelete(id) {
  const s = _adminSubjects.find(x => x.subject_id === id);
  if (!confirm(`蝣箏?閬?扎?{s ? s.name : '#'+id}??嚗迨???⊥?敺拙??)) return;
  try {
    const r = await apiFetch(`/subjects/${id}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
    if (!r.ok) {
      const txt = await r.text();
      alert(`?芷憭望?嚗?{r.status}嚗?${txt}`);
      return;
    }
    _adminSubjects = _adminSubjects.filter(x => x.subject_id !== id);
    await renderAdminSubjects();
  } catch (e) {
    alert(`?芷憭望?嚗?{e.message}`);
  }
}

async function submitSubject() {
  const v = _getSubjectFormValues();

  // ?箸撽?
  if (!v.name)            { alert('隢撓?亙???); return; }
  if (!v.email || !v.email.includes('@')) { alert('隢撓?交??? Email'); return; }
  if (!v.phone)           { alert('隢撓?交?璈?); return; }
  if (!v.birth_date)      { alert('隢????); return; }

  // ?典?霈嚗?敺??恍雿輻嚗?  _currentSubjectName   = v.name;
  _currentSubjectEmail  = v.email;
  _currentSubjectGender = v.gender || '';
  const ageStr = _calcAgeStr(v.birth_date);
  _currentSubjectAge = ageStr ? parseInt(ageStr, 10) : null;

  // 撖怠敺垢嚗?蝡舀????嚗??喳祕?摮? row ??subject_id嚗?  try {
    const r = await apiFetch('/subjects', {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify(v),
    });
    if (r.ok) {
      const saved = await r.json();
      _currentSubjectId = saved.subject_id;
      console.log('[?葫? 撌脣摮?, saved);
    } else {
      const err = await r.text();
      console.warn('[?葫? ?脣?憭望?', r.status, err);
      alert('?脣??唬撩?憭望?嚗??舐匱蝥?蝔?雿蝑???曉?歇??皜祈???);
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn('[?葫? 敺垢?Ｙ?嚗?蝜潛?瘚?', e);
    alert('隡箸??券??憭望?嚗??舐匱蝥?蝔?雿蝑??摮?);
  }

  goto('screen-reports');
}

// ??? ?葫????靘????蝞僑朣??????????????????????????????????????
function updateSubjectAge() {
  const input   = document.getElementById('subject-birth-input');
  const display = document.getElementById('subject-age-display');
  if (!input || !display) return;

  const v = (input.value || '').trim();
  if (!v) {
    display.textContent = '?? ?桀?撟湧翩嚗?隢????';
    return;
  }

  // input[type=date] ? 'YYYY-MM-DD'嚗??銝脤???????off-by-one
  const m = v.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
  if (!m) {
    display.textContent = '?? ?桀?撟湧翩嚗??交??澆??航炊嚗?;
    return;
  }
  const by = parseInt(m[1], 10);
  const bm = parseInt(m[2], 10);
  const bd = parseInt(m[3], 10);

  const now = new Date();
  const ny = now.getFullYear();
  const nm = now.getMonth() + 1;
  const nd = now.getDate();

  let age = ny - by;
  if (nm < bm || (nm === bm && nd < bd)) age--;

  if (age < 0 || by < 1900 || by > ny) {
    display.textContent = '?? ?桀?撟湧翩嚗?隢?????';
    return;
  }
  display.textContent = `?? ?桀?撟湧翩嚗?{age} 甇深;
}

let _mbtiAnswers = []; // ??index 撠? _MBTI_QUESTIONS嚗?A' or 'B' or null
let _mbtiIndex   = 0;

function _resetMBTI() {
  _mbtiAnswers = new Array(_MBTI_QUESTIONS.length).fill(null);
  _mbtiIndex   = 0;
  // 撱?dots
  const dots = document.getElementById('mbti-dots');
  if (dots) {
    dots.innerHTML = _MBTI_QUESTIONS.map((_, i) =>
      `<div class="mbti-dot" data-idx="${i}"></div>`
    ).join('');
  }
  document.getElementById('mbti-step-badge').textContent = '甇仿? 3/4';
  renderMBTI();
}

function renderMBTI() {
  const total = _MBTI_QUESTIONS.length;
  const q = _MBTI_QUESTIONS[_mbtiIndex];

  document.getElementById('mbti-progress-label').textContent = `蝚?${_mbtiIndex+1} / ${total} 憿;
  // dots
  document.querySelectorAll('#mbti-dots .mbti-dot').forEach((d, i) => {
    d.classList.remove('done', 'active');
    if (_mbtiAnswers[i] != null) d.classList.add('done');
    if (i === _mbtiIndex) d.classList.add('active');
  });

  // 蝬剖漲敺賜?
  const dimColor = {
    EI: { bg:'#e8f5e9', fg:'#2e7d32', label:'E / I 蝬剖漲嚗???/ ?批?嚗? },
    SN: { bg:'#e3f2fd', fg:'#1565c0', label:'S / N 蝬剖漲嚗祕??/ ?渲死嚗? },
    TF: { bg:'#fff3e0', fg:'#e65100', label:'T / F 蝬剖漲嚗?/ ??嚗? },
    JP: { bg:'#fce4ec', fg:'#c2185b', label:'J / P 蝬剖漲嚗??/ ?亥死嚗? },
  }[q.dim];
  const badge = document.getElementById('mbti-dim-badge');
  badge.textContent = dimColor.label;
  badge.style.background = dimColor.bg;
  badge.style.color = dimColor.fg;

  document.getElementById('mbti-q-num').textContent  = `蝚?${_mbtiIndex+1} 憿;
  document.getElementById('mbti-q-text').textContent = q.text;

  const ans = _mbtiAnswers[_mbtiIndex];
  document.getElementById('mbti-options').innerHTML = `
    <div class="q-option ${ans==='A'?'selected':''}" onclick="answerMBTI('A')">
      <div class="opt-radio">${ans==='A'?'??:''}</div>
      ${q.a.label}
    </div>
    <div class="q-option ${ans==='B'?'selected':''}" onclick="answerMBTI('B')">
      <div class="opt-radio">${ans==='B'?'??:''}</div>
      ${q.b.label}
    </div>
  `;

  document.getElementById('mbti-prev-btn').style.opacity = _mbtiIndex === 0 ? '0.4' : '1';
  document.getElementById('mbti-prev-btn').style.pointerEvents = _mbtiIndex === 0 ? 'none' : 'auto';
  document.getElementById('mbti-next-btn').textContent =
    (_mbtiIndex === total - 1) ? '摰?? ????閮剖?' : '銝?憿???;
}

function answerMBTI(choice) {
  _mbtiAnswers[_mbtiIndex] = choice;
  renderMBTI();
  // ?芸?頝喃?銝憿?  setTimeout(() => {
    if (_mbtiIndex < _MBTI_QUESTIONS.length - 1) {
      _mbtiIndex++;
      renderMBTI();
    }
  }, 220);
}

function prevMBTI() {
  if (_mbtiIndex > 0) { _mbtiIndex--; renderMBTI(); }
}

function nextMBTI() {
  if (_mbtiIndex < _MBTI_QUESTIONS.length - 1) {
    _mbtiIndex++; renderMBTI();
  } else {
    // ?園? MBTI 閮?蝯?
    const score = { E:0,I:0, S:0,N:0, T:0,F:0, J:0,P:0 };
    _MBTI_QUESTIONS.forEach((q, i) => {
      const ans = _mbtiAnswers[i];
      if (ans === 'A') score[q.a.side]++;
      else if (ans === 'B') score[q.b.side]++;
    });
    const mbti = (score.E>=score.I?'E':'I') + (score.S>=score.N?'S':'N') +
                 (score.T>=score.F?'T':'F') + (score.J>=score.P?'J':'P');
    console.log('[MBTI ?蝯?]', mbti, score);
    goto('screen-connect');
  }
}

  if (!confirm('蝣箏?頝喲??嚗n蝟餌絞撠誑?行郭?葫蝯??湔?方? MBTI嚗?蝣箏漲?乩??澆??港?蝑???)) return;
  goto('screen-connect');
}

function _canPreviewRoles() {
  const u = _currentUser || _getAuth() || {};
  if (u.role === 'admin') return true;
  const ot = u.org_type || '';
  return ot === '撌乩?鈭箏' || ot === '撠?鈭箏';
}

// 蝞∠???/ 撌乩?鈭箏 / 撠?鈭箏?臬???閬賭??ｇ??渡???vs ????
let _adminPreviewRole = (() => {
  try { return sessionStorage.getItem('adminPreviewRole') || 'franchise'; }
  catch (e) { return 'franchise'; }
})();

function setPreviewRole(role) {
  _adminPreviewRole = (role === 'direct') ? 'direct' : 'franchise';
  try { sessionStorage.setItem('adminPreviewRole', _adminPreviewRole); } catch (e) {}
  _renderRolePreviewBar();
  _applyRolePricing();
}

function _renderRolePreviewBar() {
  const bar = document.getElementById('role-preview-bar');
  if (!bar) return;
  const show = _canPreviewRoles();
  bar.style.display = show ? 'block' : 'none';
  if (!show) return;

  const btnF = document.getElementById('role-btn-franchise');
  const btnD = document.getElementById('role-btn-direct');
  const active = _adminPreviewRole;
  if (btnF) {
    const on = active === 'franchise';
    btnF.style.border = on ? '2px solid #ff9800' : '2px solid #e0e0e0';
    btnF.style.background = on ? '#fff3e0' : 'white';
    btnF.style.color = on ? '#e65100' : '#666';
  }
  if (btnD) {
    const on = active === 'direct';
    btnD.style.border = on ? '2px solid #4caf50' : '2px solid #e0e0e0';
    btnD.style.background = on ? '#e8f5e9' : 'white';
    btnD.style.color = on ? '#2e7d32' : '#666';
  }
}

function _currentRole() {
  if (_canPreviewRoles()) return _adminPreviewRole;
  const ot = (_currentUser && _currentUser.org_type) || '';
  return ot === '?渡??? ? 'direct' : 'franchise';
}

// ????reportKey 撠?蝙?刻??寞 / 憿舐內?? / QR ?恍 ID??// ?曆??唬誨銵具迨閫銝都?獢?靘??渡?????撽?嚗???null??function getReportPricing(reportKey) {

// ?脣 screen-reports ??靘??脤??鞈????撖怠銝??桃?憿舐內?寡? onclick??function _applyRolePricing() {

  // 2) 撠銝＊蝷箇??∠?嚗?閫?湔憿舐內?寞 + onclick 撣嗥??寞?

    // ?湔 .report-price ??

    // ?蝯?onclick嚗?????vs ?頃???拍車?澆蝪賢?嚗?    if (key.startsWith('up_')) {

        'onclick',

      );
    } else {

        'onclick',

    }
  });
}

// 撖Ⅳ甈＊蝷??梯???嚗策??????// ?身 type=password ???啣? ??暺?銝? type=text ??????????password??function togglePwdVisibility(inputId, btnEl) {

    btnEl.setAttribute('aria-label', '?梯?撖Ⅳ');
  }
  // ??摰??阡??頛詨獢?銝行?皜豢?蝘餃摮葡撠曄垢嚗靘輻匱蝥撓?乓?  try {

  } catch (e) { /* ???汗?典 type=number 蝑?憓?銝嚗蕭??*/ }
}

// ?典耦 ASCII嚗+FF01 ~ U+FF5E嚗? ?耦嚗敶Ｙ征??U+3000 / nbsp U+00A0 ??銝?祉征?潦?// 銝剜?頛詨瘜???頛詨??嚗?嚗?嚚?嚚?嚗???韏瑚???敶Ｖ?璅??
// 雿?Unicode code point 摰銝?嚗?撠撣唾?嚗?蝣潭?撠仃??function _toHalfWidth(s) {
  if (!s) return '';
  let out = '';
  for (const ch of s) {
    const c = ch.charCodeAt(0);
    if (c >= 0xFF01 && c <= 0xFF5E)      out += String.fromCharCode(c - 0xFEE0);
    else if (c === 0x3000 || c === 0x00A0) out += ' ';
    else                                   out += ch;
  }
  return out;
}

function _getAuth() {
  // 1) ?岫 localStorage
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(_AUTH_KEY);
    if (raw) return JSON.parse(raw);
  } catch (e) {}
  // 2) localStorage 憭望? / 蝛???閰?Android 蝡?SharedPreferences ?遢
  //    嚗?鈭?OEM 蝟餌絞? APP ?脰??臬?皜?WebView 鞈?嚗?  try {
    if (typeof AndroidBridge !== 'undefined' && AndroidBridge && typeof AndroidBridge.getSavedAuth === 'function') {
      const backup = AndroidBridge.getSavedAuth();
      if (backup && backup.length > 5) {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(backup);
        if (parsed && parsed.token) {
          // ??撖怠? localStorage ??甈∟???          try { localStorage.setItem(_AUTH_KEY, backup); } catch (e2) {}
          console.log('[auth] 敺?Android SharedPreferences ???餃???);
          return parsed;
        }
      }
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  return null;
}
function _syncAdminDevMode() {
  const u = _currentUser || {};
  const isAdmin = !!(u.role === 'admin');
  const isTestUser = isAdmin || u.org_type === '撌乩?鈭箏' || u.org_type === '撠?鈭箏';
  document.documentElement.classList.toggle('is-admin-user', isAdmin);
  document.documentElement.classList.toggle('is-test-user', isTestUser);
}
function _saveAuth(obj) {
  const json = JSON.stringify(obj);
  try { localStorage.setItem(_AUTH_KEY, json); } catch (e) {}
  // ???嚗??神??Android SharedPreferences嚗???APP 銋敺?靘?
  try {
    if (typeof AndroidBridge !== 'undefined' && AndroidBridge && typeof AndroidBridge.saveAuth === 'function') {
      AndroidBridge.saveAuth(json);
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  _currentUser = obj;
  _syncAdminDevMode();
}
function _clearAuth() {
  try { localStorage.removeItem(_AUTH_KEY); } catch (e) {}
  try {
    if (typeof AndroidBridge !== 'undefined' && AndroidBridge && typeof AndroidBridge.clearSavedAuth === 'function') {
      AndroidBridge.clearSavedAuth();
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  _currentUser = null;
  _syncAdminDevMode();
}

async function apiFetch(path, options = {}) {
  const auth = _getAuth();
  const headers = Object.assign(
    { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    options.headers || {},
    auth && auth.token ? { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${auth.token}` } : {}
  );
  const url = path.startsWith('http') ? path : `${API_BASE}${path}`;
  const resp = await fetch(url, Object.assign({}, options, { headers, cache: 'no-store' }));
  if (resp.status === 401) {
    // Token 憭望? / ?芰?伐?皜? + 頝喳??餃??    _clearAuth();
    if (typeof goto === 'function') goto('screen-login');
    throw new Error('?芰?交??餃撌脤暹?');
  }
  return resp;
}

async function loginConsultant() {
  const phoneRaw = (document.getElementById('login-phone-input')?.value || '').trim();
  const passRaw  = (document.getElementById('login-pass-input')?.value || '').trim();
  const phone = _toHalfWidth(phoneRaw).replace(/[\s-]/g, '');
  const pass  = _toHalfWidth(passRaw);
  if (!phone || !pass) { alert('隢撓?交?璈?撖Ⅳ'); return; }

  if (phone !== phoneRaw || pass !== passRaw) {
    console.warn('[login] ?菜葫?啣敶Ｗ???撌脰????敶Ｗ???遣霅唬?甈∟撓?交???銝剜?頛詨瘜?);
  }

  let resp;
  try {
    resp = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/auth/login`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ phone, password: pass }),
    });
  } catch (e) {
    alert('隡箸??券??憭望?嚗? + e.message);
    return;
  }
  if (!resp.ok) {
    const err = await resp.json().catch(() => ({ detail: '撣唾???蝣潮隤? }));
    alert(err.detail || '?餃憭望?');
    return;
  }
  const data = await resp.json();
  _saveAuth(data);

  // 閮????Ⅳ嚗?甈⊿???亦?Ｘ??芸??‵
  try { localStorage.setItem('saved_login_phone', phone); } catch (e) {}

  // 銝?祇“?脤??dmin 銋脤???閬脩恣?敺?血?韏啣???
  goto('screen-home');

  // ?亙?蝡舀?閮?雿輻??憪?蝣潦?撘瑕敶靽格撖Ⅳ撠店獢?  if (typeof _maybePromptInitialPwdChange === 'function') {
    _maybePromptInitialPwdChange();
  }
}

function logoutConsultant() {
  if (!confirm('蝣箏?閬?箏?嚗?)) return;
  _clearAuth();
  goto('screen-login');
}

// ?頛???交? token ?岫 /me ?蝙?刻?????token ?芸???仿?
async function _initAuthOnBoot() {
  const cached = _getAuth();
  if (cached && cached.token) {
    _currentUser = cached;
    // ??/me 撽? token ?臬????    try {
      const r = await apiFetch('/auth/me');
      if (r.ok) {
        const me = await r.json();
        // ?蔥??唬蝙?刻???靽???token嚗?        _saveAuth(Object.assign({}, cached, me));
        // ?乩?雿輻??撖Ⅳ嚗?蝷箇??喃耨?對???渡?????孛?潘?
        if (typeof _maybePromptInitialPwdChange === 'function') {
          _maybePromptInitialPwdChange();
        }
      }
    } catch (e) { /* apiFetch 401 撌脩??芸?頝喟?仿? */ }
  } else {
    // ???餃 ??頝喟?仿?
    if (typeof goto === 'function') goto('screen-login');
  }
}

// ??璇辣嚗遙銝銝遛頞喳??嚗?
//   - bw ?拐辣摮
//   - sample_count >= 30 嚗?30 蝘誑銝??炎皜穿?
//   - ?喳??????嗥??行郭?詨潘?attn / medi / 隞颱??餅挾 > 0嚗?// ????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????function _validateBrainwaveCapture(bw) {
  if (!bw || typeof bw !== 'object') {
    return { ok: false, reason: 'brainwave_data 蝻箏仃嚗??芣??嚗? };
  }
  const sc = Number(bw.sample_count) || 0;
  if (sc < 30) {
    return { ok: false, reason: `瑼Ｘ葫璅??訾?頞喉?${sc} 蝑??喳?? 30 蝑蝝?30 蝘誑銝???瑼Ｘ葫嚗 };
  }
  const att = Number(bw.attention_percentage) || 0;
  const med = Number(bw.meditation_percentage) || 0;
  const bands = bw.bands_avg || {};
  const bandsTotal = Object.values(bands).reduce((s, v) => s + (Number(v) || 0), 0);
  if (att === 0 && med === 0 && bandsTotal === 0) {
    return { ok: false, reason: '?行郭?詨澆?粹嚗瘜Ｗ??芷???璆萄??冽?亥孛嚗? };
  }
  // ?餅扔?亥孛銝嚗Sense ?澆??0嚗畾菜??潔?銝靽∴?
  if (att === 0 && med === 0) {
    return { ok: false, reason: '?餅扔?亥孛銝嚗?瘜刻??暸??潛???0嚗hinkGear ?⊥?閮? eSense嚗???敺?皜? };
  }
  // ?餅扔?亥孛璆萄榆嚗tt ??5 銝?med ??5 隞?”撟曆??⊥??閫賂?憒?session 44 att=5, med=2嚗?  if (att <= 5 && med <= 5) {
    return { ok: false, reason: `?餅扔?亥孛璆萄榆嚗????行郭?敺?皜穿?撠釣=${att}?擛?${med}嚗???瑼駁? ??10嚗 };
  }
  return { ok: true };
}

}

//   variant         trial / full / vip嚗?閮?full嚗?//   chaptersToGenerate  靘?[1] 銵函內?芰??洵銝蝡??敹急葫閰佗?嚗ull = ?券
//   brainwaveData   EEG 閮?敺??詨?dict嚗ull = ??demo 鞈?
async function startFullReportGeneration(opts) {
  opts = opts || {};
  const card      = document.getElementById('rg-progress-card');
  const emailBar  = document.getElementById('result-email-bar');
  const emailText = document.getElementById('result-email-text');
  if (!card) return;

  // 蝑?EEG 蝯梯?撖怠 DB ?? session_id嚗?憭?15 蝘?嚗見憭 React App ? callback /reports/record
  if (window._lastEegPersistPromise && !window._lastEegSessionId) {
    try {
      await Promise.race([
        window._lastEegPersistPromise,
        new Promise(res => setTimeout(res, 15000)),
      ]);
    } catch (e) { /* 敹賜嚗ession_id ?舫 */ }
  }
  if (!window._lastEegSessionId) {
    console.warn('[startFullReportGeneration] ?? session_id 隞??嚗EG 鞈??航撠撖怠 DB嚗??勗?撠?閰衣 subject_id ?芸?鋆????);

  // ?芸???嚗pts ?Ⅱ?喳 > window._testModeChapters > ?? chapter1Only ?? > null嚗蝡?
  const chaptersToGen = opts.chaptersToGenerate !== undefined
    ? opts.chaptersToGenerate
    : (window._testModeChapters !== undefined && window._testModeChapters !== null)
      ? window._testModeChapters
      : (_testMode_chapter1Only ? [1] : null);

    ? opts.subjectEmail
    : (_currentSubjectEmail || null);

  // ?脣漲?∩?憿舐內??UI嚗??臭?????嚗????email 璈急?
  card.style.display = 'none';
  if (emailBar) {
    emailBar.className = 'rg-email-bar pending';
    const spin = emailBar.querySelector('.rg-spin');
    if (spin) spin.style.display = 'inline-block';
  }
  if (emailText) {

  const bwCheck = _validateBrainwaveCapture(opts.brainwaveData);
  if (!bwCheck.ok) {
    console.warn('[startFullReportGeneration] 撌脣?瘨?', bwCheck.reason);
    _showRedetectModal(bwCheck.reason);

  }

  // ? 蝚砌????葫????霅??見銝?舫?閮剖潘??血????迨???
  const subjectName = (opts.subjectName || _currentSubjectName || '').trim();
  if (!subjectName || subjectName === '?葫?? || subjectName === '?喳??? || subjectName === '皜祈岫璅∪?') {

      '?? ?葫???撩憭望??粹?閮剖潘?\n\n' +
      '?亦匱蝥??遢?勗??誑??蝞∠??⊥葫閰血?撘???\n' +
      '銝???唬遙雿?皜祈????⊥???行郭鞈??n\n' +
      '撱箄降???憪瑼Ｘ葫 ???葫?????Ｗ‵?亦?撖血??????n\n' +
      '蝣箏?閬誑?葫閰血?撘匱蝥?嚗?
    );
    if (!proceedAsTest) {
      console.warn('[startFullReportGeneration] 撌脣?瘨??葫???撩憭?);
      return;
    }
  }
  // ?芸??箸葫閰血??銝??閮??嫣噶 admin 敺?霅
  const finalSubjectName = (subjectName && subjectName !== '?葫?? && subjectName !== '?喳??? && subjectName !== '皜祈岫璅∪?')
    ? subjectName
    : '?妒 蝞∠??⊥葫閰?' + new Date().toLocaleString('zh-TW', { hour12:false }).replace(/[\/:\s]/g, '').slice(0,12);

  try {
    const resp = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/report-gen/start`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        subject_name:         finalSubjectName,
        subject_email:        subjectEmail,
        subject_age:          _currentSubjectAge,
        subject_gender:       _currentSubjectGender || '',
        subject_id:           _currentSubjectId || null,  // ?? ?葫?蜓瑼?FK嚗???摮文?
        report_type:          opts.reportType   || _currentReportSubType || 'life_script',
        variant:              opts.variant      || _currentReportVariant || 'full',
        chapters_to_generate: chaptersToGen,
        brainwave_data:       opts.brainwaveData|| null,
        session_id:           window._lastEegSessionId || null,  // 霈???React App callback /reports/record ?葆銝?      }),
    });
    if (!resp.ok) {
      // ? 敺垢蝖祆?嚗NVALID_BRAINWAVE 蝑平?隤方?撅? modal嚗?閬?雿輻????raw error
      let errBody = null;
      try { errBody = await resp.json(); } catch(e) {}
      const detail = errBody && errBody.detail;
      if (resp.status === 400 && detail && typeof detail === 'object' && detail.code === 'INVALID_BRAINWAVE') {
        _showRedetectModal(detail.reason || detail.message || '?行郭鞈?銝???);
        return;
      }
      const msg = (detail && (detail.message || detail.reason)) || (typeof detail === 'string' ? detail : `HTTP ${resp.status}`);
      throw new Error(msg);
    }
    const data = await resp.json();
    if (data.mode === 'external') {
      // ?? 憭蝟餌絞??嚗?韏啣??SSE嚗?????????????????????????????????
      const xmode = data.external_mode || '';

      // 1) headless嚗蜓敺垢??Playwright ?刻??航? Vercel React App嚗蝙?刻??冽?閬箔???      //    ???身閮?100% 靽?嚗sPDF + html2canvas ??Chromium 銝剖銵?
      //    ??雿輻??? APP
      if (xmode === 'headless') {
        if (emailBar) emailBar.className = 'rg-email-bar pending';
        const shortJob = (data.job_id || '').slice(0, 8);
        if (emailText && subjectEmail) {
          emailText.innerHTML = `?? ?勗?撌脣???job=${shortJob}?佗?<br>甇?銝餃?蝡航??舐?????銝憭拙撖 <b>${subjectEmail}</b><br><u>?典??甇日???/ APP嚗?敶梢??</u>`;
        } else if (emailText) {
          emailText.textContent = `?? ?勗?撌脣???job=${shortJob}?佗?嚗??舐??葉?圳;
        }
        return;
      }

      // 1b) vite_prefill / redirect嚗laywright ?芸??冽???fallback嚗?靽??汗?券???
      if (xmode === 'vite_prefill' || xmode === 'redirect') {
        if (emailBar) emailBar.className = 'rg-email-bar done';
        if (emailText) {
          emailText.innerHTML = `?? ?勗?蝟餌絞撌脣???<a href="${data.redirect_url}" target="_blank" style="color:#fff;text-decoration:underline;font-weight:700;">?交?芸?頝唾?隢?甇???/a>${subjectEmail ? `<br>摰?敺??芸?撖 <b>${subjectEmail}</b>` : ''}<br><small>(fallback: ?靽????)</small>`;
        }
        setTimeout(() => {
          try { window.location.href = data.redirect_url; }
          catch (e) { try { window.open(data.redirect_url, '_blank'); } catch (e2) {} }
        }, 1500);
        return;
      }

      // 2) parent_child_rest嚗omeAnalysisReport ??撌脖???/generate嚗歲?啣??
      if (xmode === 'parent_child_rest') {
        if (data.result_url) {
          if (emailBar) emailBar.className = 'rg-email-bar done';
          if (emailText) {
            emailText.innerHTML = `?? 閬芸??勗???銝凌?a href="${data.result_url}" target="_blank" style="color:#fff;text-decoration:underline;font-weight:700;">暺迨?亦??脣漲 ??/a>`;
          }
          setTimeout(() => {
            try { window.location.href = data.result_url; }
            catch (e) { try { window.open(data.result_url, '_blank'); } catch (e2) {} }
          }, 1500);
        } else {
          if (emailBar) emailBar.className = 'rg-email-bar error';
          if (emailText) emailText.textContent = `??閬芸??勗?銝憭望?嚗?{data.error || ''}`;
        }
        return;
      }

      // 3) marital_rest嚗arital-report 撌脩?亙? PDF嚗??曆?頛??
      if (xmode === 'marital_rest' && data.result_url) {
        if (emailBar) emailBar.className = 'rg-email-bar done';
        if (emailText) {
          emailText.innerHTML = `??憭怠氖?勗?撌脩???<a href="${data.result_url}" target="_blank" style="color:#fff;text-decoration:underline;font-weight:700;">銝? PDF ??/a>${subjectEmail ? '嚗歇撖?Email嚗? : ''}`;
        }
        return;
      }

      // fallback嚗??mode
      if (data.ok) {
        if (emailBar) emailBar.className = 'rg-email-bar pending';
        if (emailText) emailText.textContent = `?? 憭?勗?蝟餌絞??銝凌佗?mode=${xmode}嚗;
      } else {
        if (emailBar) emailBar.className = 'rg-email-bar error';
        if (emailText) emailText.textContent = `??憭?勗?憭望?嚗?{data.error || '?芰 mode: ' + xmode}`;
      }
      return;
    }
    _rgJobId = data.job_id;
    // ?批遣璅∪?嚗???thread ?其蜓敺垢頝????喃蝙雿輻????APP嚗DF/GCS/Email 瘚?隞?摰?
    if (emailBar) emailBar.className = 'rg-email-bar pending';
    if (emailText && subjectEmail) {
      emailText.innerHTML = `?? ?勗?撌脣???job=${data.job_id.slice(0,8)}?佗?<br>??銝憭拙撖 <b>${subjectEmail}</b>嚗?u>?典??甇日???/u>`;
    } else if (emailText) {
      emailText.textContent = `?? ?勗?撌脣???job=${data.job_id.slice(0,8)}?佗?嚗??葉?圳;
    }
    _connectReportSSE(_rgJobId);
  } catch (e) {

    if (emailBar) emailBar.className = 'rg-email-bar error';
  }
}

function _connectReportSSE(jobId) {
  if (_rgEventSource) { try { _rgEventSource.close(); } catch(e){} }
  const url = `${API_BASE}/report-gen/stream/${jobId}`;
  _rgEventSource = new EventSource(url);

  _rgEventSource.onmessage = (ev) => {
    try {
      const data = JSON.parse(ev.data);
      if (data.error) { _handleReportError(data.error); return; }
      _renderReportProgress(data);
      if (data.status === 'completed') {
        _handleReportCompleted();
        _rgEventSource.close();
      } else if (data.status === 'error') {
        _handleReportError(data.error_message || '??憭望?');
        _rgEventSource.close();
      }
    } catch (e) { console.error('SSE parse err', e); }
  };
  _rgEventSource.onerror = (e) => {
    console.warn('SSE ???銝剜嚗?箄憚閰Ｗ???, e);
    try { _rgEventSource.close(); } catch (err) {}
    _rgEventSource = null;
    _pollReportStatus(jobId);   // SSE 憭望???fallback 頛芾岷
  };
}

async function _pollReportStatus(jobId) {
  while (true) {
    try {
      const resp = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/report-gen/status/${jobId}`);
      if (!resp.ok) break;
      const data = await resp.json();
      _renderReportProgress(data);
      if (data.status === 'completed') { _handleReportCompleted(); break; }
      if (data.status === 'error') { _handleReportError(data.error_message); break; }
      await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 3000));
    } catch (e) { console.error('poll err', e); break; }
  }
}

function _renderReportProgress(d) {
  const fill = document.getElementById('rg-progress-fill');
  const stat = document.getElementById('rg-progress-stat');
  const curText = document.getElementById('rg-progress-current-text');
  const chList = document.getElementById('rg-chapter-list');

  if (fill) fill.style.width = (d.progress || 0) + '%';
  if (stat) stat.textContent = `${d.completed_sections || 0} / ${d.total_sections || 0} 蝭嚗?{d.progress || 0}%嚗;
  if (curText) curText.textContent = d.current_section || '??銝?..';

  if (chList && d.chapters_list && d.chapters_list.length) {
    // ?芸蝚砌?甈∪‵??    if (!chList.dataset.populated) {
      chList.innerHTML = d.chapters_list.map(c =>
        `<span class="rg-chapter-pill" data-ch="${c.num}">${c.icon || '??'} 蝚?{c.num}蝡?/span>`
      ).join('');
      chList.dataset.populated = '1';
      d.chapters_list.forEach(c => { _rgChaptersMap[c.num] = c; });
    }
    // ?湔?????current_chapter_num 銋? = done?? current??敺?= pending嚗?    const curNum = d.current_chapter_num;
    chList.querySelectorAll('.rg-chapter-pill').forEach(el => {
      const n = parseInt(el.dataset.ch, 10);
      el.classList.remove('done', 'current');
      if (curNum && n < curNum) el.classList.add('done');
      else if (curNum && n === curNum) el.classList.add('current');
      else if (d.status === 'completed') el.classList.add('done');
    });
  }
}

async function _handleReportCompleted() {
  const emailBar  = document.getElementById('result-email-bar');
  const emailText = document.getElementById('result-email-text');
  const curBox    = document.getElementById('rg-progress-current');
  const spin = emailBar?.querySelector('.rg-spin');
  if (spin) spin.style.display = 'none';
  if (curBox) curBox.innerHTML = '<span style="color:#a5d6a7;">??撌脣???蝭??</span>';

  // 敺?/status ??email 蝯?
  try {
    const r = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/report-gen/status/${_rgJobId}`);
    const d = await r.json();
    const es = d.email_status || 'skipped';
    if (es === 'sent') {
      if (emailBar) emailBar.className = 'rg-email-bar done';
      if (emailText) emailText.textContent = `? ?勗?撌脣?? ${d.email_to || '?葫??Email'}`;
    } else if (es === 'failed') {
      if (emailBar) emailBar.className = 'rg-email-bar error';
      if (emailText) emailText.textContent = `??Email 撖憭望?嚗?{d.email_error || '隢炎??GMAIL 閮剖?'}`;
    } else {
      // skipped嚗?? email
      if (emailBar) emailBar.className = 'rg-email-bar done';
      if (emailText) emailText.textContent = '??蝡???摰?嚗?? Email?撖?';
    }
  } catch (e) {
    if (emailBar) emailBar.className = 'rg-email-bar done';
    if (emailText) emailText.textContent = '??蝡???摰?';
  }
}

function _handleReportError(msg) {
  const emailBar = document.getElementById('result-email-bar');
  const emailText = document.getElementById('result-email-text');
  if (emailBar) emailBar.className = 'rg-email-bar error';
  if (emailText) emailText.textContent = `????憭望?嚗?{msg || '?芰?航炊'}`;
}

// HTML escape utility
function escapeHtml(s) {
  if (s === null || s === undefined) return '';
  return String(s)
    .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
    .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
    .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
    .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
    .replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
}

async function createPaymentOrder(reportType, amount, subjectName) {
  try {
    const resp = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/payments/create`, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ report_type: reportType, amount, subject_name: subjectName })
    });
    const data = await resp.json();
    return data;
  } catch (e) {
    console.error("撱箇?閮憭望?嚗?, e);
    return null;
  }
}

async function pollPaymentStatus(orderId) {
  try {
    const resp = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/payments/${orderId}/status`);
    const data = await resp.json();
    return data.status;
  } catch (e) {
    return "pending";
  }
}

function startPaymentPolling(orderId, qrScreenId) {
  _currentOrderId = orderId;
  _pollCountdown  = 15 * 60;

  // ?迫銝?甈∠?閮???  if (_pollTimer)    clearInterval(_pollTimer);
  if (_countdownTimer) clearInterval(_countdownTimer);

  // ?閮?嚗?蝘?啁?Ｖ??擗???
  _countdownTimer = setInterval(() => {
    _pollCountdown--;
    const min = Math.floor(_pollCountdown / 60).toString().padStart(2, "0");
    const sec = (_pollCountdown % 60).toString().padStart(2, "0");
    const els = document.querySelectorAll(".qr-countdown");
    els.forEach(el => el.textContent = `隢 ${min}:${sec} ?批???甈霉);

    if (_pollCountdown <= 0) {
      clearInterval(_countdownTimer);
      clearInterval(_pollTimer);
      showPaymentResult("expired", qrScreenId);
    }
  }, 1000);

  // 瘥?3 蝘憚閰Ｖ?甈曄???  _pollTimer = setInterval(async () => {
    const status = await pollPaymentStatus(orderId);
    console.log(`[隞狡頛芾岷] 閮 ${orderId} ???${status}`);

    if (status === "paid") {
      // ??雿輻蝯曹??賢?嚗? simulatePaid 摰?詨?頝臬?嚗?      _onPaymentPaid(qrScreenId);
    } else if (status === "expired" || status === "failed") {
      clearInterval(_pollTimer);
      clearInterval(_countdownTimer);
      showPaymentResult(status, qrScreenId);
    }
  }, 3000);
}

function showPaymentResult(status, qrScreenId) {
  const overlay = document.getElementById("payment-result-overlay");
  const icon    = document.getElementById("payment-result-icon");
  const title   = document.getElementById("payment-result-title");
  const msg     = document.getElementById("payment-result-msg");
  const btn     = document.getElementById("payment-result-btn");

  // ?臬?箝?鞈澆?蝝?蝔?隞狡??銝??行郭瑼Ｘ葫嚗?仿＊蝷箏?蝝???  const isUpgrade = (qrScreenId || '').startsWith('screen-qr-up-');

  // paid 頝臬??璅??  const targetScreen = isUpgrade ? 'screen-upgrade-done' : 'screen-connect';

  if (status === "paid" && isUpgrade) {
    icon.textContent  = "??";
    title.textContent = "??隞狡??嚗?;
    msg.textContent   = "甇?????敺??啁??砍??;
    btn.textContent   = "?亦???蝯? ??;
    btn.style.display = '';

    // ??頝臬?嚗?閮?pointerEvents=auto嚗耨甇??蝝?overlay ???⊥?暺???bug嚗?    overlay.style.display = "flex";

  if (status === "paid") {

    btn.textContent   = "蝡??瑼Ｘ葫 ??;
    btn.style.display = '';
    // ??暺????賜??唾歲頧?    btn.onclick = () => { _forceCloseAllOverlays(); goto(targetScreen); };
  } else if (status === "expired") {
    icon.textContent  = "??;
    title.textContent = "隞狡?暹?";
    msg.textContent   = "QR Code 撌脣仃??隢??圈???;
    btn.textContent   = "??豢?";
    btn.onclick       = () => { overlay.style.display = "none"; overlay.style.pointerEvents = "none"; goto("screen-reports"); };
  } else {
    icon.textContent  = "??;
    title.textContent = "隞狡憭望?";
    msg.textContent   = "隢?閰行??舐窗摰Ｘ?";

// ?????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????
