function _reportKeyToZh(key) {
  const map = {
    'life_trial':  '?行郭??鈭箇?? 繚 擃???,
    'life_full':   '?行郭??鈭箇?? 繚 摰??,
    'life_vip':    '?行郭??鈭箇?? 繚 VIP??,
    'child_trial': '?咱?行郭憭抵釵閫?Ⅳ 繚 擃???,
    'child_full':  '?咱?行郭憭抵釵閫?Ⅳ 繚 摰??,
    'child_vip':   '?咱?行郭憭抵釵閫?Ⅳ 繚 VIP??,
    'test_1':      '?妒 NT$1 ?皜祈岫嚗??洵銝蝡?+ 撖縑嚗?,
    'up_trial_full': '?頃??嚗?撽? ??摰??,
    'up_full_vip':   '?頃??嚗??渡? ??VIP??,
    'up_trial_vip':  '?頃??嚗?撽? ??VIP??,
  };
  return map[key] || key || '??;
}

function _populateConnectScreen() {
  // ?葫??閮?  const nameEl = document.getElementById('connect-subject-name');
  const metaEl = document.getElementById('connect-subject-meta');
  if (nameEl) nameEl.textContent = _currentSubjectName || '??;
  if (metaEl) {
    const parts = [];
    if (_currentSubjectAge != null) parts.push(`${_currentSubjectAge}甇深);
    if (_currentSubjectGender)      parts.push(_currentSubjectGender);
    parts.push(`?勗?嚗?{_reportKeyToZh(_currentReportType)}`);
    metaEl.textContent = parts.join(' 繚 ');
  }

  // 憿舐內銝活?⊿?憭望??隤歹??交?嚗?  const errBanner = document.getElementById('connect-error-banner');
  const errReason = document.getElementById('connect-error-reason');
  if (errBanner && window._connectScreenError) {
    if (errReason) errReason.textContent = window._connectScreenError;
    errBanner.style.display = 'block';
    window._connectScreenError = null;
  } else if (errBanner) {
    errBanner.style.display = 'none';
  }

  // ???單? BLE ??蒂?湔 UI
  _refreshConnectScreen();
}

function _refreshConnectScreen() {
  const bridge = (typeof AndroidBridge !== 'undefined' && AndroidBridge) ? AndroidBridge : null;

  // ?? 霈??BLE debug 鞈? ??????????????????????????????????????????????
  let dbg = null;
  try { if (bridge && bridge.getBleDebugInfo) dbg = JSON.parse(bridge.getBleDebugInfo()); } catch(e) {}

  // GATT 銝脫????嚗??startStreamingEeg ?澆敺?? true嚗onnect ???虜??false嚗?  let gattConnected = false;
  try { gattConnected = !!(dbg && dbg.ble_connected); } catch(e) {}
  try { if (bridge && bridge.isBrainwaveConnected) gattConnected = gattConnected || !!bridge.isBrainwaveConnected(); } catch(e) {}

  // ?駁?嚗ATT ?????霈?堆?
  let bat = -1;
  try { if (bridge && bridge.getDeviceBattery) bat = parseInt(bridge.getDeviceBattery(), 10); } catch(e) {}
  if (bat < 1 && dbg && dbg.battery >= 1) bat = dbg.battery;
  const batOk = (bat >= 1 && bat <= 100);

  // 撌脤?撠?蝵格??蝟餌絞????嚗onnect ???舫???璅?
  const pairedCount = dbg ? (dbg.paired || dbg.paired_count || 0) : 0;
  const mindsensor  = (dbg && dbg.mindsensor) ? dbg.mindsensor : '';
  const btEnabled   = !!(dbg && (dbg.bt_enabled || dbg.ble_connected || dbg.battery_readable));

  // ?? ?斗????⊿???撌脤?撠?OR GATT 撌脤?OR ?駁??航? ??????????????
  // ?嚗onnect ??? GATT 撠撱箇?嚗?鋆蔭撌脤?撠停隞?”?臭誑??
  const isPaired   = pairedCount > 0;
  const isGatt     = gattConnected || batOk || (dbg && dbg.battery_readable);
  const canStart   = !bridge ? true   // ?汗?冽葫閰行芋撘?交銵?                   : (isPaired || isGatt);

  // ?? 閮剖?????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????
  const scanLabel  = document.getElementById('connect-scan-label');
  const scanSub    = document.getElementById('connect-scan-sub');
  const notConn    = document.getElementById('connect-not-connected');
  const devFound   = document.getElementById('connect-device-found');
  const devName    = document.getElementById('connect-dev-name');
  const devMac     = document.getElementById('connect-dev-mac');
  const sigLabel   = document.getElementById('connect-signal-label');
  const scanAnim   = document.getElementById('connect-scan-anim');

  if (!bridge) {
    if (scanLabel) scanLabel.textContent = '?汗?冽芋撘??∟???';
    if (scanSub)   scanSub.textContent   = '撖阡?閮剖???????APP 銝剜?雿?;
    if (notConn)   notConn.style.display = 'none';
    if (devFound)  devFound.style.display = 'none';
  } else if (isGatt) {
    // GATT 銝脫?撌脤???⊿?銝剜???
    if (scanLabel) scanLabel.textContent = '???行郭?撌脤??嚗葡瘚葉嚗?;
    if (scanSub)   scanSub.textContent   = batOk ? `?駁? ${bat}%` : '?駁??芣?靘?甇?虜嚗?;
    if (scanAnim)  scanAnim.style.display = 'none';
    if (notConn)   notConn.style.display = 'none';
    if (devFound)  devFound.style.display = 'flex';
    if (devName)   devName.textContent   = mindsensor || `撌脤?撠身??(${pairedCount} ??`;
    if (devMac)    devMac.textContent    = dbg && dbg.model ? `鋆蔭??嚗?{dbg.model}` : '';
    const sigStrength = batOk ? Math.max(1, Math.min(4, Math.ceil(bat / 25))) : 3;
    ['csb1','csb2','csb3','csb4'].forEach((id, i) => {
      const el = document.getElementById(id);
      if (el) el.className = 'sig-bar' + (i < sigStrength ? ' active' : '');
    });
    if (sigLabel) { sigLabel.textContent = '?臬末'; sigLabel.style.color = '#00C853'; }
  } else if (isPaired) {
    // 撌脤?撠? GATT 撠撱箇?嚗onnect ?迤撣貊????⊿???敺??遣 GATT嚗?    if (scanLabel) scanLabel.textContent = '???行郭?撌脤?撠?;
    if (scanSub)   scanSub.textContent   = '????憪??撠?遣蝡??';
    if (scanAnim)  scanAnim.style.display = 'none';
    if (notConn)   notConn.style.display = 'none';
    if (devFound)  devFound.style.display = 'flex';
    if (devName)   devName.textContent   = mindsensor || `撌脤?撠身??(${pairedCount} ??`;
    if (devMac)    devMac.textContent    = dbg && dbg.model ? `鋆蔭??嚗?{dbg.model}` : '???⊿?敺???';
    ['csb1','csb2','csb3','csb4'].forEach((id, i) => {
      const el = document.getElementById(id);
      if (el) el.className = 'sig-bar' + (i < 2 ? ' active' : '');
    });
    if (sigLabel) { sigLabel.textContent = '??銝?; sigLabel.style.color = '#FFA000'; }
  } else {
    // 撠??
    if (scanLabel) scanLabel.textContent = '????閮剖?銝凌?;
    if (scanSub)   scanSub.textContent   = '隢??冽?璈??身摰葉???行郭?嚗???ㄐ';
    if (scanAnim)  scanAnim.style.display = '';
    if (notConn)   notConn.style.display = 'block';
    if (devFound)  devFound.style.display = 'none';
  }

  // ?? ??蜇閬賢? ???????????????????????????????????????????????????????
  const rows = [];
  if (dbg) {
    rows.push({ ok: !dbg.adapter_null,  label:'??璅∠?', val: dbg.adapter_null ? '??銝?? : '???舐' });
    rows.push({ ok: btEnabled,          label:'????', val: btEnabled ? '??撌脤??? : '???芷??? });
    rows.push({ ok: !!dbg.perm_connect, label:'???甈?', val: dbg.perm_connect ? '??撌脫?甈? : '???芣?甈? });
    rows.push({ ok: isPaired,
                label:'撌脤?撠?蝵?,
                val: isPaired ? `??${pairedCount} ??{mindsensor ? '嚗? + mindsensor + '嚗? : ''}` : '??0 ?堆?隢??函頂蝯梯???撠?' });
  }
  // GATT ???????⊿??虜?箝?撠葉??
  rows.push({
    ok:  isGatt,
    advisory: isPaired && !isGatt,
    label: '?行郭????',
    val:  isGatt   ? (batOk ? `??撌脤??嚗??${bat}%嚗 : '??撌脤??') :
          isPaired ? '????摰?嚗???芸????' :
                     '???芷??嚗???撠?',
  });

  const rowsEl = document.getElementById('connect-status-rows');
  if (rowsEl) {
    rowsEl.innerHTML = rows.map(r => {
      const color = r.ok ? '#2e7d32' : (r.advisory ? '#e65100' : '#c62828');
      return `<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-size:13px;">
        <span style="color:#555;">${r.label}</span>
        <span style="color:${color};font-weight:600;">${r.val}</span>
      </div>`;
    }).join('');
  }

  // ?? ?餅扔?亥孛?釭 ?????????????????????????????????????????????????????
  const elecEl = document.getElementById('connect-electrode-rows');
  if (elecEl) {
    if (!bridge) {
      elecEl.innerHTML = '<span style="color:#aaa;font-size:12px;">?汗?冽芋撘??餅扔鞈????APP 銝剖?敺?/span>';
    } else if (isGatt) {
      elecEl.innerHTML = `
        <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;">
          <span><span style="color:#2e7d32;">??/span> 憿嚗p1嚗?/span>
          <span style="color:#2e7d32;font-weight:600;">撌脫閫?/span>
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;">
          <span><span style="color:#2e7d32;">??/span> 撌西喳冗嚗1嚗?/span>
          <span style="color:#2e7d32;font-weight:600;">撌脫閫?/span>
        </div>
        <div style="margin-top:8px;padding:8px 10px;background:#e8f5e9;border-radius:8px;font-size:12px;color:#2e7d32;">
          ??Fp1 ?桅?撠梁?嚗???⊿?
        </div>`;
      const ch = document.getElementById('connect-channel-note');
      if (ch) ch.style.display = 'block';
    } else if (isPaired) {
      elecEl.innerHTML = `
        <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;">
          <span><span style="color:#FFA000;">?</span> 憿嚗p1嚗?/span>
          <span style="color:#e65100;">?⊿???敺Ⅱ隤?/span>
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;">
          <span><span style="color:#FFA000;">?</span> 撌西喳冗嚗1嚗?/span>
          <span style="color:#e65100;">?⊿???敺Ⅱ隤?/span>
        </div>
        <div style="margin-top:8px;padding:8px 10px;background:#fff8e1;border-radius:8px;font-size:12px;color:#e65100;">
          ???行郭?撌脤?撠???憪???餅扔????芸??湔
        </div>`;
    } else {
      elecEl.innerHTML = `
        <div style="font-size:12px;color:#aaa;">隢??冽?璈??身摰葉???行郭?</div>`;
    }
  }

  // ?? ????嚗歇???喳??嚗??蝑?GATT嚗????????????????????????????
  const startBtn = document.getElementById('connect-start-btn');
  if (startBtn) {
    startBtn.disabled = !canStart;
    startBtn.style.opacity = canStart ? '1' : '0.5';
    if      (isGatt)   startBtn.textContent = '??撌脤??嚗?憪??臬蝺????;
    else if (isPaired) startBtn.textContent = '??撌脤?撠?????箇??⊿? ??;
    else               startBtn.textContent = '?? 隢??函頂蝯梯???撠瘜Ｗ?';
  }
}

function _connectScreenStart() {
  const bridge = (typeof AndroidBridge !== 'undefined' && AndroidBridge) ? AndroidBridge : null;

  if (!bridge) { goto('screen-baseline'); return; }   // ?汗?冽葫閰行銵?
  // 霈?????  let dbg = null;
  try { dbg = JSON.parse(bridge.getBleDebugInfo()); } catch(e) {}
  const pairedCount = dbg ? (dbg.paired || dbg.paired_count || 0) : 0;
  const isGatt = !!(dbg && (dbg.ble_connected || dbg.battery_readable));

  if (!pairedCount && !isGatt) {
    window._connectScreenError = '隢??冽?璈??身摰葉???行郭?敺?閰艾?;
    _refreshConnectScreen();
    return;
  }
  goto('screen-baseline');
}

// ?????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????
// ?瘜Ｘ炎皜砌葉??ｇ??單??豢?? + 蝝舐?蝯衣?????// ?祕 EEG嚗PK ?? window.JSBridge.onEegSample(json) ?刻??脖?
// ?汗?冽葫閰佗???_simNextSample() 璅⊥??韏啣
// ?????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????
function _populateDetectHeader() {
  const sEl = document.getElementById('detect-subject');
  if (!sEl) return;
  const ageTxt = (_currentSubjectAge != null) ? ` 繚 ${_currentSubjectAge}甇深 : '';
  sEl.textContent = `? ${_currentSubjectName || '?葫??}${ageTxt}`;
}

function _resetEegLiveUI() {
  const ids = ['metric-attn-val','metric-medi-val',
               'band-val-delta','band-val-theta','band-val-alpha','band-val-beta','band-val-gamma',
               'faa-ratio'];
  ids.forEach(id => { const e = document.getElementById(id); if (e) e.textContent = '??; });
  ['metric-attn-bar','metric-medi-bar'].forEach(id => {
    const e = document.getElementById(id); if (e) e.style.width = '0%';
  });
  ['band-bar-delta','band-bar-theta','band-bar-alpha','band-bar-beta','band-bar-gamma'].forEach(id => {
    const e = document.getElementById(id); if (e) e.style.height = '0%';
  });
  const fill = document.getElementById('detect-fill'); if (fill) fill.style.width = '0%';
  const lbl  = document.getElementById('detect-label'); if (lbl) lbl.textContent = `撌脫??0 / ${_eegLiveDurationSec} 蝘;
  const tm   = document.getElementById('detect-time'); if (tm) tm.textContent = _formatMs(_eegLiveDurationSec);
  const chips = document.getElementById('detect-chips');
  if (chips) chips.innerHTML = '<span style="color:rgba(255,255,255,0.4);font-size:12px;">?⊿???敺＊蝷算?/span>';
  const stxt = document.getElementById('detect-status-text'); if (stxt) stxt.textContent = '?⊿?銝?;
  const fhint = document.getElementById('faa-hint'); if (fhint) fhint.textContent = '?⊿?銝凌?;
}

function _formatMs(sec) {
  if (sec < 0) sec = 0;
  const m = Math.floor(sec / 60);
  const s = sec % 60;
  return `${m}:${String(s).padStart(2,'0')}`;
}

function _startEegLiveCapture() {
  // 撌脣頝停銝???  if (_eegLiveTimer) return;
  _populateDetectHeader();
  _resetEegLiveUI();

  // 瘥活???唳?皜銝?甈∠? session ?嚗??銝頛迎?憒鞈???箏?嚗?  // ??session_id ??async persist 蝡嗆????頛芸??  window._lastEegCapture    = null;
  window._lastEegSessionId  = null;
  window._lastEegPersistPromise = null;
  // 撘瑕?蔭?亙熒瑼Ｘ頝舐??嚗銋??瑁??亙熒瑼Ｘ敺撣賊?綽?
  // _eegHealthActive ?航畾? true嚗??湔迤撘?? EEG 璅??券
  // ?脣?亙熒瑼Ｘ sink ??銝餅?蝔??? 3 ??敺?霅仃??頝喲?
  window._eegHealthActive = false;

  _eegLiveAccum = { attn:[], medi:[], delta:[], theta:[], alpha:[], beta:[], gamma:[],
                    low_alpha:[], high_alpha:[], low_beta:[], high_beta:[], low_gamma:[], high_gamma:[],
                    // ThinkGear ????嚗? ?餅挾嚗??冽蝎曄Ⅱ閮? High?ow?irebase ?郊?BTI ??蝒?                    r_delta:[], r_theta:[],
                    r_lalpha:[], r_halpha:[], r_lbeta:[], r_hbeta:[], r_lgamma:[], r_hgamma:[],
                    _zeroConsec: 0,   // ?詨捆???                    _poorConsec: 0 }; // ??? att?? AND med?? 閮嚗?券嚗?  // 皜銝?甈∠?銝?霅血???????閮?  ['detect-poor-signal-warn', 'detect-no-signal-warn'].forEach(id => {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if (el) el.remove();
  });
  const stxt = document.getElementById('detect-status-text');
  if (stxt) delete stxt.dataset.poorWarned;
  _eegLiveStartTs = Date.now();
  _eegSimState = null;

  // ? APK ????????交?璈嚗?  try {
    if (window.AndroidBridge && typeof window.AndroidBridge.startStreamingEeg === 'function') {
      window.AndroidBridge.startStreamingEeg();
    }
  } catch (e) { /* ignore */ }

  // 250ms 銝甈∴??湔??/?脣漲嚗蒂嚗汗?冽芋撘?璅⊥銝?蝑?  _eegLiveTimer = setInterval(() => {
    const elapsedSec = Math.floor((Date.now() - _eegLiveStartTs) / 1000);
    const remain = Math.max(0, _eegLiveDurationSec - elapsedSec);
    const fill   = document.getElementById('detect-fill');
    const lbl    = document.getElementById('detect-label');
    const tm     = document.getElementById('detect-time');
    if (fill) fill.style.width = `${Math.min(100, (elapsedSec / _eegLiveDurationSec) * 100)}%`;
    if (lbl)  lbl.textContent  = `撌脫??${Math.min(elapsedSec, _eegLiveDurationSec)} / ${_eegLiveDurationSec} 蝘;
    if (tm)   tm.textContent   = _formatMs(remain);

    // ?汗?冽葫閰佗???AndroidBridge嚗?瘥?~1 蝘芋?砌?蝑?sample
    if (!window.AndroidBridge || typeof window.AndroidBridge.startStreamingEeg !== 'function') {
      _eegSimTick = (_eegSimTick || 0) + 1;
      if (_eegSimTick % 4 === 0) {
        const s = _simNextSample();
        _ingestEegSample(s);
      }
    }

    // ??? ?拇?閮??釭霅血?嚗PK 璅∪?嚗????????????????????????????????
    if (window.AndroidBridge && _eegLiveAccum) {
      const poorConsec = _eegLiveAccum._poorConsec || 0;

      // 15 蝘???? 15 蝑?att?? 銝?med?? ??憿舐內暺霅血?嚗?蝷粹??圈???      if (elapsedSec >= 15 && poorConsec >= 15) {
        const stxt = document.getElementById('detect-status-text');
        if (stxt && !stxt.dataset.poorWarned) {
          stxt.dataset.poorWarned = '1';
          stxt.textContent = '?? ?餅扔?亥孛銝嚗??隤踵?行郭?雿蔭';
        }
        const warnId = 'detect-poor-signal-warn';
        if (!document.getElementById(warnId)) {
          const hint = document.getElementById('detect-hint-card') || document.querySelector('#screen-detect .detect-body');
          if (hint) {
            const warnEl = document.createElement('div');
            warnEl.id = warnId;
            warnEl.style.cssText = 'background:#fff3e0;border:1px solid #ffb74d;border-radius:12px;padding:12px 14px;font-size:12px;color:#e65100;line-height:1.6;margin:10px 16px;';
            warnEl.innerHTML = '?? <b>?餅扔?亥孛銝嚗歇?? 15 蝘?</b><br>'
              + '撠釣 &amp; ?暸??潭扔雿?隞?” ThinkGear ?⊥??菜葫?啗瘜Ｕ?br>'
              + '隢???隤踵?行郭?鞎潛???銝剖? ??蝣箄??惇?餅扔?閫貊??br>'
              + '<b style="color:#c62828;">??30 蝘??芣??蝟餌絞撠?葉甇Ｖ蒂閬??葫??/b>';
            hint.insertBefore(warnEl, hint.firstChild);
          }
        }
      }

      // 30 蝘???? 30 蝑?att?? 銝?med?? ??蝡銝剜迫嚗??圈???皜?      if (elapsedSec >= 30 && poorConsec >= 30) {
        _stopEegLiveCapture();
        _showNoSignalAndRetry(`?餅扔?亥孛銝撌脫?蝥?30 蝘?隢??圈??渲瘜Ｗ?敺?皜穿?撠釣?擛潭?蝥???5嚗);
        return;
      }
    }

    if (elapsedSec >= _eegLiveDurationSec) {
      _stopEegLiveCapture();
      const stxt = document.getElementById('detect-status-text');

      // ??? ?⊿?蝯?敺??喲?霅?????撖西瘜Ｚ???????????????????
      const bwCheck = _validateBrainwaveCapture(window._lastEegCapture);
      if (!bwCheck.ok) {
        // ?∠?撖西?????銝憿舐內????銝???勗?
        if (stxt) stxt.textContent = '?? ?芣?啗瘜Ｚ???;
        // ??⊿??＊蝷粹?皜砍?閰望?嚗?頝喳 screen-results嚗?        _showNoSignalAndRetry(bwCheck.reason);
        return;
      }

      // 撽???敺?摮?DB
      _persistEegStats(window._lastEegCapture);
      if (stxt) stxt.textContent = '???⊿?摰?嚗脣????;
      setTimeout(() => goto('screen-results'), 600);
    }
  }, 250);
}
let _eegSimTick = 0;

function _stopEegLiveCapture() {
  if (_eegLiveTimer) { clearInterval(_eegLiveTimer); _eegLiveTimer = null; }
  try {
    if (window.AndroidBridge && typeof window.AndroidBridge.stopStreamingEeg === 'function') {
      window.AndroidBridge.stopStreamingEeg();
    }
  } catch (e) {}

  // ?敞蝛????啣??敺?撽???敺??澆 _persistEegStats 摮?DB
  // 嚗??冽迨??嚗??鞈芯??舐??豢?鋡怠神?亥??澈嚗?  if (_eegLiveAccum) {
    try {
      window._lastEegCapture = _summarizeEegAccum(_eegLiveAccum);
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('[EEG] _summarizeEegAccum 憭望?嚗蝙?典?憪敞蝛???, e);
      // fallback嚗?亦蝝舐?????像?潘?蝣箔?銝? JS 靘??餅?敺?頝喲?
      const avg = (arr) => arr && arr.length ? Math.round(arr.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/arr.length) : 0;
      window._lastEegCapture = {
        sample_count:          _eegLiveAccum.attn.length,
        attention_percentage:  avg(_eegLiveAccum.attn),
        meditation_percentage: avg(_eegLiveAccum.medi),
        bands_avg: {
          delta: avg(_eegLiveAccum.delta), theta: avg(_eegLiveAccum.theta),
          alpha: avg(_eegLiveAccum.alpha), beta:  avg(_eegLiveAccum.beta),
          gamma: avg(_eegLiveAccum.gamma),
        },
      };
    }
  }
}

// ??暺????????嚗?霅??? DB 銝阡脩???
function _manualStopAndValidate() {
  _stopEegLiveCapture();
  const bwCheck = _validateBrainwaveCapture(window._lastEegCapture);
  if (!bwCheck.ok) {
    _showNoSignalAndRetry(bwCheck.reason);
    return;
  }
  // 撽???敺?摮?DB
  _persistEegStats(window._lastEegCapture);
  goto('screen-results');
}

window._lastEegPersistPromise = null;
function _persistEegStats(stats) {
  if (!stats || !stats.sample_count) {
    window._lastEegPersistPromise = Promise.resolve(null);
    return window._lastEegPersistPromise;
  }
  window._lastEegPersistPromise = (async () => {
    try {
      const reportType = (_currentReportType || '').startsWith('child_') ? 'child' : 'adult';
      const r = await apiFetch('/eeg/save-stats', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          subject_name:          _currentSubjectName || '?葫??,
          subject_birthday:      (document.getElementById('subject-birth-input')||{}).value || '',
          subject_gender:        _currentSubjectGender || '',
          subject_age:           _currentSubjectAge,
          subject_id:            _currentSubjectId,
          report_type:           reportType,
          order_id:              _currentOrderId || null,
          paid_amount:           null,
          sample_count:          stats.sample_count,
          attention_percentage:  stats.attention_percentage,
          meditation_percentage: stats.meditation_percentage,
          bands_avg:             stats.bands_avg,
          raw_arrays:            stats.raw_arrays || null,
        }),
      });
      if (r && r.ok) {
        const d = await r.json();
        window._lastEegSessionId = d.session_id;
        console.log('[EEG] 撌脣神??DB session_id=', d.session_id);
        return d.session_id;
      } else {
        console.warn('[EEG] save-stats 憭望?', r && r.status);
        return null;
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn('[EEG] save-stats 靘?', e);
      return null;
    }
  })();
  return window._lastEegPersistPromise;
}

// ?亙? APK 擗菟脖??蝑?sample嚗? native ?澆 window.JSBridge.onEegSample嚗?// 蝯?撱箄降嚗?-band 摰?澆?嚗?佗?嚗?//   { attn, medi, delta, theta, low_alpha, high_alpha, low_beta, high_beta, low_gamma, high_gamma }
// ???詨捆?澆?嚗?-band ?蔥嚗?
//   { attn, medi, delta, theta, alpha, beta, gamma }
// 頝舐嚗瘜Ｗ??亙熒瑼Ｘ???鞈??芷脣摨瑟炎??sink嚗????蜓瘚???_eegLiveAccum
window.JSBridge = window.JSBridge || {};
window.JSBridge.onEegSample = function(payload) {
  try {
    const obj = (typeof payload === 'string') ? JSON.parse(payload) : payload;
    if (window._eegHealthActive) {
      _eegHealthSampleSink(obj);
      return;
    }
    _ingestEegSample(obj);
  } catch (e) { console.warn('onEegSample parse error', e); }
};

function _ingestEegSample(s) {
  if (!_eegLiveAccum) return;
  const clamp = (v) => Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Number(v) || 0));
  const a = clamp(s.attn);
  const m = clamp(s.medi);
  const d = clamp(s.delta);
  const th= clamp(s.theta);
  // ?舀 8-band嚗ow_alpha/high_alpha 蝑???5-band嚗lpha 蝑?
  const loAl = s.low_alpha  != null ? clamp(s.low_alpha)  : null;
  const hiAl = s.high_alpha != null ? clamp(s.high_alpha) : null;
  const loBe = s.low_beta   != null ? clamp(s.low_beta)   : null;
  const hiBe = s.high_beta  != null ? clamp(s.high_beta)  : null;
  const loGa = s.low_gamma  != null ? clamp(s.low_gamma)  : null;
  const hiGa = s.high_gamma != null ? clamp(s.high_gamma) : null;
  // ?蔥?潘???澆停?典像???血??湔??alpha/beta/gamma
  const al= (loAl != null && hiAl != null) ? Math.round((loAl+hiAl)/2) : clamp(s.alpha);
  const be= (loBe != null && hiBe != null) ? Math.round((loBe+hiBe)/2) : clamp(s.beta);
  const ga= (loGa != null && hiGa != null) ? Math.round((loGa+hiGa)/2) : clamp(s.gamma);

  // ?湔?行郭??駁?嚗???APK ?典 bat ??堆??踹?閬? AndroidBridge 頛芾岷?潘?
  if (typeof s.bat === 'number' && s.bat >= 0) {
    const pct = s.bat;
    document.querySelectorAll('.eeg-battery').forEach(function(el) {
      el.textContent = '??' + pct + '%';
      el.className = 'eeg-battery ' + (pct >= 60 ? 'high' : pct >= 20 ? 'mid' : 'low');
    });
  }

  _eegLiveAccum.attn.push(a);
  _eegLiveAccum.medi.push(m);
  _eegLiveAccum.delta.push(d);
  _eegLiveAccum.theta.push(th);
  _eegLiveAccum.alpha.push(al);
  _eegLiveAccum.beta.push(be);
  _eegLiveAccum.gamma.push(ga);
  // 8-band嚗??敞蝛??∪??仿?嚗summarizeEegAccum ??瑟?行?鞈?嚗?  if (loAl != null) _eegLiveAccum.low_alpha.push(loAl);
  if (hiAl != null) _eegLiveAccum.high_alpha.push(hiAl);
  if (loBe != null) _eegLiveAccum.low_beta.push(loBe);
  if (hiBe != null) _eegLiveAccum.high_beta.push(hiBe);
  if (loGa != null) _eegLiveAccum.low_gamma.push(loGa);
  if (hiGa != null) _eegLiveAccum.high_gamma.push(hiGa);

  // ThinkGear ????嚗 8 ?餅挾嚗?瘥?敹??券 push嚗 0嚗?靽? index 撠?
  // ?券?BrainDNA 雿?瞍?瘜?瘥? ??餅挾 繩 ?恍畾萇蜇????180 蝘像??
  const _raw = (v) => Math.max(0, Number(v) || 0);
  const _rD  = _raw(s.raw_delta);
  const _rTh = _raw(s.raw_theta);
  const _rLa = _raw(s.raw_lalpha);
  const _rHa = _raw(s.raw_halpha);
  const _rLb = _raw(s.raw_lbeta);
  const _rHb = _raw(s.raw_hbeta);
  const _rLg = _raw(s.raw_lgamma);
  const _rHg = _raw(s.raw_hgamma);
  _eegLiveAccum.r_delta.push(_rD);
  _eegLiveAccum.r_theta.push(_rTh);
  _eegLiveAccum.r_lalpha.push(_rLa);
  _eegLiveAccum.r_halpha.push(_rHa);
  _eegLiveAccum.r_lbeta.push(_rLb);
  _eegLiveAccum.r_hbeta.push(_rHb);
  _eegLiveAccum.r_lgamma.push(_rLg);
  _eegLiveAccum.r_hgamma.push(_rHg);

  // ??? ?⊿??恍?單? bar嚗?蝯????勗?雿輻摰?詨???BrainDNA proportionRange 瞍?瘜????
  // 鞈? ??5 蝘??嚗?頞?5 蝘?隞乓????雿???皜⊿＊蝷?  let displayD = d, displayTh = th, displayAl = al, displayBe = be, displayGa = ga;
  const _isChildLive = (_currentReportType || '').toLowerCase().indexOf('child') !== -1;
  const _liveBdna = _calcBrainDNABands(_eegLiveAccum, _isChildLive);
  if (_liveBdna) {
    displayD  = _liveBdna.deV;
    displayTh = _liveBdna.thV;
    displayAl = Math.round((_liveBdna.loA + _liveBdna.hiA) / 2);
    displayBe = Math.round((_liveBdna.loB + _liveBdna.hiB) / 2);
    displayGa = Math.round((_liveBdna.loG + _liveBdna.hiG) / 2);
  } else {
    const _rTot = _rD + _rTh + _rLa + _rHa + _rLb + _rHb + _rLg + _rHg;
    if (_rTot > 0) {
      displayD  = Math.round(_rD  / _rTot * 100);
      displayTh = Math.round(_rTh / _rTot * 100);
      displayAl = Math.round((_rLa + _rHa) / _rTot * 100);
      displayBe = Math.round((_rLb + _rHb) / _rTot * 100);
      displayGa = Math.round((_rLg + _rHg) / _rTot * 100);
    }
  }

  // ???雿?鞈芾??賂?att ??5 AND med ??5嚗?券嚗? ?餅扔?亥孛銝
  // ThinkGear 閬嚗tt=0 ?Ⅱ隞?”?霈?詻?1-5 ?冽迤撣賊??港?璆萎??航?箇
  if (a <= 5 && m <= 5) {
    _eegLiveAccum._poorConsec = (_eegLiveAccum._poorConsec || 0) + 1;
    _eegLiveAccum._zeroConsec = _eegLiveAccum._poorConsec; // ?詨捆???  } else {
    _eegLiveAccum._poorConsec = 0;  // ?芾???蝑??停?身
    _eegLiveAccum._zeroConsec = 0;
  }

  // ?湔 UI
  const set = (id, v) => { const e = document.getElementById(id); if (e) e.textContent = String(v); };
  const setW = (id, w) => { const e = document.getElementById(id); if (e) e.style.width  = `${w}%`; };
  const setH = (id, h) => { const e = document.getElementById(id); if (e) e.style.height = `${h}%`; };
  const lvl = (v) => v >= 70 ? '擃? : v >= 35 ? '銝? : '雿?;

  set('metric-attn-val', a); setW('metric-attn-bar', a);
  set('metric-medi-val', m); setW('metric-medi-bar', m);

  set('band-val-delta', lvl(displayD)); setH('band-bar-delta', displayD);
  set('band-val-theta', lvl(displayTh));setH('band-bar-theta', displayTh);
  set('band-val-alpha', lvl(displayAl));setH('band-bar-alpha', displayAl);
  set('band-val-beta',  lvl(displayBe));setH('band-bar-beta',  displayBe);
  set('band-val-gamma', lvl(displayGa));setH('band-bar-gamma', displayGa);

  // 帣/帢 瘥潘??見雿輻 BrainDNA proportionRange ?潘?
  const ratio = displayAl > 0 ? (displayBe / displayAl) : 0;
  const ratioEl = document.getElementById('faa-ratio');
  const hintEl  = document.getElementById('faa-hint');
  const barEl   = document.getElementById('faa-bar');
  if (ratioEl) ratioEl.textContent = ratio.toFixed(2);
  if (hintEl)  hintEl.textContent  = ratio >= 1 ? `憭??曉?嚗?> 帢嚗 : `?批??曉?嚗?< 帢嚗;
  if (barEl) {
    // ratio 0~2 撠? 0%~100%
    const pct = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, ratio * 50));
    barEl.style.width = `${pct}%`;
  }

  // ?單???霈 chips
  const chips = document.getElementById('detect-chips');
  if (chips) {
    const tags = [];
    if (a >= 60) tags.push({txt:'? 撠釣?臬末',  c:'#00C853', bg:'rgba(0,200,83,0.2)'});
    else if (a <= 35) tags.push({txt:'? 撠釣??', c:'#ef5350', bg:'rgba(239,83,80,0.2)'});
    if (m >= 60) tags.push({txt:'? ?暸??臬末',  c:'#00BCD4', bg:'rgba(0,188,212,0.2)'});
    else if (m <= 35) tags.push({txt:'?? ??蝜?,  c:'#ff9800', bg:'rgba(255,152,0,0.2)'});
    if (displayAl >= Math.max(displayBe, displayTh)) tags.push({txt:'? Alpha 銝餃?', c:'#4caf50', bg:'rgba(76,175,80,0.2)'});
    if (displayBe >  displayAl) tags.push({txt:'??Beta 銝餃?', c:'#ff9800', bg:'rgba(255,152,0,0.2)'});
    if (displayTh >  displayAl) tags.push({txt:'?? Theta 銝餃?', c:'#9c27b0', bg:'rgba(156,39,176,0.2)'});
    chips.innerHTML = tags.map(t =>
      `<span style="background:${t.bg};color:${t.c};padding:4px 10px;border-radius:10px;font-size:11px;font-weight:600;">${t.txt}</span>`
    ).join('') || '<span style="color:rgba(255,255,255,0.4);font-size:12px;">鞈??園?銝凌?/span>';
  }
}

function _summarizeEegAccum(acc) {
  const avg = (arr) => arr.length ? Math.round(arr.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0) / arr.length) : 0;

  // BrainDNA MindValueTop嚗??餅挾??憪潔???頞??單?瘀?
  const CAP = { r_delta:98000, r_theta:98000,
                r_lalpha:50000, r_halpha:50000,
                r_lbeta:50000,  r_hbeta:50000,
                r_lgamma:10000, r_hgamma:10000 };
  const RKEYS = ['r_delta','r_theta','r_lalpha','r_halpha','r_lbeta','r_hbeta','r_lgamma','r_hgamma'];

  // ??r_lalpha ?鞈?????8-band 璅∪?嚗ndroid v1.1.9+ ??raw_lalpha嚗?  // 瘜冽?嚗ingestEegSample ?璇辣 push 0嚗??瑼Ｘ?臬???嗅?  const has8 = (acc.r_lalpha && acc.r_lalpha.some(v => v > 0));

  const thetaVal = avg(acc.theta);

  const bands = {
    delta: avg(acc.delta),
    theta: thetaVal,
    alpha: avg(acc.alpha),
    beta:  avg(acc.beta),
    gamma: avg(acc.gamma),
  };
  // ?交? 8-band ?鞈?嚗?雿萄??伐?敺垢 eeg/save-stats ???亙摮?low_* / high_*嚗?  let bands_7 = null;
  if (has8) {
    // BrainDNA 雿?瞍?瘜?摰?拇郊撽?嚗?蝡乩蝙??CHILD_PROP_RANGE ?曉?    const _isChild = (_currentReportType || '').startsWith('child_') || _currentReportType === 'child';
    const bdna = _calcBrainDNABands(acc, _isChild);
    const deV = bdna ? bdna.deV : avg(acc.delta);
    const thV = bdna ? bdna.thV : avg(acc.theta);
    const loA = bdna ? bdna.loA : 0;
    const hiA = bdna ? bdna.hiA : 0;
    const loB = bdna ? bdna.loB : 0;
    const hiB = bdna ? bdna.hiB : 0;
    const loG = bdna ? bdna.loG : 0;
    const hiG = bdna ? bdna.hiG : 0;
    // 撖怠 bands_avg嚗靘輯?頝臬?霈??
    bands.delta      = deV;
    bands.theta      = thV;
    bands.low_alpha  = loA;  bands.high_alpha = hiA;
    bands.low_beta   = loB;  bands.high_beta  = hiB;
    bands.low_gamma  = loG;  bands.high_gamma = hiG;
    // ??撱箇? bands_7嚗?敺垢 _session_to_brainwave_data ?澆?銝?湛?霈?headless_renderer ?芸?霈??
    bands_7 = {
      delta:      deV,
      theta:      thV,   // ?曉??蝡?DB 銝?湛?BrainDNA proportionRange嚗?      alpha_high: hiA, alpha_low: loA,
      beta_high:  hiB, beta_low:  loB,
      gamma_high: hiG, gamma_low: loG,
    };
  }
  const result = {
    sample_count:          acc.attn.length,
    attention_percentage:  avg(acc.attn),
    meditation_percentage: avg(acc.medi),
    bands_avg:             bands,
  };
  if (bands_7) result.bands_7 = bands_7;

  // 靽????????嚗? Firebase ?行郭鞈?摨?& MBTI ??蝒??蝙?剁?
  // 瘥?摨衣? 180嚗? ?? ? 1Hz嚗??喟策敺垢敺???Firebase Firestore + BigQuery
  result.raw_arrays = {
    attn:     (acc.attn    || []).slice(),   // 瘥? attention嚗?-100嚗?    medi:     (acc.medi    || []).slice(),   // 瘥? meditation嚗?-100嚗?    r_delta:  (acc.r_delta || []).slice(),   // ?? ThinkGear delta ?餅挾??
    r_theta:  (acc.r_theta || []).slice(),   // ?? theta嚗BTI ??蝒??敹?
    r_lalpha: (acc.r_lalpha|| []).slice(),   // ?? lowAlpha嚗BTI ??蝒??敹?
    r_halpha: (acc.r_halpha|| []).slice(),   // ?? highAlpha
    r_lbeta:  (acc.r_lbeta || []).slice(),   // ?? lowBeta
    r_hbeta:  (acc.r_hbeta || []).slice(),   // ?? highBeta
    r_lgamma: (acc.r_lgamma|| []).slice(),   // ?? lowGamma
    r_hgamma: (acc.r_hgamma|| []).slice(),   // ?? highGamma
  };
  return result;
}

// ?祕???瘜Ｗ‵??screen-results ??chip / bw-row??// data-key 撠?嚗?//   intuition  = theta            ?暸? = meditation_percentage
//   attention  = attention_pct    vitality   = alpha (銝?
//   calmness   = alpha (銝?       hyperfocus = beta  (銝?
//   logic      = beta  (銝?       observation= gamma (銝?
//   compassion = gamma (銝?
function _renderResultsFromEeg() {
  const stats = window._lastEegCapture;
  const meta  = document.getElementById('result-meta');
  if (meta) {
    const name = _currentSubjectName || '?葫??;
    const age  = _currentSubjectAge != null ? ` 繚 ${_currentSubjectAge}甇深 : '';
    const ts   = new Date().toLocaleString('zh-TW', { year: 'numeric', month: '2-digit', day: '2-digit', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
    const sc   = stats?.sample_count ? `${stats.sample_count} 蝑 : '撠?⊿?';
    meta.textContent = `${name}${age} 繚 ${ts} 繚 ${sc}`;
  }
  // ?典???葡???瘜Ｗ?皜祈岫?????狡嚗? ID ?韌??r2-嚗?  if (_eegLiveAccum && _eegLiveAccum.attn && _eegLiveAccum.attn.length) {
    _renderBrainAnalysisInto('r2-');
  }
  // ?日?Ｘ嚗?恣?/撌乩?鈭箏?航?
  const u = _currentUser || {};
  const isStaff = u.role === 'admin' || u.org_type === '撌乩?鈭箏';
  const panel = document.getElementById('eeg-debug-panel');
  if (panel) panel.style.display = isStaff ? '' : 'none';
  if (isStaff && stats) {
    const b7 = stats.bands_7 || {};
    const ba = stats.bands_avg || {};
    const lines = [
      `璅???  ${stats.sample_count ?? '??}`,
      `瘜冽???  ${stats.attention_percentage ?? '??}  ?暸?摨? ${stats.meditation_percentage ?? '??}`,
      `??? BrainDNA 雿?蝞? ???????`,
      `帤  delta:      ${b7.delta      ?? ba.delta      ?? '??}`,
      `庛  theta:      ${b7.theta      ?? ba.theta      ?? '??}`,
      `帢  low_alpha:  ${b7.alpha_low  ?? b7.low_alpha  ?? ba.low_alpha  ?? '??}`,
      `帢  high_alpha: ${b7.alpha_high ?? b7.high_alpha ?? ba.high_alpha ?? '??}`,
      `帣  low_beta:   ${b7.beta_low   ?? b7.low_beta   ?? ba.low_beta   ?? '??}`,
      `帣  high_beta:  ${b7.beta_high  ?? b7.high_beta  ?? ba.high_beta  ?? '??}`,
      `帠  low_gamma:  ${b7.gamma_low  ?? b7.low_gamma  ?? ba.low_gamma  ?? '??}`,
      `帠  high_gamma: ${b7.gamma_high ?? b7.high_gamma ?? ba.high_gamma ?? '??}`,
    ];
    const dbgEl = document.getElementById('eeg-debug-content');
    if (dbgEl) dbgEl.textContent = lines.join('\n');
  }
}

// 蝪∪?冽?韏啣璅⊥?剁??汗?冽葫閰衣嚗?function _simNextSample() {
  if (!_eegSimState) {
    _eegSimState = {
      attn:55, medi:55,
      delta:35, theta:45, alpha:55, beta:45, gamma:25,
    };
  }
  const step = (cur, lo, hi, drift) => {
    let v = cur + (Math.random() - 0.5) * drift;
    if (v < lo) v = lo + Math.random()*4;
    if (v > hi) v = hi - Math.random()*4;
    return Math.round(v);
  };
  _eegSimState.attn  = step(_eegSimState.attn , 25, 95, 14);
  _eegSimState.medi  = step(_eegSimState.medi , 25, 95, 12);
  _eegSimState.delta = step(_eegSimState.delta, 15, 60, 8);
  _eegSimState.theta = step(_eegSimState.theta, 25, 70, 10);
  _eegSimState.alpha = step(_eegSimState.alpha, 30, 85, 12);
  _eegSimState.beta  = step(_eegSimState.beta , 25, 80, 12);
  _eegSimState.gamma = step(_eegSimState.gamma, 10, 55, 8);
  return { ..._eegSimState };
}

// ?亙熒瑼Ｘ撠????蜓瘚?摰?嚗?let _ethcState = null;     // { startTs, sampleCount, signals:[], batteries:[], lastSampleTs, abort, durationSec }

// ?亙嚗?????恬?
function startEegTest() {
  _exitEegTestMode();  // 皜?隞颱?畾????  goto('screen-eegtest-connect');
  setTimeout(_refreshEegHealthPreCheck, 100);
  if (_ethcPreCheckTimer) clearInterval(_ethcPreCheckTimer);
  _ethcPreCheckTimer = setInterval(_refreshEegHealthPreCheck, 3000);
}

// ????????炎?伐?瘥?3 蝘?唬?甈∴?
function _refreshEegHealthPreCheck() {
  const list = document.getElementById('ethc-precheck-list');
  const startBtn = document.getElementById('ethc-start-btn');
  if (!list) return;
  const bridge = (typeof AndroidBridge !== 'undefined' && AndroidBridge) ? AndroidBridge : null;
  const rows = [];

  if (!bridge) {
    rows.push({ ok:false, label:'?瑁??啣?', val:'?汗?冽芋撘?繚 ?⊥?皜祈岫??閮剖?嚗?雿輻 APP ??嚗? });
    list.innerHTML = rows.map(_ethcRowHtml).join('');
    if (startBtn) { startBtn.disabled = true; startBtn.style.opacity = '0.4'; }
    return;
  }

  // BLE 閮箸鞈?嚗???剁?
  let dbg = null;
  try {
    if (typeof bridge.getBleDebugInfo === 'function') {
      dbg = JSON.parse(bridge.getBleDebugInfo());
    }
  } catch(e) {}

  // ?單??駁?嚗?鞈?????銝??粹???斗靘?嚗?  // ?典?璈?嚗?蝐??? BLE 撽?嚗瘝?蝵格?銋??摰?100嚗?隞仿???賭??粹??霅?
  let bat = -1;
  try { bat = bridge.getDeviceBattery && bridge.getDeviceBattery(); } catch(e) {}
  if (typeof bat !== 'number') bat = parseInt(bat, 10);
  if (isNaN(bat)) bat = -1;
  const batReadable = (bat >= 1 && bat <= 100);

  // ??????蝖祆扳?隞???靘 ThinkGear ?????嚗?  let connected = false;
  try { connected = bridge.isBrainwaveConnected && bridge.isBrainwaveConnected(); } catch(e) {}

  // 撌脤?撠?蝵格嚗??????瞈暹?隞塚?
  const pairedCount = (dbg && (dbg.paired || dbg.paired_count)) || 0;
  const hasMindsensor = !!(dbg && dbg.mindsensor);

  // ?? ?迤????文?嚗?潘???
  // 敹?璇辣嚗?1) BLE ???????true  銝? (2) Android 蝟餌絞?扳???鋆蔭
  // 銝?閮勗???敺?隢撌脤??嚗?餈唳???false-positive嚗?  const trulyConnected = !!(connected && (pairedCount > 0 || hasMindsensor));

  if (dbg) {
    rows.push({ ok: !dbg.adapter_null, label:'??璅∠?', val: dbg.adapter_null ? '??蝟餌絞?芣?靘????? : '???舐' });
    rows.push({
      ok: !!dbg.bt_enabled,
      label:'????',
      val: dbg.bt_enabled ? '??撌脤??? : '???芷???隢蝟餌絞閮剖???嚗?
    });
    rows.push({ ok: !!dbg.perm_connect, label:'???甈?', val: dbg.perm_connect ? '??撌脫?甈? : '???芣?甈?隢?閮?APP ????甈?嚗? });
    rows.push({ ok: !!dbg.perm_scan,    advisory:true, label:'??甈?',   val: dbg.perm_scan    ? '??撌脫?甈? : '?對? ?芣?甈?撌脤???喳嚗?甈⊿????閬?' });
    if (dbg.perm_fine_loc !== undefined) {
      rows.push({ ok: !!dbg.perm_fine_loc, advisory:true, label:'雿蔭甈?',   val: dbg.perm_fine_loc ? '??撌脫?甈? : '?對? ?芣?甈??典? Android ?閬?' });
    }
    rows.push({
      ok: pairedCount > 0,
      label:'撌脤?撠?蝵?,
      val: pairedCount > 0
        ? (pairedCount + ' ?? + (dbg.mindsensor ? '嚗嚗? + dbg.mindsensor + '嚗? : ''))
        : '???芷?撠?隢蝟餌絞??閮剖???撠瘜Ｗ?嚗?
    });
  }

  // ?行郭????嚗′?扳?隞塚?銝?鋡恍?炊撠?  rows.push({
    ok: trulyConnected,
    label:'?行郭????',
    val: trulyConnected
      ? '??撌脤??'
      : (connected && pairedCount === 0
          ? '???芷??嚗???璅???雿??鋆蔭嚗??蝟餌絞閮剖????行郭?嚗?
          : '???芷??嚗?蝣箄??行郭?撌脤?璈蒂摰???嚗?)
  });

  // ?駁?嚗?撱?擃???祕?駁?嚗摰??喳??潘?銝敺＊蝷箝?撱????  rows.push({ ok: null, advisory:true, label:'?駁?霈??, val: '???芣?靘? });

  // 閮??釭嚗????憿舐內?詨?  // ??嚗indsensor ???餅扔?芣閫貊??? PoorSignal=0嚗炊?勗?蝢?嚗?  // ??????啁?瘞賊???100嚗瘜???撖行閫貊???  // ?祕閮??釭???憪摨瑟炎?乓??蝔葉????  if (trulyConnected) {
    rows.push({ ok: null, advisory:true, label:'閮??釭', val: '????脯?憪?1 ???亙熒瑼Ｘ?????? });
  }

  list.innerHTML = rows.map(_ethcRowHtml).join('');

  // ??????璇辣嚗?????????箸甈? OK
  const blockingOk = trulyConnected && (
    !dbg || (
      !dbg.adapter_null &&
      !!dbg.bt_enabled &&
      dbg.perm_connect
    )
  );
  if (startBtn) {
    startBtn.disabled = !blockingOk;
    startBtn.style.opacity = blockingOk ? '1' : '0.55';
    // 瘞賊?憿舐內?? ????璅???踹?雿輻?炊隞亦?瘨仃
    // ?芷?????銝餅?憿???+ ?舀?憿?蝷粹?閬????憿?    startBtn.innerHTML = blockingOk
      ? '<div style="font-size:18px;font-weight:700;">???? 1 ???亙熒瑼Ｘ ??/div>'
      : '<div style="font-size:18px;font-weight:700;">?梧? ?? 1 ???亙熒瑼Ｘ</div>'
        + '<div style="font-size:12px;font-weight:500;margin-top:4px;opacity:0.95;">?? 隢?蝣箄??行郭?撌脤??嚗???/ 甈? / ??嚗?/div>';
  }
  // 撘瑕???嚗?其蜓??銝??＊蝷綽?蝯虫蝙?刻?蝣箏? OK??????  const forceBtn = document.getElementById('ethc-force-start-btn');
  if (forceBtn) {
    forceBtn.style.display = blockingOk ? 'none' : 'block';
  }
}

function _ethcRowHtml(r) {
  // advisory 憭望?嚗??餅?嚗? ?啗嚗′?批仃????蝝嚗K ??蝬
  let color;
  if (r.ok) color = '#2e7d32';
  else if (r.advisory) color = '#757575';
  else color = '#c62828';
  return `<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:flex-start;gap:8px;">
    <span style="color:#555;flex-shrink:0;">${r.label}</span>
    <span style="color:${color};font-weight:600;text-align:right;">${r.val}</span>
  </div>`;
}

function _eegHealthReconnect() {
  try {
    if (typeof AndroidBridge !== 'undefined' && AndroidBridge && typeof AndroidBridge.reconnectBrainwave === 'function') {
      AndroidBridge.reconnectBrainwave();
      alert('撌脣?閰阡??圈?撠瘜Ｗ???蝑? 3-5 蝘??炎?仿?????);
    } else {
      alert('甇文??賢???APP ?批?剁??汗?函瘜?雿?????);
    }
  } catch(e) { alert('???憭望?嚗? + e.message); }
  setTimeout(_refreshEegHealthPreCheck, 1500);
}

// ?? 1 ???亙熒瑼Ｘ?⊿?
function _startEegHealthCheckSession() {
  // 皜? PreCheck 頛芾岷
  if (_ethcPreCheckTimer) { clearInterval(_ethcPreCheckTimer); _ethcPreCheckTimer = null; }

  _ethcState = {
    startTs: Date.now(),
    sampleCount: 0,
    signals: [],          // ?? iGoodSignal ?詨潘?0=摰?, 200=?⊥閫賂?嚗?蝘?蝑?    batteries: [],        // ?⊿????????    lastSampleTs: 0,
    abort: false,
    durationSec: 60,
    bridgeAvailable: typeof AndroidBridge !== 'undefined' && !!AndroidBridge,
  };
  window._eegHealthActive = true;  // 頝舐璅?嚗SBridge.onEegSample ?粥?亙熒瑼Ｘ sink
  // ?蔭 MBTI ??鞈?蝝舐??剁?瘥活?亙熒瑼Ｘ?函?嚗?  window._ethrAccum = { attn:[], medi:[], delta:[], theta:[], alpha:[], beta:[], gamma:[] };

  // ??銝脫?嚗?? APP ?改?
  try {
    if (_ethcState.bridgeAvailable && typeof AndroidBridge.startStreamingEeg === 'function') {
      AndroidBridge.startStreamingEeg();
    }
  } catch(e) {}

  goto('screen-eegtest-detect');

  // 250ms 銝甈⊥??UI
  if (_ethcTimer) clearInterval(_ethcTimer);
  _ethcTimer = setInterval(_tickEegHealthCheck, 250);
  _tickEegHealthCheck();  // 蝡?思?甈?}

function _tickEegHealthCheck() {
  if (!_ethcState) return;
  const elapsed = Math.floor((Date.now() - _ethcState.startTs) / 1000);
  const remain = Math.max(0, _ethcState.durationSec - elapsed);

  // ?
  const mm = String(Math.floor(remain / 60)).padStart(2, '0');
  const ss = String(remain % 60).padStart(2, '0');
  const tEl = document.getElementById('ethc-timer');
  if (tEl) tEl.textContent = mm + ':' + ss;

  const pct = Math.min(100, (elapsed / _ethcState.durationSec) * 100);
  const pBar = document.getElementById('ethc-progress');
  if (pBar) pBar.style.width = pct + '%';
  const pLbl = document.getElementById('ethc-progress-label');
  if (pLbl) pLbl.textContent = `撌脫??${Math.min(elapsed, _ethcState.durationSec)} / ${_ethcState.durationSec} 蝘;

  // ?單???
  const bridge = (typeof AndroidBridge !== 'undefined' && AndroidBridge) ? AndroidBridge : null;
  let bt = false, conn = false, bat = -1;
  if (bridge) {
    try { bt = JSON.parse(bridge.getBleDebugInfo && bridge.getBleDebugInfo() || '{}').bt_enabled || false; } catch(e) {}
    try { conn = bridge.isBrainwaveConnected && bridge.isBrainwaveConnected(); } catch(e) {}
    try { bat = bridge.getDeviceBattery && bridge.getDeviceBattery(); } catch(e) {}
    if (typeof bat !== 'number') bat = parseInt(bat, 10);
    if (isNaN(bat)) bat = -1;
    if (bat >= 1 && bat <= 100) _ethcState.batteries.push(bat);
  }
  _setText('ethc-stat-bt',   bridge ? (bt ? '??撌脤??? : '???芷???) : '???汗?冽芋撘?);
  _setText('ethc-stat-conn', bridge ? (conn ? '??撌脤??' : '???芷??') : '???汗?冽芋撘?);
  _setText('ethc-stat-samples', _ethcState.sampleCount + ' 蝑?);
  const rate = elapsed > 0 ? (_ethcState.sampleCount / elapsed).toFixed(1) : '??;
  _setText('ethc-stat-rate', rate + ' 蝑?蝘?);

  // 閮??釭嚗?蝙?典?撱?iGoodSignal嚗?=摰?, 200=?⊥閫賂?嚗allback ?冽璅???函?
  let signalLabel = '撠?交';
  if (_ethcState.sampleCount === 0 && elapsed > 5) {
    signalLabel = '???∟???;
  } else if (_ethcState.sampleCount > 0) {
    const lastSec = (Date.now() - _ethcState.lastSampleTs) / 1000;
    if (lastSec > 5) {
      signalLabel = '??閮?銝剜';
    } else {
      const sigArr = _ethcState.signals;
      if (sigArr.length > 0) {
        // ??餈?5 蝑?撟喳?嚗??????
        // iGoodSignal = (200 - PoorSignal) / 2嚗???0~100嚗?潸?擃?憟?        const recent = sigArr.slice(-5);
        const avgSig = recent.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0) / recent.length;
        if (avgSig >= 95)      signalLabel = '??摰?嚗? + Math.round(avgSig) + '嚗?;
        else if (avgSig >= 85) signalLabel = '???芾嚗? + Math.round(avgSig) + '嚗?;
        else if (avgSig >= 70) signalLabel = '???臬末嚗? + Math.round(avgSig) + '嚗?;
        else if (avgSig >= 50) signalLabel = '??撠嚗? + Math.round(avgSig) + '嚗?;
        else if (avgSig >= 30) signalLabel = '??銝蔔嚗? + Math.round(avgSig) + '嚗?隤踵???圈???;
        else                   signalLabel = '???餅扔?芣閫賂?' + Math.round(avgSig) + '嚗?隤踵???圈???;
      } else {
        // ?? Java 撠??signal 甈???fallback
        if (_ethcState.sampleCount / Math.max(1, elapsed) >= 0.6) signalLabel = '???臬末';
        else signalLabel = '??閮?撘?;
      }
    }
  }
  _setText('ethc-stat-signal', signalLabel);

  // ?駁?嚗?撱?擃???祕?駁?嚗?敺＊蝷箝?撱????  _setText('ethc-stat-bat', '???芣?靘?);

  if (elapsed >= _ethcState.durationSec) {
    _finishEegHealthCheck(false);
  }
}

function _setText(id, v) { const e = document.getElementById(id); if (e) e.textContent = v; }

// ?亙熒瑼Ｘ璅??交?剁???window.JSBridge.onEegSample 頝舐?脖?嚗蜓瘚???_ingestEegSample 銝?鋡怠?恬?
function _eegHealthSampleSink(obj) {
  if (!_ethcState) return;
  _ethcState.sampleCount++;
  _ethcState.lastSampleTs = Date.now();
  // ?交? bat 甈?嚗靽∟瘜Ｗ????  if (obj && typeof obj.bat === 'number' && obj.bat >= 1 && obj.bat <= 100) {
    _ethcState.batteries.push(obj.bat);
  }
  // ?? iGoodSignal 閮??釭嚗?=摰?, 200=?⊥閫賂?
  if (obj && typeof obj.signal === 'number' && obj.signal >= 0 && obj.signal <= 200) {
    _ethcState.signals.push(obj.signal);
  }
  // 蝝舐??畾菔??策 MBTI ??雿輻嚗?撖怠 DB / 銝?啣?蝡荔?蝝?蝡航?蝞?
  if (!window._ethrAccum) {
    window._ethrAccum = {
      attn:[], medi:[], delta:[], theta:[], alpha:[], beta:[], gamma:[],
      r_delta:[], r_theta:[],
      r_lalpha:[], r_halpha:[],
      r_lbeta:[],  r_hbeta:[],
      r_lgamma:[], r_hgamma:[],
    };
  }
  const acc = window._ethrAccum;
  const num = (v) => (typeof v === 'number' && isFinite(v)) ? v : 0;
  acc.attn.push(num(obj && obj.attn));
  acc.medi.push(num(obj && obj.medi));
  acc.delta.push(num(obj && obj.delta));
  acc.theta.push(num(obj && obj.theta));
  acc.alpha.push(num(obj && obj.alpha));
  acc.beta .push(num(obj && obj.beta));
  acc.gamma.push(num(obj && obj.gamma));
  // ?? ThinkGear 8 ?餅挾嚗???撌桃嚗??MBTI 閮?嚗?  acc.r_delta .push(num(obj && obj.raw_delta));
  acc.r_theta .push(num(obj && obj.raw_theta));
  acc.r_lalpha.push(num(obj && obj.raw_lalpha));
  acc.r_halpha.push(num(obj && obj.raw_halpha));
  acc.r_lbeta .push(num(obj && obj.raw_lbeta));
  acc.r_hbeta .push(num(obj && obj.raw_hbeta));
  acc.r_lgamma.push(num(obj && obj.raw_lgamma));
  acc.r_hgamma.push(num(obj && obj.raw_hgamma));
}

// 蝯?嚗???蝙?刻???????
function _finishEegHealthCheck(byUser) {
  if (!_ethcState) return;
  const state = _ethcState;
  _ethcState = null;
  window._eegHealthActive = false;
  if (_ethcTimer) { clearInterval(_ethcTimer); _ethcTimer = null; }

  // ?迫銝脫?
  try {
    if (typeof AndroidBridge !== 'undefined' && AndroidBridge && typeof AndroidBridge.stopStreamingEeg === 'function') {
      AndroidBridge.stopStreamingEeg();
    }
  } catch(e) {}

  _renderEegHealthResult(state, byUser);
  goto('screen-eegtest-result');
}

// 銝剝??????嚗?function _abortEegHealthCheck() {
  if (!_ethcState) return;
  _ethcState = null;
  window._eegHealthActive = false;
  if (_ethcTimer) { clearInterval(_ethcTimer); _ethcTimer = null; }
  try {
    if (typeof AndroidBridge !== 'undefined' && AndroidBridge && typeof AndroidBridge.stopStreamingEeg === 'function') {
      AndroidBridge.stopStreamingEeg();
    }
  } catch(e) {}
}

// 皜脫?蝯???function _renderEegHealthResult(state, byUser) {
  const elapsed = Math.floor((Date.now() - state.startTs) / 1000);
  const elapsedSec = Math.min(elapsed, state.durationSec);
  const sampleCount = state.sampleCount;
  const expectedSamples = elapsedSec; // 蝝?蝘?1 蝑?  const sampleRate = elapsedSec > 0 ? (sampleCount / elapsedSec) : 0;
  const batAvg = state.batteries.length ? Math.round(state.batteries.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0) / state.batteries.length) : -1;
  const batMin = state.batteries.length ? Math.min(...state.batteries) : -1;
  const batMax = state.batteries.length ? Math.max(...state.batteries) : -1;

  // ??閮??釭嚗GoodSignal = (200 - PoorSignal) / 2嚗???0~100嚗?潸?擃?憟?  // 撟喳???撌殷??雿???  const hasRealSig   = state.signals.length > 0;
  const sigAvg       = hasRealSig ? state.signals.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0) / state.signals.length : -1;
  const sigWorst     = hasRealSig ? Math.min(...state.signals) : -1;  // ?撌?= ?雿?  // 閮??釭蝑?
  function _sigGrade(avg) {
    if (avg < 0)    return { ok: null,  label: '???⊥??????嚗? };
    if (avg >= 95)  return { ok: true,  label: '摰?嚗? + Math.round(avg) + '嚗? };
    if (avg >= 85)  return { ok: true,  label: '?芾嚗? + Math.round(avg) + '嚗? };
    if (avg >= 70)  return { ok: true,  label: '?臬末嚗? + Math.round(avg) + '嚗? };
    if (avg >= 50)  return { ok: null,  label: '撠嚗? + Math.round(avg) + '嚗? };
    if (avg >= 30)  return { ok: false, label: '銝蔔嚗? + Math.round(avg) + '嚗?隤踵???圈??? };
    return            { ok: false, label: '?餅扔?芣閫賂?' + Math.round(avg) + '嚗?隤踵???圈??? };
  }
  const sigGrade = _sigGrade(sigAvg);

  // ?亙熒?斗嚗????閮??釭嚗allback ?冽璅??
  let overallStatus, summaryEmoji, summaryTitle, summaryDesc;
  if (!state.bridgeAvailable) {
    overallStatus = 'browser';
    summaryEmoji = '??';
    summaryTitle = '?汗?冽芋撘?;
    summaryDesc = '甇文??賡???APP ?批銵??賣炎皜祈??瘜Ｗ???;
  } else if (sampleCount === 0) {
    overallStatus = 'no_signal';
    summaryEmoji = '??;
    summaryTitle = '?芣?嗅?行郭鞈?';
    summaryDesc = '?航??嚗瘜Ｗ??芷?璈?港??????嚗??????銝剜??蝣箄??行郭??餅???虫漁韏瘀?銝阡??圈?撠?;
  } else if (hasRealSig && sigAvg < 30) {
    overallStatus = 'poor';
    summaryEmoji = '??';
    summaryTitle = '?餅扔?亥孛銝';
    summaryDesc = `撟喳?閮??釭 ${Math.round(sigAvg)}嚗?撌?${sigWorst}嚗遛??00嚗??餅扔??閫訾?蝛拙??????行郭?敺?甈⊥炎皜研;
  } else if (hasRealSig && sigAvg < 70) {
    overallStatus = 'fair';
    summaryEmoji = '?';
    summaryTitle = '閮??釭撠';
    summaryDesc = `撟喳?閮??釭 ${Math.round(sigAvg)}嚗遛??00嚗??交????甇?虜雿輻嚗遣霅啗矽?港蔗?港?蝵桐誑?脣??港蔔閮??;
  } else if (!hasRealSig && sampleRate < 0.3) {
    overallStatus = 'poor';
    summaryEmoji = '??';
    summaryTitle = '閮??釭銝';
    summaryDesc = `60 蝘?嗅 ${sampleCount} 蝑???甇?虜?? 60 蝑???質瘜Ｗ??餅扔?亥孛銝嚗?頝?????;
  } else if (!hasRealSig && sampleRate < 0.7) {
    overallStatus = 'fair';
    summaryEmoji = '?';
    summaryTitle = '閮??釭撠';
    summaryDesc = `?望??${sampleCount} 蝑???${sampleRate.toFixed(1)} 蝑?蝘?嚗雿???遣霅圈??圈??渲瘜Ｗ??;
  } else {
    overallStatus = 'good';
    summaryEmoji = '??;
    summaryTitle = '?行郭???甇?虜';
    summaryDesc = hasRealSig
      ? `撟喳?閮??釭 ${Math.round(sigAvg)}嚗遛??00嚗??嗅 ${sampleCount} 蝑???閮?蝛拙?嚗隞交迤撣賊脰?瑼Ｘ葫雿平?
      : `?望??${sampleCount} 蝑???${sampleRate.toFixed(1)} 蝑?蝘?嚗??帘摰??臭誑甇?虜?脰?瑼Ｘ葫雿平?;
  }

  _setText('ethr-summary-emoji', summaryEmoji);
  _setText('ethr-summary-title', summaryTitle);
  _setText('ethr-summary-desc', summaryDesc);

  // ?寡??航?賊＊???  const card = document.getElementById('ethr-summary-card');
  if (card) {
    const bgMap = { good:'#e8f5e9', fair:'#fff8e1', poor:'#ffebee', no_signal:'#ffebee', browser:'#fff3e0' };
    card.style.background = bgMap[overallStatus] || '#fff';
  }

  // ?? Java 蝡臭葡瘚????祕閮箸鞈?嚗???connected_ticks?isconnected_ticks嚗?  let streamDiag = null;
  if (state.bridgeAvailable) {
    try {
      if (typeof AndroidBridge.getStreamingDiag === 'function') {
        streamDiag = JSON.parse(AndroidBridge.getStreamingDiag() || '{}');
      }
    } catch(e) {}
  }

  // 閰喟敦瑼Ｘ
  const checks = [];
  if (state.bridgeAvailable) {
    let bt = false;
    try { bt = JSON.parse(AndroidBridge.getBleDebugInfo() || '{}').bt_enabled || false; } catch(e) {}
    checks.push({ ok: bt, label:'?? ????', val: bt ? '撌脤??? : '?芷??? });
    // ???????芸??其葡瘚??? connected_ticks 靘?瘀??湔?蝣綽?
    // ??典歇?????賣?⊥?敺??瑟?嚗?隞乩葡瘚???ticks ??祕?瘜?    let conn = false;
    try { conn = AndroidBridge.isBrainwaveConnected(); } catch(e) {}
    if (streamDiag) {
      const wasConnectedDuringCapture = (streamDiag.connected_ticks || 0) > 0;
      checks.push({
        ok: wasConnectedDuringCapture,
        label:'?? ?⊿?????????,
        val: wasConnectedDuringCapture
          ? `?????甇?虜嚗?{streamDiag.connected_ticks} 甈∪??梧?`
          : (streamDiag.disconnected_ticks > 0
              ? `???⊿????瑞?嚗?{streamDiag.disconnected_ticks} 甈⊥蝺??梧?`
              : `???湔挾?賣?嗅 BLE ?嚗????芸???`)
      });
    } else {
      checks.push({ ok: conn, label:'?? ?行郭????', val: conn ? '撌脤??' : '?芷??' });
    }
  } else {
    checks.push({ ok: false, label:'?? ?瑁??啣?', val: '?汗?剁??∟???' });
  }
  checks.push({
    ok: sampleCount > 0,
    label: '? ?交?啁??行郭璅?',
    val: sampleCount === 0 ? '0 蝑??∟???' : sampleCount + ' 蝑?,
  });
  checks.push({
    ok: sampleRate >= 0.7,
    label: '??撟喳??交?餌?',
    val: sampleCount === 0 ? '???∟??? : sampleRate.toFixed(2) + ' 蝑?蝘?璅? ??1.0嚗?,
  });
  checks.push({
    ok: sigGrade.ok,
    label: '?? 閮??釭?斗',
    // ??撱?潑?憿舐內?祕?釭+隤芣?嚗allback ?冽璅???函?
    val: sampleCount === 0 ? '?∟??? :
         hasRealSig ? sigGrade.label + '嚗?撱?0~100嚗?擃?憟踝?' :
         sampleRate >= 0.7 ? '?臬末嚗璅???函?嚗? :
         sampleRate >= 0.3 ? '撠嚗璅???函?嚗? : '銝嚗璅???函?嚗?,
  });
  // ?駁?嚗?撱?擃???祕?駁?嚗?敺＊蝷箝?撱????  checks.push({ ok: null, label:'?? ?駁?霈??, val: '???芣?靘? });
  checks.push({
    ok: byUser ? null : true,
    label: '?梧? ?⊿???',
    val: elapsedSec + ' / ' + state.durationSec + ' 蝘? + (byUser ? '嚗?????' : '嚗???'),
  });

  const checkBox = document.getElementById('ethr-checks');
  if (checkBox) {
    let html = checks.map(c => {

        <span style="color:#555;flex:1;">${icon} ${c.label}</span>

      </div>`;
    }).join('');

    // ? ?祕閮箸?憛?蝯血極蝔葦?敦蝭嚗??????唬??蹂??啗?????
    if (streamDiag || sampleCount === 0) {
      html += '<div style="margin-top:12px;padding:10px;background:#f3e5f5;border-left:4px solid #9c27b0;border-radius:8px;font-size:11px;color:#4a148c;line-height:1.7;">';

      if (streamDiag) {
        html += `繚 Java ?券見?祆嚗?{streamDiag.samples || 0} 蝑?br>`;
        html += `繚 BrainwaveValue callback嚗?{streamDiag.connected_ticks || 0} 甈?br>`;

      } else {
        html += '繚 ?⊥?霈??Java 蝡舐絞閮??? APK嚗???蝝 v1.1.7+嚗?br>';
      }
      html += `繚 JS 蝡舀??onEegSample嚗?{sampleCount} 甈?br>`;
      // ?方?蝯?
      if (streamDiag) {

          html += '<br>?? <b>?方?</b>嚗LE 敺閫貊 callback?虜閬??????冽???◤???脩?鋡怎頂蝯望捏?le ?拐辣?芸?憪???;
        } else if ((streamDiag.disconnected_ticks || 0) > 0 && (streamDiag.connected_ticks || 0) === 0) {
          html += '<br>?? <b>?方?</b>嚗LE ?湔挾?賢??晞????瘜Ｗ??航?琿???ａ??? ThinkGear 鞈???瘝???蝣箄??餅??漁韏瑚蒂?????;
        } else if ((streamDiag.connected_ticks || 0) > 0 && (streamDiag.samples || 0) === 0) {
          html += '<br>?? <b>?方?</b>嚗LE ???甇?虜雿??見?祇瘝??WebView??賣 webView.evaluateJavascript 憭望?嚗?瑼Ｚ? logcat??;
        } else if ((streamDiag.samples || 0) > 0 && sampleCount === 0) {
          html += '<br>?? <b>?方?</b>嚗ava ?璅?雿?JS 蝡舀??嗅?indow.JSBridge.onEegSample ?航鋡怨?撖急???憭?;
        }
      }
      html += '</div>';
    }
    checkBox.innerHTML = html;
  }

  // ? 蝞∠??⊿?舫?選?BrainDNA ?詨潮?霅?  const u2 = _currentUser || {};
  const isStaff2 = u2.role === 'admin' || u2.org_type === '撌乩?鈭箏';
  const dPanel = document.getElementById('ethr-debug-panel');
  if (dPanel) dPanel.style.display = isStaff2 ? '' : 'none';
  if (isStaff2 && window._ethrAccum && sampleCount > 0) {
    const acc2 = window._ethrAccum;
    const avg2 = arr => arr && arr.length ? Math.round(arr.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/arr.length) : 0;
    const isChildSession2 = (_currentReportType || '').startsWith('child_') || _currentReportType === 'child';
    const bdna2 = _calcBrainDNABands(acc2, isChildSession2);
    const lines2 = [
      `璅???  ${sampleCount}嚗?0蝘摨瑟炎?伐?`,
      `瘜冽???  ${avg2(acc2.attn)}  ?暸?摨? ${avg2(acc2.medi)}`,
      `??? BrainDNA 雿?蝞?嚗roportionRange嚗???`,
      `帤  delta:      ${bdna2 ? bdna2.deV : avg2(acc2.delta) + ' (bandTo100 fallback)'}`,
      `庛  theta:      ${bdna2 ? bdna2.thV : avg2(acc2.theta) + ' (bandTo100 fallback)'}`,
      `帢  low_alpha:  ${bdna2 ? bdna2.loA : '??}`,
      `帢  high_alpha: ${bdna2 ? bdna2.hiA : '??}`,
      `帣  low_beta:   ${bdna2 ? bdna2.loB : '??}`,
      `帣  high_beta:  ${bdna2 ? bdna2.hiB : '??}`,
      `帠  low_gamma:  ${bdna2 ? bdna2.loG : '??}`,
      `帠  high_gamma: ${bdna2 ? bdna2.hiG : '??}`,
      bdna2 ? '' : '\n?? r_lalpha 鞈?銝雲嚗瘜?蝞?BrainDNA嚗? APK v1.1.9+嚗?,
    ].filter(l => l !== undefined);
    const dbgEl2 = document.getElementById('ethr-debug-content');
    if (dbgEl2) dbgEl2.textContent = lines2.join('\n');
  }
}

// 靽????詨捆嚗??憒????????航?澆嚗?銝??遙雿蜓瘚???嚗?function _exitEegTestMode() {
