// ?? JS ??蝞?嚗宏璊 Python services/algorithms.py嚗??????????????????????
function _etSafeDiv(a, b, def = 0) { return b > 1e-6 ? a / b : def; }
function _etClamp(v, lo = 0, hi = 100) { return Math.max(lo, Math.min(hi, v)); }
function _etSigmoid(raw) { return 1 / (1 + Math.exp(-3 * (raw - 1))); }

// ??? BrainDNA proportionRange ?梁閮?嚗summarizeEegAccum ??_etComputeAll ?梁嚗??
// 摰撠?敺垢 braindna_algorithms.py ??頛荔???best-30s window ?詨?嚗?const _BDNA_CAP  = { r_delta:98000, r_theta:98000, r_lalpha:50000, r_halpha:50000,
                     r_lbeta:50000, r_hbeta:50000, r_lgamma:10000, r_hgamma:10000 };
const _BDNA_KEYS = ['r_delta','r_theta','r_lalpha','r_halpha','r_lbeta','r_hbeta','r_lgamma','r_hgamma'];
// ?咱撠 CAP ?潘?report_type='child' ?蝙?剁?
// 靘? Springer Nature 2025嚗?50?摨瑕?蝡伐?嚗elta ?? ~44%?heta ~25%
// ?犖 CAP=98K ?典?蝡?delta ??826K 銝?芸 12%嚗??? CAP 靽?瘥??儔
const _BDNA_CAP_CHILD = {
  r_delta:400000, r_theta:230000,
  r_lalpha:50000, r_halpha:50000,
  r_lbeta:50000, r_hbeta:50000,
  r_lgamma:20000, r_hgamma:20000,
};
// ?咱撠 proportionRange ?曉潘?甇?Ⅱ CAP 敺?撖阡?雿????
const _BDNA_PR_CHILD = {
  r_delta:[0.35,0.55], r_theta:[0.08,0.22],
  r_lalpha:[0.015,0.060], r_halpha:[0.015,0.060],
  r_lbeta:[0.010,0.040], r_hbeta:[0.020,0.070],
  r_lgamma:[0.015,0.040], r_hgamma:[0.010,0.030],
};
const _BDNA_PR   = { r_delta:[0.60,0.80], r_theta:[0.15,0.30],
                     r_lalpha:[0.10,0.20], r_halpha:[0.10,0.20],
                     r_lbeta:[0.05,0.10],  r_hbeta:[0.05,0.10],
                     r_lgamma:[0.03,0.06], r_hgamma:[0.03,0.06] };
function _bdnaPR(v,l1,l2){ if(l1>l2||l1<0||v<=0)return 0; if(v>=l2)return 1; if(v<=l1)return v/l1*0.5; return (v-l1)/(l2-l1)*0.5+0.5; }

// Best-30s window selection嚗???BrainDNA MindValueAlgorithm.calcBand嚗?// ??N 蝘??? 30 蝘???蝒???lowGamma 雿??擃?閬?
function _bdnaSelectBestWindow(acc, isChild) {
  const n = (acc.r_lalpha||[]).length;
  const WIN = 30;
  if (n < WIN) return acc;
  // ?咱雿輻頛???lgamma CAP ???? proportionRange ?曉?  const lgCap = isChild ? _BDNA_CAP_CHILD['r_lgamma'] : _BDNA_CAP['r_lgamma'];
  const prL1  = isChild ? 0.015 : 0.03;
  const prL2  = isChild ? 0.040 : 0.06;
  const numW = Math.ceil(n / WIN);
  let bestIdx = 0, bestScore = -1;
  for (let w = 0; w < numW; w++) {
    const s = w * WIN, e = Math.min(s + WIN, n);
    let ps = 0, vs = 0;
    for (let i = s; i < e; i++) {
      // BrainDNA calcColumnSumArray嚗?瘥??芣??憪?      let uncappedTot = 0;
      for (const k of _BDNA_KEYS) uncappedTot += ((acc[k]||[])[i]||0);
      if (uncappedTot <= 0) continue;
      // ????瘀?雿輻撠???lgamma CAP嚗?      const cappedLg = Math.min((acc['r_lgamma']||[])[i]||0, lgCap);
      ps += cappedLg / uncappedTot; vs++;
    }
    if (vs > 0) {
      const score = _bdnaPR(ps/vs, prL1, prL2);
      if (score > bestScore) { bestScore = score; bestIdx = w; }
    }
  }
  // ??雿唾?蝒???
  const s = bestIdx * WIN, e = s + WIN;
  const result = {};
  for (const k of [..._BDNA_KEYS, 'attn', 'medi']) {
    result[k] = ((acc[k]||[]).slice(s, e));
  }
  return result;
}

function _calcBrainDNABands(acc, isChild) {
  const n = (acc.r_lalpha||[]).length;
  if (n < 5) return null;
  // ?咱雿輻頛???CAP ?潘?霈?delta/theta 瘥???瘞游像嚗pringer 2025嚗?  const capTable = isChild ? _BDNA_CAP_CHILD : _BDNA_CAP;
  // 甇仿??塚???best 30-second window嚗gamma ?曉潮 CAP 隤踵嚗?  const win = _bdnaSelectBestWindow(acc, isChild);
  const wn = (win.r_lalpha||[]).length;
  if (wn < 5) return null;
  // 靽∟??釭銝?嚗elta < 30000 隞?”?餅扔?亥孛銝嚗?蝢文???198K嚗?0K??5%嚗?  // ?甇斤?隞仿甇?beta/gamma ??瘥?撠?擃???蝡舫?頛臭??湛?
  const MIN_DELTA_QUALITY = 30000;
  const ps = {}; for(const k of _BDNA_KEYS) ps[k]=0;
  let vs = 0;
  for(let i=0;i<wn;i++){
    // BrainDNA calcColumnSumArray嚗?瘥??芣??憪潘???蝡臭??湛?
    let uncappedTot = 0;
    for(const k of _BDNA_KEYS) uncappedTot += ((win[k]||[])[i]||0);
    if(uncappedTot<=0) continue;
    // ?餅扔?釭?蕪嚗elta 璆萎?隞?”?亥孛銝
    if(((win['r_delta']||[])[i]||0) < MIN_DELTA_QUALITY) continue;
    // ????瘀?雿輻撠???CAP 銵剁?
    for(const k of _BDNA_KEYS){
      const capped = Math.min((win[k]||[])[i]||0, capTable[k]);
      ps[k] += capped / uncappedTot;
    }
    vs++;
  }
  if(vs===0) return null;
  const prTable = isChild ? _BDNA_PR_CHILD : _BDNA_PR;
  const p = k => Math.round(_bdnaPR(ps[k]/vs, ...prTable[k]) * 100);
  return { deV:p('r_delta'), thV:p('r_theta'), loA:p('r_lalpha'), hiA:p('r_halpha'),
           loB:p('r_lbeta'),  hiB:p('r_hbeta'),  loG:p('r_lgamma'), hiG:p('r_hgamma') };
}

function _etComputeAll(acc, isChild) {
  // acc = { attn:[], medi:[], delta:[], theta:[], alpha:[], beta:[], gamma:[], r_lalpha:[], ... }
  const avg = arr => arr.length ? arr.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/arr.length : 0;
  // ?芸???BrainDNA proportionRange嚗?蝡乩蝙??CHILD_PROP_RANGE ?曉潘?
  const bdna = _calcBrainDNABands(acc, !!isChild);
  const d  = bdna ? bdna.deV : avg(acc.delta);
  const t  = bdna ? bdna.thV : avg(acc.theta);
  const la = bdna ? bdna.loA : avg(acc.alpha) * 0.5;
  const ha = bdna ? bdna.hiA : avg(acc.alpha) * 0.5;
  const lb = bdna ? bdna.loB : avg(acc.beta)  * 0.5;
  const hb = bdna ? bdna.hiB : avg(acc.beta)  * 0.5;
  const lg = bdna ? bdna.loG : avg(acc.gamma) * 0.5;
  const hg = bdna ? bdna.hiG : avg(acc.gamma) * 0.5;
  const a  = la + ha;
  const b  = lb + hb;
  const g  = lg + hg;
  const att= avg(acc.attn);
  const med= avg(acc.medi);
  const total = d + t + a + b + g || 1;

  const pct = (raw, isRatioStat) => {
    if (isRatioStat) return _etClamp(_etSigmoid(raw) * 100, 0, 100);
    return _etClamp(raw, 0, 100);
  };

  return {
    // 瘙箇???
    // 瘙箇???靽格迤??嚗?靽?ha) + ?拙漲?(hb?0.4) 繩 ?嗉惚庛?0.8 + ?脤?帤?0.2
    // ?撘?hb/(lb+a) 隞仿?憯脰﹛?捱?瘀?憯?憭把?瘙箇?憟?    DEC: { label:'瘙箇???,    cat:'leadership', pct: pct(_etSafeDiv(ha + hb*0.4, t*0.8 + d*0.2), true) },
    // ????靽格迤??嚗?靽∴?high_alpha嚗? 蝑?雁嚗heta嚗? 蝷曆漱瘣?嚗igh_gamma嚗?    //   繩 ?行?瑁?憯?嚗igh_beta ? 1.2 + low_beta ? 0.8嚗?    LDR: { label:'????,    cat:'leadership', pct: pct(_etSafeDiv(ha + t*0.7 + hg*0.3, hb*1.2 + lb*0.8), true) },
    ACT: { label:'蝛扔銝餃?',  cat:'leadership', pct: pct(_etSafeDiv(b+g, d+t), true) },
    VIS: { label:'憿?雁',  cat:'leadership', pct: pct(_etSafeDiv(t, b),     true) },
    // ?菟?嚗耨甇??嚗?庛??瘣??0.6 + low_alpha?暸?閬箏??0.4 繩 high_beta?行?0.8 + delta?0.2
    CRE: { label:'?菟?',    cat:'leadership', pct: pct(_etSafeDiv(t*0.6 + la*0.4, hb*0.8 + d*0.2), true) },
    RES: { label:'????,  cat:'leadership', pct: pct(_etSafeDiv(a, b+d+t), true) },
    // 擃漲?嚗耨甇??嚗?隞?hb 雿?1/8 蝮賡???50% ?箸?嚗igmoid 甇?????? hb/total 瘞賊???嚗?    FOC: { label:'擃漲?',  cat:'leadership', pct: pct(_etSafeDiv(hb*8, total), true) },
    // 隤
    ATT: { label:'撠釣??,    cat:'cognitive',  pct: _etClamp(att, 0, 100) },
    MED: { label:'?暸??',  cat:'cognitive',  pct: _etClamp(med, 0, 100) },
    EBI: { label:'?瑁??血?',  cat:'cognitive',  pct: pct(_etSafeDiv(b, a+t), true) },
    CCR: { label:'隤皜',  cat:'cognitive',  pct: pct(_etSafeDiv(a, b+t), true) },
    // ??
    EI:  { label:'??蝛拙?',  cat:'emotional',  pct: pct(_etSafeDiv(a, b+d), true) },
    EQ:  { label:'???',  cat:'emotional',  pct: pct(_etSafeDiv(a+t, b), true) },
    // 隞乩?瘥???璅絞銝靽格迤嚗igmoid(x?8繩total)嚗誑 1/8 蝮賡???50% 銝剝?嚗圾瘙箄??偶??雿?憿?    INN: { label:'?批摰?',  cat:'emotional',  pct: pct(_etSafeDiv(la*8, total), true) },
    VIT: { label:'?瘣餃?',  cat:'emotional',  pct: pct(_etSafeDiv(ha*8, total), true) },
    INS: { label:'?渲死??',  cat:'emotional',  pct: pct(_etSafeDiv(t*8,  total), true) },
    EMO: { label:'????',  cat:'emotional',  pct: pct(_etSafeDiv(lg*8, total), true) },
    // 鈭粹?
    SOC: { label:'蝷曆漱??',  cat:'social',     pct: pct(_etSafeDiv(g, a+b), true) },
    // EMP ?怠?餅挾嚗? 4嚗?8繩2嚗蝙?畾萄像??隞 50%
    EMP: { label:'???梢陷',  cat:'social',     pct: pct(_etSafeDiv((t+lg)*4, total), true) },
    // 皞”??靽格迤??嚗??湔?(ha)?0.6 + 蝷曆漱?(hg)?0.4 繩 ?行帣
    COM: { label:'皞”??,  cat:'social',     pct: pct(_etSafeDiv(ha*0.6 + hg*0.4, hb*0.7 + lb*0.3), true) },
    OBS: { label:'?啣?閫撖?,  cat:'social',     pct: pct(_etSafeDiv(hg, total)*100, false) },
    _bands: { d, t, la, ha, lb, hb, lg, hg, a, b, g, att, med },
  };
}

// ??? ?蝯梯?撌亙 ?????????????????????????????????????????????????
function _etAvg(arr) { return arr && arr.length ? arr.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/arr.length : 0; }
// _etGeoMean 撌脣??剁??寧 _etAvg嚗?銵像??嚗? BrainDNA ??瞍?瘜???function _etStd(arr) {
  if (!arr || arr.length < 2) return 0;
  const m = _etAvg(arr);
  const v = arr.reduce((s,x)=> s + (x-m)*(x-m), 0) / arr.length;
  return Math.sqrt(v);
}
function _etTrend(arr) {
  if (!arr || arr.length < 4) return 0;
  const half = Math.floor(arr.length / 2);
  return _etAvg(arr.slice(half)) - _etAvg(arr.slice(0, half));
}
function _etSafeRatio(num, den) {
  if (!(den > 1e-6) || !(num > 1e-6)) return 1;
  return num / den;
}
function _etLogSig(ratio, slope) {
  const l = Math.log10(ratio);
  const x = 1 / (1 + Math.exp(-(slope || 2) * l));
  return _etClamp(x * 100, 0, 100);
}
// ?⊥???sigmoid嚗誑??蝢文??mid? 50 ???嚗cale ?批??摨艾?// ?誨?? _etLogSig嚗摰誑瘥?1 ?箔葉敹?撠??犖?賢?銝??????嚗?function _etCenterSig(feature, mid, scale) {
  const s = (scale && scale > 1e-6) ? scale : 0.1;
  const x = 1 / (1 + Math.exp(-((feature - mid) / s)));
  return _etClamp(x * 100, 0, 100);
}

// ?? ?黎?曉?雿極?瘀???蝡?app/services/algorithms.py ?梁??蝯虜?賂???
// 璅?撣豢? CDF嚗elen & Severo 餈撮嚗炊撌?< 1e-7嚗?function _etNormCdf(z) {
  const t = 1 / (1 + 0.2316419 * Math.abs(z));
  const d = 0.3989422804014327 * Math.exp(-z * z / 2);
  const p = d * t * (0.31938153 + t * (-0.356563782 + t * (1.781477937 + t * (-1.821255978 + t * 1.330274429))));
  return z >= 0 ? 1 - p : p;
}
// 銝?鈭箄瘜Ｘ見?祉??og10 敺ean/std嚗?皞?BrainDNA evaluationReport.py ??data_stats.py嚗?const _ET_DATA_STATS = {
  delta:     { mean: 5.29835995039, std: 0.542447565538 },
  theta:     { mean: 4.72538897932, std: 0.441840459259 },
  lowAlpha:  { mean: 4.07285436418, std: 0.424115475935 },
  highAlpha: { mean: 4.09023791618, std: 0.376780766056 },
  lowBeta:   { mean: 4.02628991082, std: 0.404178025673 },
  highBeta:  { mean: 4.08907476245, std: 0.392740498864 },
  lowGamma:  { mean: 3.7574299841,  std: 0.426077247618 },
  midGamma:  { mean: 3.75332489124, std: 0.628883941715 },
};
function _etMakeMbtiResult(ei, ns, tf, jp) {
  const dims = [
    { pair:'E/I', score:ei, dominant: ei>=50?'E':'I', pct:ei },
    { pair:'N/S', score:ns, dominant: ns>=50?'N':'S', pct:ns },
    { pair:'T/F', score:tf, dominant: tf>=50?'T':'F', pct:tf },
    { pair:'J/P', score:jp, dominant: jp>=50?'J':'P', pct:jp },
  ];
  return { type: dims.map(d => d.dominant).join(''), dims };
}

// ?? ?迤??BrainDNA ?怠?BTI 瞍?瘜?????????????????????????????????????????
// 蝘餅???BrainDNA/braindna/algorithms/bagua.py (Bagua.calcBagua) +
//          BrainDNA/braindna/algorithms/MBTIPersonality.py (getPersonalityFromBagua)
//
// 頛詨嚗?憪?ThinkGear ?餅挾?潘?r_lalpha?_theta 蝑??祉?桐?嚗?// 瘚?嚗owAlpha ?黎?曉?雿???銝嚗嗾??撌賢??桀嚗? theta ?曉?雿??拙? ??MBTI 16 ??// useLi=true ? 8 ?衣頂蝯梧??怒?嚗?
//   ??laPct 0.250~0.375 撣塚???thPct > 0.5 ???Ｗ嚗NFJ/INFP嚗??血?隞?嚗NFJ/ENFP嚗?//   甇斗????INFJ/INFP ?拙?嚗????扳閬???function _etBaguaMBTI(rawLA, rawTH, useLi = false) {
  const LA_MEAN = _ET_DATA_STATS.lowAlpha.mean;
  const LA_STD  = _ET_DATA_STATS.lowAlpha.std;

  // 1. lowAlpha ?曉?雿???7 ??????BrainDNA ??瞍?瘜??湛?
  const laPct = (rawLA > 0) ? _etNormCdf((Math.log10(rawLA) - LA_MEAN) / LA_STD) : 0.4;
  const BOUNDS = [0, 0.125, 0.250, 0.375, 0.500, 0.625, 0.750, 1.0];
  let bagua = 6;
  for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) { if (laPct < BOUNDS[i+1]) { bagua = i; break; } }

  // 2. theta ?曉?雿?  const thPct = (rawTH > 0) ? _etNormCdf((Math.log10(rawTH) - LA_MEAN) / LA_STD) : 0.4;
  const high  = thPct > 0.5;

  // 3. 8 ?佗??恍?佗?嚗one 2嚗?.250~0.375嚗? theta 擃????Ｗ?? INFJ/INFP
  //    ?血?蝬剜? 7 ?行?撠?? ??ENFJ/ENFP嚗?  const liActive = useLi && bagua === 2 && high;
  const MAP = [
    ['INTJ','INTP'],                                  // 0: 銋?0.000~0.125
    ['ENTJ','ENTP'],                                  // 1: ??0.125~0.250
    liActive ? ['INFJ','INFP'] : ['ENFJ','ENFP'],     // 2: ??8??/??7?? 0.250~0.375
    ['ISTJ','ISFJ'],                                  // 3: 撌?0.375~0.500
    ['ESTJ','ESFJ'],                                  // 4: ??0.500~0.625
    ['ISTP','ISFP'],                                  // 5: ??0.625~0.750
    ['ESTP','ESFP'],                                  // 6: ??0.750~1.000
  ];
  const primaryType = MAP[bagua][high ? 0 : 1];

  // 4. 靽∪??嚗?-100嚗?  const zoneStart = BOUNDS[bagua];
  const zoneEnd   = BOUNDS[bagua + 1];
  const zoneHalf  = (zoneEnd - zoneStart) / 2;
  const distToBound = Math.min(laPct - zoneStart, zoneEnd - laPct);
  const laConf = Math.min(1, distToBound / zoneHalf);
  const thConf = Math.min(1, Math.abs(thPct - 0.5) / 0.3);
  const confidence = Math.round((laConf * 0.65 + thConf * 0.35) * 100);

  // 5. ??甈∪?嚗aPct ???????嚗?蝞?啣雿? MBTI 雿甈∟??航??  let secondary = null;
  if (laConf < 0.6) {
    const closerToLower = (laPct - zoneStart) < (zoneEnd - laPct);
    const adjBagua = closerToLower ? bagua - 1 : bagua + 1;
    if (adjBagua >= 0 && adjBagua <= 6) {
      // ?賊?虫?銋?憟 liActive ?斗嚗???zone 2嚗?      const adjLiActive = useLi && adjBagua === 2 && high;
      const adjMap = adjLiActive ? ['INFJ','INFP'] : MAP[adjBagua];
      const adj = adjMap[high ? 0 : 1];
      if (adj !== primaryType) secondary = adj;
    }
  }
  if (!secondary && thConf < 0.4) {
    const alt = MAP[bagua][high ? 1 : 0];
    if (alt !== primaryType) secondary = alt;
  }

  return { type: primaryType, laPct, thPct, bagua, confidence, secondary };
}

// 敺?laPct / thPct ?典?憿舐內?典??賂??孵???type 銝?湛?撟漲??靽∟?撘瑕漲嚗?// ??蝡?compute_mbti 憿舐內?摩摰撠?
function _etBaguaScores(type, laPct, thPct) {
  // E/I嚗挾?找?蝵殷?7 畾蛛?瘥挾 0.125 撖穿?鈭斗 I/E嚗?畾萄偏頞? ??靽∟?頞撥嚗?  const seg    = Math.min(Math.floor(laPct / 0.125), 6);
  const segPos = (laPct - seg * 0.125) / 0.125;
  const eiStr  = _etClamp(segPos * 45 + 5, 5, 50);
  const eiPct  = (type[0] === 'E') ? 50 + eiStr : 50 - eiStr;
  // N/S嚗aPct < 0.375 = N ?蝯?頝?0.375 ??頞? ??靽∟?頞撥
  const nsDist = (laPct < 0.375) ? (0.375 - laPct) / 0.375 : (laPct - 0.375) / 0.625;
  const nsBase = _etClamp(50 + nsDist * 45, 50, 95);
  const nsPct  = (type[1] === 'N') ? nsBase : 100 - nsBase;
  // T/F & J/P嚗heta ?曉?雿撥摨佗?? 0.5 ??靽∟?頞撥嚗?  const tfStr  = _etClamp(Math.abs(thPct - 0.5) * 80 + 10, 10, 45);
  const tfPct  = (type[2] === 'T') ? 50 + tfStr : 50 - tfStr;
  const jpStr  = _etClamp(Math.abs(thPct - 0.5) * 70 + 10, 10, 45);
  const jpPct  = (type[3] === 'J') ? 50 + jpStr : 50 - jpStr;
  return _etMakeMbtiResult(eiPct, nsPct, tfPct, jpPct);
}

// ??? 4 撅?MBTI ?刻?嚗????挾 + 8 ?衣頂蝯梧??????????????????????????????
// Direction C嚗? 3 ?????行郭靘??? 3 畾蛛??挾蝞??潸??函? MBTI
//   ?冽挾撟喳?  ??archetype嚗擃敹?
//   ?挾 0~1/3 ??social  嚗?憪郎閬箸?嚗??亥??祉/蝷暹??嚗?//   銝剜挾 1/3~2/3 ??peer  嚗?擛?嚗??亥???/???ｇ?
//   敺挾 2/3~1   ??family嚗楛摨衣帘摰?嚗??亥?摰嗅滬/?祆??ｇ?
// Direction B嚗?畾萄?? 8 ?衣頂蝯梧?useLi=true嚗?撘?Ｗ INFJ/INFP
// ??? BrainDNA ?? Personality.calcPersonality()嚗?敺垢摰撠?嚗????????????
// 頛詨嚗agua(0-6), color('orange'/'green'/'blue'/'yellow'), rawBeta, rawTh嚗?憪潘?
// 頛詨嚗BTI 摮葡
function _etPersonalityFromBaguaColor(bagua, color, rawBeta, rawTh) {
  if (bagua === 0) return color === 'green' ? 'ENTJ' : 'ESTJ';      // 銋?  if (bagua === 1) return rawBeta > rawTh    ? 'ISFJ' : 'ISTJ';     // ??  if (bagua === 2) return color === 'blue'   ? 'INFJ' : 'INTJ';     // ??li_active=false嚗?  if (bagua === 3) return rawBeta > rawTh    ? 'ESFP' : 'ESTP';     // 撌?  if (bagua === 4) return color === 'green'  ? 'INTP' : 'ISTP';     // ??  if (bagua === 5) return color === 'blue'   ? 'ENFP' : 'ENTP';     // ??  return color === 'blue'   ? 'INFP' : 'ISFP';                      // ??6)
}

// ??? ?西閮?嚗rainDNA MindColorAlgorithm.calcForWeights 摰????????????
// factorFirstRange ???脩? f=gamma/alpha 蝭?嚗?銋?100嚗?const _ET_COLOR_F1_RANGE = {
  orange: [0.25, 0.40],
  green:  [0.20, 0.45],
  blue:   [0.30, 0.55],
  yellow: [0.15, 0.35],
};
const _ET_COLOR_ORDER = ['orange', 'green', 'blue', 'yellow'];
const _ET_PRIORITY_ORDER = 213;   // BrainDNA PRIORITY_ORDER
const _ET_PRIORITY_THAN  = 0xD5F1; // BrainDNA PRIORITY_THAN

function _etFactorFirstRange(color, factor) {
  const r = _ET_COLOR_F1_RANGE[color];
  if (!r) return false;
  return r[0] < factor && factor < r[1];
}

function _etThanToBool(color1, color2) {
  // BrainDNA _than_to_bool嚗??喳??粹???  const i1 = _ET_COLOR_ORDER.indexOf(color1);
  const i2 = _ET_COLOR_ORDER.indexOf(color2);
  if (i1 < 0 || i2 < 0) return color1;
  const mask = 1 << i2;
  const bits = (_ET_PRIORITY_THAN >> (4 * i1)) & 0xF;
  return (bits & mask) > 0 ? color1 : color2;
}

function _etColorForMeans(la, ha, lb, hb, lg, hg) {
  // BrainDNA MindColorAlgorithm.calcForWeights嚗???brainwave.py line 336嚗?  const alpha = la + ha, beta = lb + hb, gamma = lg + hg;
  if (alpha <= 0 || beta <= 0) return 'orange';
  const f1 = gamma / alpha;  // BrainDNA ?湔?冽?靘?銝? 100
  const f2 = gamma / beta;

  const meets = { orange: 0, green: 0, blue: 0, yellow: 0 };
  const meets2 = [];

  // 蝚砌?頛迎?f1 ?函?? ??meets +1
  for (const color of _ET_COLOR_ORDER) {
    if (_etFactorFirstRange(color, f1)) {
      meets[color]++;
      meets2.push(color);
    }
  }
  if (meets2.length === 1) return meets2[0];

  // 蝚砌?頛迎?f2 銝蝭?????meets -1嚗rainDNA 撠?f2 銋 factorFirstRange嚗?  for (const color of _ET_COLOR_ORDER) {
    if (!_etFactorFirstRange(color, f2)) meets[color]--;
  }

  // 靘??摨?PRIORITY_ORDER=213嚗活雿?1蝬?擐?=2????meets>0 ?洵銝??  let w = _ET_PRIORITY_ORDER;
  while (w > 0) {
    w = Math.floor(w / 10);
    const idx = w % 10;
    if (idx < 4 && meets[_ET_COLOR_ORDER[idx]] > 0) return _ET_COLOR_ORDER[idx];
  }
  return 'orange';
}

// ??? MBTI ?頝嚗amming distance嚗?-4嚗????????????????????????????????????
function _etMbtiContradiction(a, b) {
  if (!a || !b || a.length < 4 || b.length < 4) return 0;
  let d = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) d += (a[i] !== b[i] ? 1 : 0);
  return d;
}

// ??? 30 蝘?蝒?MBTI 璅?嚗rainDNA calcBand 蝎曄?嚗????????????????????????????
// ??蝡?compute_window_mbtis 摰撠?
function _etComputeWindowMbtis(acc) {
  const WINDOW = 30;
  const LA_MEAN = _ET_DATA_STATS.lowAlpha.mean;
  const LA_STD  = _ET_DATA_STATS.lowAlpha.std;
  const BOUNDS  = [0, 0.125, 0.250, 0.375, 0.500, 0.625, 0.750, 1.0];
  const _arr = k => (acc[k] || []).map(Number);
  const _segMean = (a, s, e) => {
    const seg = a.slice(s, Math.min(e, a.length)).filter(v => v > 0);
    return seg.length ? seg.reduce((x,y)=>x+y,0)/seg.length : 0;
  };

  const r_la = _arr('r_lalpha'), r_th = _arr('r_theta');
  const r_ha = _arr('r_halpha'), r_hb = _arr('r_hbeta');
  const r_lb = _arr('r_lbeta'),  r_lg = _arr('r_lgamma'), r_hg = _arr('r_hgamma');

  const n = r_la.length;
  if (n < WINDOW || !r_th.length) return [];

  const results = [];
  // BrainDNA evaluationReport.py 蝚?36 銵???敺?頞?30 蝘??典?閬?
  const numWindows = Math.ceil(n / WINDOW);
  for (let wi = 0; wi < numWindows; wi++) {
    const s = wi * WINDOW, e = (wi + 1) * WINDOW;
    const rawLA = _segMean(r_la, s, e), rawTH = _segMean(r_th, s, e);
    if (rawLA <= 0 || rawTH <= 0) continue;

    const laPct = _etNormCdf((Math.log10(rawLA) - LA_MEAN) / LA_STD);
    let bagua = 6;
    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) { if (laPct < BOUNDS[i+1]) { bagua = i; break; } }

    const rawHA = _segMean(r_ha, s, e), rawHB = _segMean(r_hb, s, e);
    const rawLB = _segMean(r_lb, s, e), rawLG = _segMean(r_lg, s, e);
    const rawHG = _segMean(r_hg, s, e);
    const color = _etColorForMeans(rawLA, rawHA, rawLB, rawHB, rawLG, rawHG);
    const rawBeta = rawHB + rawLB;
    const mbtiType = _etPersonalityFromBaguaColor(bagua, color, rawBeta, rawTH);

    results.push({ type: mbtiType, windowIdx: wi, laMean: rawLA, thMean: rawTH, color, baguaInt: bagua });
  }
  return results;
}

// ??? ?頝???惜嚗?敺垢 assign_personality_layers 撠?嚗?????????????????
function _etAssignPersonalityLayers(archetypeType, windowMbtis) {
  if (!windowMbtis || !windowMbtis.length) return {};
  const scores = windowMbtis.map(w => ({
    type: w.type, dist: _etMbtiContradiction(archetypeType, w.type), idx: w.windowIdx
  }));
  const peer   = scores.reduce((a,b) => (b.dist < a.dist || (b.dist===a.dist && b.idx<a.idx)) ? b : a);
  const family = scores.reduce((a,b) => (b.dist > a.dist || (b.dist===a.dist && b.idx>a.idx)) ? b : a);
  const mid    = (peer.dist + family.dist) / 2;
  const remain = scores.filter(s => s.idx !== peer.idx && s.idx !== family.idx);
  const social = remain.length
    ? remain.reduce((a,b) => Math.abs(b.dist-mid) < Math.abs(a.dist-mid) ? b : a)
    : { type: archetypeType, dist: 0 };

  const _mk = type => ({ type, secondary: null, confidence: 70 });
  return {
    peer:   _mk(peer.type),
    family: _mk(family.type),
    social: _mk(social.type),
    _contradiction: { peer: peer.dist, family: family.dist, social: Math.abs(social.dist - mid) },
  };
}

// ??? ?惜?扳?啣?銝餃撘?BrainDNA 30s閬? + ?頝??嚗??????????????????
function _etComputeMBTILayers(acc) {
  // Archetype嚗畾?lowAlpha + theta ?????怠 ??MBTI
  const r_la = (acc.r_lalpha || []).filter(v => v > 0);
  const r_th = (acc.r_theta  || []).filter(v => v > 0);

  if (r_la.length < 4 || r_th.length < 4) {
    const act  = _etClamp(_etAvg(acc.attn || []) / 100, 0, 1);
    const calm = _etClamp(_etAvg(acc.medi || []) / 100, 0, 1);
    const fb = _etMakeMbtiResult(
      _etClamp(50 + (act  - 0.5) * 60, 5, 95),
      _etClamp(50 + (calm - 0.5) * 50, 5, 95),
      _etClamp(50 + (act  - 0.5) * 40, 5, 95),
      _etClamp(50 + (calm - 0.5) * 40, 5, 95),
    );
    return { archetype: fb, family: fb, social: fb, peer: fb, _source: 'fallback' };
  }

  const rawLA = r_la.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0) / r_la.length;
  const rawTH = r_th.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0) / r_th.length;
  const archetypeBr  = _etBaguaMBTI(rawLA, rawTH, true);
  const archetypeScores = _etBaguaScores(archetypeBr.type, archetypeBr.laPct, archetypeBr.thPct);
  const archetype = { ...archetypeScores, confidence: archetypeBr.confidence, secondary: archetypeBr.secondary };
  const archetypeType = archetype.type;

  // 30 蝘?蝒?MBTI 璅?嚗rainDNA ??蝞?嚗??+ ?西 ???嚗?  const windowMbtis = _etComputeWindowMbtis(acc);

  // ?頝??銝惜嚗eer / social / family嚗?  const layerAssign = _etAssignPersonalityLayers(archetypeType, windowMbtis);

  const _mkLayer = (src) => {
    if (!src || !src.type) return archetype;
    const br = _etBaguaMBTI(rawLA, rawTH, true);  // ??銝 laPct/thPct 雿?scores
    const s  = _etBaguaScores(src.type, archetypeBr.laPct, archetypeBr.thPct);
    return { ...s, type: src.type, confidence: src.confidence || 70, secondary: src.secondary };
  };

  return {
    archetype,
    peer:   _mkLayer(layerAssign.peer),
    social: _mkLayer(layerAssign.social),
    family: _mkLayer(layerAssign.family),
    _contradiction: layerAssign._contradiction || {},
    _source: windowMbtis.length > 0 ? 'window_contradiction' : 'fallback',
  };
}

// ???詨捆 wrapper嚗??蜓?刻?????撅斗活嚗?function _etComputeMBTI(acc) {
  const layers = _etComputeMBTILayers(acc);
  return layers.archetype;
}

// 4 撅斗扳??鞈?

function _etCalcBrainColor(acc) {
  // BrainDNA ReportResult.calcColor嚗? 閬??巨嚗?敺?頞?30 蝘??典?閬?嚗?  // 瘥?蝒 calcForWeights嚗reen/blue/yellow ? ?? 蟡剁?orange ?敺飛??  const WINDOW = 30;
  const _avg = arr => arr && arr.length ? arr.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/arr.length : 0;
  const _seg = (arr, s, e) => { const a = (arr||[]).slice(s, Math.min(e,arr.length)); return a.length ? a.reduce((x,y)=>x+y,0)/a.length : 0; };

  const hasRaw = (acc.r_lalpha && acc.r_lalpha.length > 0);
  const n = hasRaw ? acc.r_lalpha.length : 0;

  if (!hasRaw || n < WINDOW) {
    // Fallback嚗???頞單??典畾萄像?甈∟?蝞?    if (hasRaw) {
      return _etColorForMeans(_avg(acc.r_lalpha), _avg(acc.r_halpha),
                               _avg(acc.r_lbeta),  _avg(acc.r_hbeta),
                               _avg(acc.r_lgamma), _avg(acc.r_hgamma));
    }
    const alpha = _avg(acc.alpha), beta = _avg(acc.beta), gamma = _avg(acc.gamma);
    if (alpha <= 0 || beta <= 0) return 'orange';
    return _etColorForMeans(alpha/2, alpha/2, beta/2, beta/2, gamma/2, gamma/2);
  }

  // ?憭?6 ??蝒?BrainDNA mindArray 銝?嚗??急?敺?頞?30 蝘??蝒?  const numWindows = Math.min(Math.ceil(n / WINDOW), 6);
  const count = [0, 0, 0, 0];  // orange, green, blue, yellow
  const colorList = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < numWindows; i++) {
    const s = i * WINDOW, e = (i + 1) * WINDOW;
    const color = _etColorForMeans(
      _seg(acc.r_lalpha, s, e), _seg(acc.r_halpha, s, e),
      _seg(acc.r_lbeta,  s, e), _seg(acc.r_hbeta,  s, e),
      _seg(acc.r_lgamma, s, e), _seg(acc.r_hgamma, s, e),
    );
    const idx = _ET_COLOR_ORDER.indexOf(color);
    if (idx >= 0) count[idx]++;
    colorList.push(color);
  }

  // 憟 threshold嚗reen/blue/yellow ? ?? 蟡剁?
  const threshold = [0, 2, 2, 2];
  for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) { if (count[i] < threshold[i]) count[i] = 0; }

  // ??green/blue/yellow ?賣 0 ???蝚砌???蝒???  if (count[1] === 0 && count[2] === 0 && count[3] === 0) {
    return colorList[0] || 'orange';
  }

  // Orange 甇賊嚗脰?瘙粹
  count[0] = 0;
  const remaining = _ET_COLOR_ORDER.filter((c,i) => count[i] > 0);

  if (remaining.length === 0) return 'orange';
  if (remaining.length === 1) return remaining[0];
  if (remaining.length === 2) return _etThanToBool(remaining[0], remaining[1]);

  // 3+ 憿嚗? PRIORITY_ORDER
  let w = _ET_PRIORITY_ORDER;
  while (w > 0) {
    w = Math.floor(w / 10);
    const idx = w % 10;
    if (idx < 4 && count[idx] > 0) return _ET_COLOR_ORDER[idx];
  }
  return 'orange';
}

    desc:'銵??撥?駁????餃????望?', traits:['蝛扔銝餃?','??鞊?','蝷曆漱瘣餉?','?渲死?'] },

// ??? 3 ??瑼Ｘ葫蝯???憭扳?? UI嚗??亙熒瑼Ｘ???函??頛荔??????????

const _ETHR_MBTI_LAYERS = {
  archetype: { label:'???扳',   icon:'?', desc:'?函??行郭?潭隢??湧??詨??扳嚗??亥?憭拍??祈釭' },

  social:    { label:'蝷暹???,     icon:'?', desc:'?⊿??挾嚗?憪郎閬箸?嚗??行郭嚗????蝷暹??鞎? },
  peer:      { label:'????',   icon:'?', desc:'?⊿?銝剜挾嚗?擛?嚗??行郭嚗??????????祕璅??' },

let _r2MbtiLayerResults  = null;
let _r2MbtiCurrentType   = '';
let _r2MbtiActiveLayer   = 'archetype';

function _renderR2MbtiList() {
  const listEl = document.getElementById('r2-mbti-list');
  if (!listEl) return;
  const layer = _r2MbtiActiveLayer;
  const layerMeta = _ETHR_MBTI_LAYERS[layer] || { label: layer, icon:'??, desc:'' };
  const layerResult = (_r2MbtiLayerResults && _r2MbtiLayerResults[layer]) || null;
  const id = (layerResult && layerResult.type) || _r2MbtiCurrentType;
  const m = id ? _ETHR_MBTI_TABLE[id] : null;
  if (!id || !m) {
    listEl.innerHTML = '<div style="color:#aaa;font-size:12px;text-align:center;padding:14px;">撠?刻??箸扳憿?</div>';
    return;
  }
  let dimsHtml = '';
  if (layerResult && layerResult.dims) {
    dimsHtml = '<div style="font-size:11px;color:#ccc;margin:8px 0 6px;line-height:1.7;">'
      + layerResult.dims.map(d =>
          `<b>${d.pair}</b>嚗?span style="color:${d.dominant === d.pair[0] ? '#ef9a9a' : '#90caf9'};font-weight:700;">${d.dominant}</span> (${Math.round(d.pct)}%)`
        ).join(' 繚 ')
      + '</div>';
  }
  listEl.innerHTML = `<div class="ethr-mbti-card is-current" style="background:rgba(255,255,255,0.08);border-radius:10px;padding:12px 14px;color:white;">
    <div style="font-size:11px;color:#90caf9;font-weight:700;margin-bottom:6px;">${layerMeta.icon} ${layerMeta.label}?<span style="color:#aaa;font-weight:400;">${layerMeta.desc}</span></div>
    <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;">
      <span style="font-size:16px;font-weight:800;color:#fff;">${m.emoji} ${id}</span>
      <span style="font-size:12px;color:#90caf9;">${m.name}</span>
    </div>
    ${dimsHtml}
    <div style="font-size:11px;color:#ccc;line-height:1.7;margin-top:6px;">${m[layer] || '??}</div>
  </div>`;
}

function _bindR2MbtiTabs() {
  const tabs = document.querySelectorAll('#r2-mbti-layer-tabs .ethr-layer-tab');
  if (!tabs.length) return;
  tabs.forEach(btn => {
    btn.onclick = () => {
      tabs.forEach(b => { b.style.background = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.15)'; b.classList.remove('active'); });
      btn.style.background = '#1a237e';
      btn.classList.add('active');
      _r2MbtiActiveLayer = btn.dataset.layer;
      _renderR2MbtiList();
    };
  });
}

function _renderR2MbtiAnalysis(acc) {
  const section = document.getElementById('r2-mbti-section');
  if (!section || !acc) return;

  let layers;
  try { layers = _etComputeMBTILayers(acc); } catch(e) { layers = null; }
  if (!layers || !layers.archetype || !layers.archetype.type) return;

  _r2MbtiLayerResults = layers;
  _r2MbtiCurrentType  = layers.archetype.type;
  _r2MbtiActiveLayer  = 'archetype';

  // ???惜 MBTI ?刻?蝯?
  const layerOrder = ['archetype','family','social','peer'];
  const typeScore  = {};
  const typeLayerMap = {};
  layerOrder.forEach(k => {
    const lay = layers[k];
    if (!lay || !lay.type) return;
    const conf = (lay.confidence != null) ? lay.confidence : 70;
    const pP = 50 + conf / 2;
    const pS = 100 - pP;
    typeScore[lay.type] = (typeScore[lay.type] || 0) + pP / 4;
    if (!typeLayerMap[lay.type]) typeLayerMap[lay.type] = [];
    if (!typeLayerMap[lay.type].includes(k)) typeLayerMap[lay.type].push(k);
    if (lay.secondary) {
      typeScore[lay.secondary] = (typeScore[lay.secondary] || 0) + pS / 4;
      if (!typeLayerMap[lay.secondary]) typeLayerMap[lay.secondary] = [];
    }
  });
  const sorted = Object.entries(typeScore)
    .filter(([, s]) => s >= 5)
    .sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])
    .map(([type, score]) => ({ type, pct: Math.round(score), layers: typeLayerMap[type] || [] }));
  const total = sorted.reduce((s, e) => s + e.pct, 0);
  if (total > 0 && total !== 100 && sorted.length) sorted[0].pct += (100 - total);

  const distinctCount = sorted.length;
  _r2MbtiCurrentType = sorted[0].type;
  const cur = _ETHR_MBTI_TABLE[sorted[0].type] || { name:'?', emoji:'?? };

  // 銝?湔扳?餈?  const consistencyMsg = (() => {
    if (distinctCount === 1) return `<div style="background:rgba(255,255,255,0.1);padding:8px 12px;border-radius:8px;font-size:12px;margin-bottom:10px;">??<b>?扳擃漲銝??/b>嚗???憓????曉?銝蝔桐犖?潭見鞎??批?閮銵?銝??/div>`;
    if (distinctCount <= 2) return `<div style="background:rgba(255,255,255,0.1);padding:8px 12px;border-radius:8px;font-size:12px;margin-bottom:10px;">? <b>?鈭箸</b>嚗????? 2 蝔桐犖?潘??祕?芣???憭耦鞊∩???銝摰榆?啜?/div>`;
    if (distinctCount <= 4) return `<div style="background:rgba(255,255,255,0.1);padding:8px 12px;border-radius:8px;font-size:12px;margin-bottom:10px;">?? <b>憭?鈭箸撅斗活</b>嚗銝???銝???${distinctCount} 蝔桐犖?潭見鞎??拇??批撥??憓??漲擃?/div>`;
    return `<div style="background:rgba(255,255,255,0.1);padding:8px 12px;border-radius:8px;font-size:12px;margin-bottom:10px;">?? <b>鞊?憭見?扳</b>嚗瘜Ｗ???${distinctCount} 蝔桐犖?澆?賣改??冽擃漲銴?銝?瘛勗漲?犖??/div>`;
  })();

  const layerLabel = (k) => (_ETHR_MBTI_LAYERS[k] && _ETHR_MBTI_LAYERS[k].label) || k;
  const groupSummary = sorted.map((g, idx) => {
    const mEntry = _ETHR_MBTI_TABLE[g.type] || { name:'?', emoji:'?? };
    const barColor = idx === 0 ? '#90caf9' : '#7986cb';
    const layersText = g.layers.length ? g.layers.map(layerLabel).join(' / ') : '??敶梢';
    return `<div style="padding:8px 10px;background:rgba(255,255,255,0.07);border-radius:8px;margin-bottom:6px;border-left:3px solid ${barColor};">
      <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;margin-bottom:4px;">
        <div style="font-weight:700;font-size:13px;color:#fff;">${mEntry.emoji} ${g.type} 繚 ${mEntry.name}</div>
        <span style="font-size:13px;font-weight:800;color:${barColor};">${g.pct}%</span>
      </div>
      <div style="height:5px;background:rgba(255,255,255,0.15);border-radius:3px;margin-bottom:4px;">
        <div style="height:100%;width:${g.pct}%;background:${barColor};border-radius:3px;transition:width 0.6s;"></div>
      </div>
      <div style="font-size:10px;color:#aaa;">${g.layers.length ? '??嚗? + layersText : '(???賊??'}</div>
    </div>`;
  }).join('');

  const sampleCount = (acc.attn || []).length;
  const summaryEl = document.getElementById('r2-mbti-summary');
  if (summaryEl) {
    summaryEl.innerHTML = `<div style="margin-bottom:8px;">?祆活 ${sampleCount} 蝑瘜Ｚ???繚 ?刻???<b>${distinctCount} 蝔格扳璅??</b>嚗?瘥?閮?100%嚗?</div>${consistencyMsg}${groupSummary}<div style="font-size:10px;color:#777;margin-top:8px;">暺?銝 tab ?舐???憓惜甈∠?閰喟敦隤芣???/div>`;
  }

  // ?湔 tab 憿 badge
  const tag = document.getElementById('r2-mbti-current');
  if (tag) tag.textContent = `${cur.emoji} ${sorted[0].type} 繚 ${cur.name}`;

  // ?梯???蝬剖漲璇?  const legacy = document.getElementById('r2-mbti-legacy');
  if (legacy) legacy.style.display = 'none';

  _bindR2MbtiTabs();
  _renderR2MbtiList();
}

// 撠瘜Ｗ????葡?撣嗆? prefix ??DOM ??嚗?et-' 蝯?eegtest-result嚗?r2-' 蝯?screen-results嚗?function _renderBrainAnalysisInto(prefix) {
  if (!_eegLiveAccum) return;
  const acc = _eegLiveAccum;
  const _isChildRender = (_currentReportType || '').startsWith('child_') || _currentReportType === 'child';
  const indices = _etComputeAll(acc, _isChildRender);
  const mbti    = _etComputeMBTI(acc);
  // 憒???r2- ?韌嚗孛?澆??扳?? UI

  const info    = _ET_BRAIN_INFO[colorKey] || _ET_BRAIN_INFO.orange;

  const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(prefix + id);
  const setTxt = (id, v) => { const e=$(id); if(e) e.textContent=v; };
  const setW   = (id, v) => { const e=$(id); if(e) e.style.width=v+'%'; };

  // ?? ?虫犖憿 ??
  const circle = $('brain-color-circle');
  if (circle) { circle.textContent = info.emoji; circle.style.background = info.bg;
                circle.style.border = `3px solid ${info.color}`; }
  setTxt('brain-color-name', info.name);
  setTxt('brain-color-desc', info.desc);

  // ?? 瘙箇???嚗??帖璇?銝??賊＊?桐?瘙箇????賂???
  const dec=Math.round(indices.DEC.pct); setW('bar-dec', dec); setTxt('pct-dec', dec+'%');
  const ldr=Math.round(indices.LDR.pct); setW('bar-ldr', ldr); setTxt('pct-ldr', ldr+'%');
  const ebi=Math.round(indices.EBI.pct); setW('bar-ebi', ebi); setTxt('pct-ebi', ebi+'%');
  const res=Math.round(indices.RES.pct); setW('bar-res', res); setTxt('pct-res', res+'%');

  // ?? ?行郭?雁?曉?璇?r2- 銝＊蝷?MBTI 隞??嚗t- 靽??＊蝷綽???
  if (prefix !== 'r2-') {
    // ?亙熒瑼Ｘ??/ eegtest ??甇?虜憿舐內 MBTI 隞??
    setTxt('mbti-type', mbti.type);
  }
  // ?雁?曉?璇??拙??ａ皜脫?嚗? r2- ??mbti-dims 憛怠 r2-mbti-dims-wrap ?嚗?  const dimsTargetId = prefix === 'r2-' ? 'mbti-dims' : 'mbti-dims';
  const dimsEl = $('mbti-dims');
  if (dimsEl) {
    // 摰儔?行郭?雁?葉?牧???誨 E/I N/S T/F J/P 摮?嚗?    const dimLabels = prefix === 'r2-'
      ? [
          { left:'憭??曉?', right:'?批??曉?' },
          { left:'?渲死??,   right:'????   },
          { left:'?扳捱蝑?, right:'??瘙箇?' },
          { left:'閮??,   right:'敶折?? },
        ]
      : null;
    dimsEl.innerHTML = mbti.dims.map((d, i) => {
      const dominated = d.pct >= 50 ? d.pct : 100 - d.pct;
      const fillL = d.pct >= 50 ? d.pct : 0;
      const fillR = d.pct < 50  ? 100 - d.pct : 0;
      const labelL = dimLabels ? dimLabels[i].left  : d.pair[0];
      const labelR = dimLabels ? dimLabels[i].right : d.pair[2];
      return `<div class="et-dim-row">
        <span class="et-dim-l" style="color:${fillL>50?'#90caf9':'rgba(255,255,255,0.3)'}${prefix==='r2-'?';font-size:10px':''}">${labelL}</span>
        <div class="et-dim-track">
          <div class="et-dim-fill-l" style="width:${fillL}%"></div>
          <div class="et-dim-fill-r" style="width:${fillR}%"></div>
        </div>
        <span class="et-dim-r" style="color:${fillR>50?'#ce93d8':'rgba(255,255,255,0.3)'}${prefix==='r2-'?';font-size:10px':''}">${labelR}</span>
        <span class="et-dim-strength">${Math.round(dominated)}%</span>
      </div>`;
    }).join('');
  }

  // ?? 鈭之?芸 ??
  const keys = Object.keys(indices).filter(k => k !== '_bands');
  const sorted = keys.map(k => ({ ...indices[k], key:k }))
                     .sort((a,b) => b.pct - a.pct).slice(0, 5);
  const strengthColors = ['#ffd700','#c0c0c0','#cd7f32','#4caf50','#2196f3'];
  const strEl = $('strengths');
  if (strEl) strEl.innerHTML = sorted.map((s, i) =>
    `<div class="et-strength-item">
      <div class="et-strength-rank" style="background:${strengthColors[i]}22;color:${strengthColors[i]};">${i+1}</div>
      <div class="et-strength-name">${s.label}</div>
      <div class="et-strength-val">${Math.round(s.pct)}%</div>
    </div>`
  ).join('');

  // ?? ?行郭?餃葆璁?嚗葉??蝢抬???
  const bnd = indices._bands;
  const bands = [
    { name:'瘜冽???,   val:Math.round(bnd.att), color:'#ff9800' },
    { name:'?暸?摨?,   val:Math.round(bnd.med), color:'#00bcd4' },
    { name:'瘛勗漲隡', val:Math.round(bnd.d),   color:'#607d8b' },
    { name:'?渲死?菜?', val:Math.round(bnd.t),   color:'#9c27b0' },
    { name:'?暸?撠釣', val:Math.round(bnd.a),   color:'#4caf50' },
    { name:'?銵?, val:Math.round(bnd.b),   color:'#ff5722' },
    { name:'擃??游?', val:Math.round(bnd.g),   color:'#f44336' },

  if (bandsEl) bandsEl.innerHTML = bands.map(b =>
    `<div class="et-bar-row">
      <span class="et-bar-name">${b.name}</span>
      <div class="et-bar-track"><div class="et-bar-fill" style="background:${b.color};width:${_etClamp(b.val,0,100)}%"></div></div>
      <span class="et-bar-pct">${b.val}%</span>
    </div>`
  ).join('');
}

// reportKey ??reportType + variant + ?臬?芰??洵銝蝡?// ?冽瘙箏??勗??????澆?芯?蝯?蝭 + ?臬?脣皜祈岫璅∪?
function _parseReportKey(key) {
  // NT$1 皜祈岫嚗 trial ??蝡? 1,3,8,12 ??4 蝡?~3-5 ??嚗??? full嚗?8 蝭 ~15-20 ??嚗?  if (key === 'test_1')          return { type: 'life_script', variant: 'full',  chapter1Only: false, chaptersToGenerate: [1,2] };
  if (key.startsWith('life_'))   return { type: 'life_script', variant: key.split('_')[1], chapter1Only: false };
  if (key.startsWith('child_'))  return { type: 'child',       variant: key.split('_')[1], chapter1Only: false };
  if (key.startsWith('up_'))     return { type: 'life_script', variant: 'vip',  chapter1Only: false };
  return { type: 'life_script', variant: 'full', chapter1Only: false };
}

              btn = document.createElement('button');
              btn.className = 'pay-now-btn';
              btn.style.cssText = 'width:100%;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#667eea,#764ba2);color:white;border:none;border-radius:14px;padding:16px;font-size:16px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;box-shadow:0 6px 20px rgba(102,126,234,0.45);margin-top:4px;';
              imgParent.insertBefore(btn, imgParent.querySelector('.qr-order-id') || null);
            }
            btn.textContent = '? 蝡隞狡嚗?祆???隞狡??';
            btn.onclick = function () {
              try {
                if (typeof AndroidBridge !== 'undefined' && AndroidBridge.openPayUrl) {
                  AndroidBridge.openPayUrl(payUrl);
                } else {
                  window.open(payUrl, '_blank') || (location.href = payUrl);
                }
              } catch (e) {
                location.href = payUrl;
              }
            };
          }
        } catch (e) { console.warn('?蝡隞狡??憭望?', e); }
      }

      // ??頛芾岷
      startPaymentPolling(order.order_id, qrScreen);
    } else {
      // 敺垢?芸???嚗??舀????匱蝥?demo ?剁?
      console.warn("敺垢?芷??嚗蝙??Demo 璅∪?");
    }
  }, 180);
}

// ??? ?頃??嚗?亥粥隞狡瘚?嚗?隞狡??敺????行郭瑼Ｘ葫 ????????????????
function selectUpgrade(el, reportKey, amount, qrScreen) {
  // 閬死?詨?
  document.querySelectorAll('#screen-reports .report-item').forEach(r => {
    r.classList.remove('selected');
    r.style.borderColor = '';
    r.style.background = '';
  });
  el.classList.add('selected');
  el.style.borderColor = '#FF6B35';
  el.style.background = '#fff5f2';

  // ? selectSingleReport ??瘚?蝔?  selectSingleReport(el, reportKey, 'NT$' + amount.toLocaleString(), qrScreen);
}

// ??? ???勗?嚗??澆??殷?VIP ?怠?鞈澆?蝝???????????????????????????????????
// ??_loadVipCustomers() 敺?蝡臬????伐?銝?撖急香
let _vipCustomers = [];   // ?瑁??‵??
// ?剖?憿瘙?靘???hash 瘙箏?嚗???偶???莎?
const _AVATAR_COLORS = [
  'linear-gradient(135deg,#667eea,#764ba2)',
  'linear-gradient(135deg,#f093fb,#f5576c)',
  'linear-gradient(135deg,#43e97b,#38f9d7)',
  'linear-gradient(135deg,#fa709a,#fee140)',
  'linear-gradient(135deg,#4facfe,#00f2fe)',
  'linear-gradient(135deg,#a18cd1,#fbc2eb)',
  'linear-gradient(135deg,#fccb90,#d57eeb)',
  'linear-gradient(135deg,#84fab0,#8fd3f4)',
];
function _avatarColor(name) {
  let h = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < name.length; i++) h = (h * 31 + name.charCodeAt(i)) & 0xffff;
  return _AVATAR_COLORS[h % _AVATAR_COLORS.length];
}

async function _loadVipCustomers() {
  // 敺?甈曇??? VIP 撌脖?甈曄??葫??life_vip / child_vip ??嚗?  // 銋? sessions ??撟湧翩鞈?嚗?雿萄??駁?
  let payments = [], sessions = [];
  try {
    const [rp, rs] = await Promise.all([
      apiFetch('/payments/my?limit=200'),
      apiFetch('/eeg/sessions?limit=200'),
    ]);
    if (rp.ok) payments = (await rp.json()).payments || [];
    if (rs.ok) sessions = (await rs.json()).sessions || [];
  } catch (e) { }

  // 蝭拚隞?VIP 甈曄??葫??? life_vip?hild_vip嚗aid ??嚗?  const VIP_TYPES = new Set(['life_vip', 'child_vip']);
  const vipPayments = payments.filter(p => VIP_TYPES.has(p.report_type) && p.status === 'paid');

  // 隞?subject_name ?駁?嚗????唬?蝑?  const byName = {};
  vipPayments.forEach(p => {
    if (!p.subject_name) return;
    if (!byName[p.subject_name] || (p.paid_at || 0) > (byName[p.subject_name].paid_at || 0)) {
      byName[p.subject_name] = p;
    }
  });

  // ?? sessions 鋆?age嚗?甈暹?銝?摰?摮?age嚗?  const sessionByName = {};
  sessions.forEach(s => {
    if (s.subject_name && !sessionByName[s.subject_name]) {
      sessionByName[s.subject_name] = s;
    }
  });

  const pad = n => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
  _vipCustomers = Object.values(byName).map((p, i) => {
    const sess = sessionByName[p.subject_name] || {};
    const d    = p.paid_at ? new Date(p.paid_at * 1000) : new Date((p.created_at || 0) * 1000);
    return {
      id:           `p${i}`,
      name:         p.subject_name,
      age:          sess.subject_age || null,
      gender:       sess.subject_gender || null,
      email:        p.subject_email || '',
      report_type:  p.report_type,
      level:        'vip',
      completedAt:  `${d.getFullYear()}/${pad(d.getMonth()+1)}/${pad(d.getDate())}`,
      avatarColor:  _avatarColor(p.subject_name),
      // ?行郭 session 鞈?嚗?靽?????典嚗?      session_id:   sess.session_id || null,
      payment_id:   p.payment_id,
    };
  });
}

const _relationConfigs = {
  parent: { title: '?豢???鈭?繚 閬芸?', label: '閬芸??行郭?望???勗?', minPersons: 3, color: '#c2185b', emoji: '???抽?? },
  couple: { title: '?豢???鈭?繚 憭怠氖', label: '憭怠氖?行郭?望???勗?', minPersons: 2, color: '#c2185b', emoji: '??' },
  lover:  { title: '?豢???鈭?繚 ?噤', label: '?噤?行郭?望???勗?', minPersons: 2, color: '#c2185b', emoji: '??' },
};
let _currentRelationType = null;
let _selectedPersonIds   = [];

async function openRelation(type) {
  _currentRelationType = type;
  _selectedPersonIds   = [];
  const cfg = _relationConfigs[type];

  // ?歲?＊蝷箄??乩葉
  document.getElementById('relation-select-title').textContent = cfg.title;
  document.getElementById('relation-hint').innerHTML =
    `? 隞乩??箏歇摰? <b>VIP ?犖?勗?</b>?犖?∴?隢??<b>${cfg.minPersons} 雿?{cfg.minPersons===3?' 隞乩?':''}</b>嚗摰?蝟餌絞撠?亦???{cfg.label}???⊿??瑼Ｘ葫?;
  document.getElementById('relation-list').innerHTML =
    '<div style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:#bbb;font-size:13px;">頛 VIP ?銝凌?/div>';
  document.getElementById('relation-count-label').textContent = '?舫鈭箏';
  document.getElementById('relation-empty').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('relation-selected-info').style.display = 'none';
  const btn = document.getElementById('relation-submit-btn');
  btn.style.opacity = '0.4';
  btn.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
  goto('screen-relation-select');

  // ??頛 VIP ?
  await _loadVipCustomers();

  const list = document.getElementById('relation-list');
  list.innerHTML = '';
  const eligible = _vipCustomers;   // _loadVipCustomers 撌脣靽? VIP
  document.getElementById('relation-count-label').textContent = `?舫鈭箏嚗 ${eligible.length} 鈭綽?`;
  document.getElementById('relation-empty').style.display = eligible.length === 0 ? 'block' : 'none';

  if (eligible.length === 0) return;

  eligible.forEach(p => {
    const initial = p.name.charAt(0);
    const ageTxt  = p.age ? `${p.age}甇?繚 ` : '';
    const badge   = `<span style="background:#fffde7;color:#f9a825;font-size:10px;padding:1px 6px;border-radius:6px;margin-left:6px;font-weight:600;">?? VIP</span>`;
    const row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'relation-person';
    row.dataset.pid = p.id;
    row.style.cssText = 'background:white;border-radius:12px;padding:12px 14px;box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.06);display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;cursor:pointer;border:2px solid transparent;transition:all 0.2s;';
    row.innerHTML = `
      <div style="width:40px;height:40px;border-radius:50%;background:${p.avatarColor};display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;color:white;font-weight:700;font-size:16px;flex-shrink:0;">${initial}</div>
      <div style="flex:1;">
        <div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:600;color:#1a1a2e;">${p.name}${badge}</div>
        <div style="font-size:11px;color:#888;margin-top:2px;">${ageTxt}${p.completedAt} 摰? VIP</div>
      </div>
      <div class="person-check" style="width:24px;height:24px;border-radius:50%;border:2px solid #ddd;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:12px;transition:all 0.2s;"></div>
    `;
    row.onclick = () => togglePerson(row);
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
}

function togglePerson(el) {
  const pid = el.dataset.pid;
  const check = el.querySelector('.person-check');
  const isSelected = _selectedPersonIds.includes(pid);

  if (isSelected) {
    el.style.borderColor = 'transparent';
    el.style.background = 'white';
    check.textContent = '';
    check.style.background = '';
    check.style.borderColor = '#ddd';
    _selectedPersonIds = _selectedPersonIds.filter(x => x !== pid);
  } else {
    el.style.borderColor = '#c2185b';
    el.style.background = '#fce4ec';
    check.textContent = '??;
    check.style.background = '#c2185b';
    check.style.borderColor = '#c2185b';
    check.style.color = 'white';
    _selectedPersonIds.push(pid);
  }

  const cfg = _relationConfigs[_currentRelationType] || { minPersons: 2 };
  const selectedNames = _selectedPersonIds
    .map(id => (_vipCustomers.find(p => p.id === id) || {}).name)
    .filter(Boolean).join('??);
  const infoEl  = document.getElementById('relation-selected-info');
  const btnEl   = document.getElementById('relation-submit-btn');
  const namesEl = document.getElementById('relation-selected-names');

  if (_selectedPersonIds.length >= cfg.minPersons) {
    infoEl.style.display = 'block';
    namesEl.textContent = selectedNames;
    btnEl.style.opacity = '1';
    btnEl.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';
  } else {
    infoEl.style.display = _selectedPersonIds.length > 0 ? 'block' : 'none';
    namesEl.textContent = selectedNames + `嚗?撌?${cfg.minPersons - _selectedPersonIds.length} 雿?`;
    btnEl.style.opacity = '0.4';
    btnEl.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
  }
}

async function generateRelationReport() {
  const cfg = _relationConfigs[_currentRelationType];
  if (!cfg) { goto('screen-home'); return; }

  const selectedPersons = _selectedPersonIds
    .map(id => _vipCustomers.find(p => p.id === id))
    .filter(Boolean);
  const names  = selectedPersons.map(p => p.name).join('??);
  const emails = [...new Set(selectedPersons.map(p => p.email).filter(Boolean))];

  // ?? UI 憿? ??敺垢 report_type
  const typeMap = { couple: 'marital', lover: 'marital', parent: 'parent_child' };
  const reportType = typeMap[_currentRelationType] || _currentRelationType;

  // ???摰??恍嚗??葉???
  document.getElementById('relation-done-icon').textContent  = cfg.emoji;
  document.getElementById('relation-done-title').textContent = '???勗???銝哨?';
  document.getElementById('relation-done-msg').innerHTML =
    `<b>${cfg.label}</b><br>撠情嚗?{names}` +
    (emails.length ? `<br><span style="font-size:11px;color:#aaa;margin-top:4px;display:block;">?勗?撠??嚗?{emails.join('??)}</span>` : '');
  document.getElementById('relation-status-bar').style.background   = '#fce4ec';
  document.getElementById('relation-status-bar').style.borderColor  = '#f48fb1';
  document.getElementById('relation-status-bar').style.color        = '#c2185b';
  document.getElementById('relation-status-bar').textContent = '??蝟餌絞甇????行郭?望鞈?嚗???撠??? Email??;
  document.getElementById('relation-result-btns').style.display = 'none';
  goto('screen-relation-done');

  // 撱箇? extra嚗?鈭箄???
  let extra = {};
  const p0 = selectedPersons[0];
  const p1 = selectedPersons[1];

  if (reportType === 'marital') {
    extra = {
      husband_name:       p0 ? p0.name : '',
      husband_session_id: p0 ? p0.session_id : null,
      wife_name:          p1 ? p1.name : '',
      wife_session_id:    p1 ? p1.session_id : null,
    };
  } else if (reportType === 'parent_child') {
    const roleMap   = ['dad', 'mom', 'child1', 'child2'];
    const roleZhMap = ['?貊', '慦賢直', '摮拙?1', '摮拙?2'];
    extra = {
      family_name: p0 ? p0.name.charAt(0) + '摰? : '??摰嗅滬',
      members: selectedPersons.map((p, i) => ({
        role:       roleMap[i]   || ('child' + i),
        role_zh:    roleZhMap[i] || ('摮拙?' + i),
        name:       p.name,
        present:    true,
        session_id: p.session_id,
        data:       null,   // 敺垢敺?session_id 鋆?
      })),
    };
  }

  // ?澆敺垢
  try {
    const resp = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/report-gen/start`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        subject_name:   p0 ? p0.name : names,
        subject_email:  emails[0] || null,
        subject_age:    p0 ? (p0.age || null) : null,
        subject_gender: '',
        report_type:    reportType,
        variant:        'vip',
        session_id:     p0 ? (p0.session_id || null) : null,
        brainwave_data: null,   // 敺垢敺?session_id 鋆?
        extra:          extra,
      }),
    });

    const data = await resp.json();
    const xmode = data.external_mode || data.mode || '';
    const statusBar   = document.getElementById('relation-status-bar');
    const resultBtns  = document.getElementById('relation-result-btns');
    const doneTitle   = document.getElementById('relation-done-title');

    if (!resp.ok || data.ok === false) {
      const errMsg = data.detail?.message || data.detail?.reason || data.error || `HTTP ${resp.status}`;
      statusBar.style.background  = '#ffeaea';
      statusBar.style.borderColor = '#f44';
      statusBar.style.color       = '#c00';
      statusBar.textContent = `????憭望?嚗?{errMsg}`;
      doneTitle.textContent = '??憭望?';
      document.getElementById('relation-done-icon').textContent = '??';
      return;
    }

    if (xmode === 'marital_rest') {
      // 憭怠氖?勗?嚗?交? PDF
      statusBar.style.background  = '#e8f5e9';
      statusBar.style.borderColor = '#66bb6a';
      statusBar.style.color       = '#2e7d32';
      statusBar.textContent = '??憭怠氖?行郭?勗?撌脩?????';
      doneTitle.textContent = '?勗?撌脣?????';
      if (data.result_url) {
        resultBtns.style.display = 'block';
        resultBtns.innerHTML = `<a href="${data.result_url}" target="_blank"
          style="display:block;background:#c2185b;color:white;border-radius:12px;padding:12px;font-weight:700;text-decoration:none;font-size:14px;margin-bottom:8px;">
          ?? 銝?憭怠氖?勗? PDF ??/a>`;
      }
    } else if (xmode === 'parent_child_rest') {
      // 閬芸??勗?嚗歲?啣??????      statusBar.style.background  = '#e8f5e9';
      statusBar.style.borderColor = '#66bb6a';
      statusBar.style.color       = '#2e7d32';
      statusBar.textContent = '??閬芸??勗?撌脤?憪???隢?銝????亦??脣漲??;
      doneTitle.textContent = '?勗???銝??';
      if (data.result_url) {
        resultBtns.style.display = 'block';
        resultBtns.innerHTML = `<a href="${data.result_url}" target="_blank"
          style="display:block;background:#c2185b;color:white;border-radius:12px;padding:12px;font-weight:700;text-decoration:none;font-size:14px;margin-bottom:8px;">
          ?? ?亦?閬芸??勗??脣漲 ??/a>`;
      }
    } else {
      statusBar.style.background  = '#fffde7';
      statusBar.style.borderColor = '#ffd54f';
      statusBar.style.color       = '#795548';
      statusBar.textContent = `???勗???銝哨?${xmode || '?芰璅∪?'}嚗?摰?敺?撖?Email?;
    }

  } catch (e) {
    const statusBar = document.getElementById('relation-status-bar');
    statusBar.style.background  = '#ffeaea';
    statusBar.style.borderColor = '#f44';
    statusBar.style.color       = '#c00';
    statusBar.textContent = `?????憭望?嚗?{e.message}`;
    document.getElementById('relation-done-title').textContent = '??憭望?';
    document.getElementById('relation-done-icon').textContent = '??';
  }
}

// ?皜祈岫嚗芋?砌?甈暹???/**
 * ??憭 URL嚗? * - Android WebView ???澆 AndroidBridge.openPayUrl()嚗蝟餌絞?汗?剁?Chrome嚗??? * - 獢??汗?????湔 window.open()
 */
function openExternalUrl(url) {
  if (!url) return;
  try {
    if (typeof AndroidBridge !== 'undefined' && AndroidBridge &&
        typeof AndroidBridge.openPayUrl === 'function') {
      AndroidBridge.openPayUrl(url);
      return;
    }
  } catch(e) { /* 璈銝?剁?fallback */ }
  window.open(url, '_blank', 'noopener');
}

function copyPayLink(anchorId) {
  const a = document.getElementById(anchorId);
  if (!a) return;
  navigator.clipboard.writeText(a.href).then(() => {
    const btn = a.nextElementSibling;
    if (btn) { btn.textContent = '撌脰?鋆???; setTimeout(() => btn.textContent = '銴ˊ', 2000); }
  }).catch(() => {
    // ???寞?
    const range = document.createRange();
    range.selectNode(a);
    window.getSelection().removeAllRanges();
    window.getSelection().addRange(range);
    document.execCommand('copy');
    window.getSelection().removeAllRanges();
  });
}

// ??? ?舐窗????唾?銵典嚗摮 localStorage 靘??啗???????????????????
const _CONTACT_KEY = 'eeg_account_requests';

function _loadContactRequests() {
  try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(_CONTACT_KEY) || '[]'); }
  catch (e) { return []; }
}
function _saveContactRequests(arr) {
  try { localStorage.setItem(_CONTACT_KEY, JSON.stringify(arr)); } catch (e) {}
}

function onOrgTypeChange() {
  const sel = document.getElementById('contact-org-type');
  const other = document.getElementById('contact-org-other');
  if (!sel || !other) return;
  other.style.display = (sel.value === '?嗡?') ? 'block' : 'none';
}

function submitContactRequest() {
  const name    = (document.getElementById('contact-name').value || '').trim();
  const phone   = (document.getElementById('contact-phone').value || '').trim();
  const email   = (document.getElementById('contact-email').value || '').trim();
  const orgType = (document.getElementById('contact-org-type').value || '').trim();
  const orgName = (document.getElementById('contact-org').value || '').trim();
  const orgOth  = (document.getElementById('contact-org-other').value || '').trim();
  const ref     = (document.getElementById('contact-ref').value || '').trim();
  const note    = (document.getElementById('contact-note').value || '').trim();

  if (!name || !phone || !email) {
    alert('隢‵撖怠???璈? Email');
    return;
  }
  if (!orgType) {
    alert('隢??撅砍雿?/ 頨思遢');
    return;
  }
  if (orgType === '?嗡?' && !orgOth) {
    alert('隢牧??澈隞?);
    return;
  }

  const orgFinal = orgType === '?嗡?' ? orgOth : orgType;
  const req = {
    id: 'REQ' + Date.now().toString(36).toUpperCase(),
    name, phone, email,
    org_type: orgType,
    org: orgName,
    role_label: orgFinal,
    ref, note,
    status: 'pending',
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  };
  const arr = _loadContactRequests();
  arr.unshift(req);
  _saveContactRequests(arr);

  // ?岫??隞賢敺垢嚗??API嚗?蝡舀????404 ?賡?暺蕭?伐?
  fetch(`${API_BASE}/contact-requests`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify(req),
  })
    .then(r => { if (!r.ok && r.status !== 404) console.warn('contact-requests POST', r.status); })
    .catch(() => { /* 敺垢?芸???敹賜 */ });

  // 憿舐內摰?????
  document.getElementById('contact-done-summary').innerHTML =
    `<div><b>?唾?蝺刻?嚗?/b>${req.id}</div>` +
    `<div style="margin-top:4px;"><b>憪?嚗?/b>${name}</div>` +
    `<div><b>??嚗?/b>${phone}</div>` +
    `<div><b>Email嚗?/b>${email}</div>` +
    `<div><b>頨思遢嚗?/b>${orgFinal}${orgName? ' 繚 '+orgName:''}</div>`;

  // 皜征甈?
  ['contact-name','contact-phone','contact-email','contact-org','contact-org-other','contact-ref','contact-note']
    .forEach(id => { const el = document.getElementById(id); if (el) el.value = ''; });
  document.getElementById('contact-org-type').value = '';
  document.getElementById('contact-org-other').style.display = 'none';

  goto('screen-contact-done');
}

// ??? 蝞∠??∪????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????
let _currentAdminTab = 'pending';

async function adminLogin() {
  const uRaw = (document.getElementById('admin-user').value || '').trim();
  const pRaw = (document.getElementById('admin-pass').value || '').trim();
  const u = _toHalfWidth(uRaw).replace(/[\s-]/g, '');
  const p = _toHalfWidth(pRaw);
  if (!u || !p) { alert('隢撓?交?璈?撖Ⅳ'); return; }

  if (u !== uRaw || p !== pRaw) {
    console.warn('[adminLogin] ?菜葫?啣敶Ｗ???撌脰????敶Ｗ???遣霅唬?甈∟撓?交???銝剜?頛詨瘜?);
  }

  // ??/auth/login 撽?嚗蒂閬? role === 'admin'
  let resp;
  try {
    resp = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/auth/login`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ phone: u, password: p }),
    });
  } catch (e) {
    alert('隡箸??券??憭望?嚗? + e.message); return;
  }
  if (!resp.ok) {
    const err = await resp.json().catch(() => ({ detail: '撣唾???蝣潮隤? }));
    alert(err.detail || '?餃憭望?');
    return;
  }
  const data = await resp.json();
  if (data.role !== 'admin') {
    alert('甇文董??蝞∠??∴??⊥??脣敺');
    return;
  }
  _saveAuth(data);
  goto('screen-admin-console');
  switchAdminTab(_currentAdminTab);
}

function switchAdminTab(tab) {
  _currentAdminTab = tab;
  document.querySelectorAll('.admin-tab').forEach(b => {
    b.classList.toggle('active', b.dataset.tab === tab);
  });

  const titleMap = {
    pending:     '撣唾?撖拇 繚 敺祟??,
    approved:    '撣唾?撖拇 繚 撌脫??,
    rejected:    '撣唾?撖拇 繚 撌脫?蝯?,
    consultants: '憿批?皜嚗B嚗?,
    subjects:    '?葫?恣??,
    earnings:    '?膜蝮質汗',
    rules:       '?膜閬?',
    reports:        '?勗?蝞∠? 繚 ?脩垢 PDF',
    monitor:        '?勗?????? 繚 ?單??脣漲?隤斗隤?,
    'eeg-compare':  '? ?行郭瘥?閮箸',
  };
  const titleEl = document.getElementById('admin-console-title');
  if (titleEl) titleEl.textContent = titleMap[tab] || '蝞∠??∪???;

  document.getElementById('admin-pane-account').style.display     = (['pending','approved','rejected'].includes(tab)) ? 'flex' : 'none';
  document.getElementById('admin-pane-consultants').style.display = (tab === 'consultants') ? 'flex' : 'none';
  document.getElementById('admin-pane-subjects').style.display    = (tab === 'subjects')    ? 'flex' : 'none';
  document.getElementById('admin-pane-earnings').style.display    = (tab === 'earnings')    ? 'flex' : 'none';
  document.getElementById('admin-pane-rules').style.display       = (tab === 'rules')       ? 'flex' : 'none';
  document.getElementById('admin-pane-reports').style.display     = (tab === 'reports')     ? 'flex' : 'none';
  const monitorEl = document.getElementById('admin-pane-monitor');
  if (monitorEl) monitorEl.style.display = (tab === 'monitor') ? 'flex' : 'none';
  const eegCmpEl = document.getElementById('admin-pane-eeg-compare');
  if (eegCmpEl) eegCmpEl.style.display = (tab === 'eeg-compare') ? 'flex' : 'none';

  // ?ａ????????甇Ｚ???  if (tab !== 'monitor' && _monitorTimer) { clearInterval(_monitorTimer); _monitorTimer = null; }

  if (['pending','approved','rejected'].includes(tab)) renderAdminList();
  else if (tab === 'consultants')  renderAdminConsultants();
  else if (tab === 'subjects')     renderAdminSubjects();
  else if (tab === 'earnings')     renderEarningsOverview();
  else if (tab === 'rules')        _loadShareRulesAsync().then(() => renderShareRulesEditor());
  else if (tab === 'reports')      renderAdminReports();
  else if (tab === 'monitor')      { renderAdminMonitor(); _toggleMonitorAutorefresh(); }
  else if (tab === 'eeg-compare')  loadEegCompare();
}

// ??? ?行郭瘥?閮箸嚗?憿舐內?餈?N 蝑?session ?畾萄像???????????????????????
async function loadEegCompare() {
  const bodyEl = document.getElementById('eeg-compare-body');
  if (!bodyEl) return;
  const limit = parseInt(document.getElementById('eeg-compare-limit')?.value || '20', 10);
  bodyEl.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align:center;color:#888;padding:30px;">??頛銝凌?/div>';

  try {
    const r = await apiFetch(`/eeg/admin/compare?limit=${limit}`);
    if (!r.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${r.status}`);
    const data = await r.json();
    const rows = data.rows || [];

    if (rows.length === 0) {
      bodyEl.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align:center;color:#aaa;padding:40px;">撠鞈?</div>';
      return;
    }

    // 閮?????憭扼?撠潘??冽?嚗?    const fields = ['attention','meditation','delta','theta','low_alpha','high_alpha','low_beta','high_beta','low_gamma','high_gamma'];
    const stats = {};
    fields.forEach(f => {
      const vals = rows.map(r => r[f]).filter(v => v != null);
      stats[f] = { min: Math.min(...vals), max: Math.max(...vals), range: Math.max(...vals) - Math.min(...vals) };
    });

    // ?脣蔗?勗???range 頞之頞??儘??    const cellBg = (field, val) => {
      if (val == null) return '#f5f5f5';
      const s = stats[field];
      if (s.range < 3) return '#fff8e1';  // 撟曆??詨? ??暺霅衣內
      const pct = (val - s.min) / s.range;
      const r = Math.round(255 - pct * 80);
      const g = Math.round(200 + pct * 30);
      const b = Math.round(200 - pct * 40);
      return `rgb(${r},${g},${b})`;
    };

    const fieldLabels = {
      attention:   '撠釣\n(eSense)',
      meditation:  '?暸?\n(eSense)',
      delta:       'Delta',
      theta:       'Theta',
      low_alpha:   '帢?n(LowAlpha)',
      high_alpha:  '帢?n(HiAlpha)',
      low_beta:    '帣?n(LowBeta)',
      high_beta:   '帣?n(HiBeta)',
      low_gamma:   '帠?n(LowGamma)',
      high_gamma:  '帠?n(HiGamma)',
    };

    // 閮?????皞榆嚗??榆?啁?摨佗?
    const stddev = (field) => {
      const vals = rows.map(r => r[field]).filter(v => v != null);
      if (vals.length < 2) return 0;
      const mean = vals.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / vals.length;
      return Math.sqrt(vals.reduce((s, v) => s + (v - mean) ** 2, 0) / vals.length).toFixed(1);
    };

    let html = `
      <div style="margin-bottom:10px;font-size:11px;color:#555;line-height:1.6;background:#fff9e6;border:1px solid #ffe082;border-radius:8px;padding:10px 14px;">
        <b>?? ?方?隤芣?</b><br>
        ??<span style="background:#fff8e1;padding:1px 5px;border-radius:3px;">暺?</span>嚗?鈭箏榆頝?撠?< 3%嚗??航?澆?蝣箄?閮??釭<br>
        ??<b>撠釣/?暸?</b> ??ThinkGear ?嗥??湔頛詨嚗????豢迤閬?敶梢嚗???賭誨銵函?撖血榆??br>
        ??<b>?餃葆??/b> 撌脤? log10 甇?????喃蝙???澆榆 5 ??憿舐內?箔?銋撌?10~15%
      </div>
      <div style="overflow-x:auto;">
      <table style="border-collapse:collapse;font-size:11px;min-width:900px;background:white;border-radius:8px;overflow:hidden;box-shadow:0 1px 4px rgba(0,0,0,0.1);">
        <thead>
          <tr style="background:#2D3561;color:white;">
            <th style="padding:8px 10px;text-align:left;min-width:70px;">?葫??/th>
            <th style="padding:8px 6px;text-align:center;min-width:40px;">撟湧翩</th>
            <th style="padding:8px 6px;text-align:center;min-width:40px;">璅???/th>
            <th style="padding:8px 6px;text-align:center;min-width:40px;">?交?</th>
            ${fields.map(f => `<th style="padding:8px 6px;text-align:center;min-width:60px;white-space:pre-line;">${fieldLabels[f]}</th>`).join('')}
          </tr>
          <tr style="background:#1a2547;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.6);font-size:10px;">
            <td colspan="4" style="padding:4px 10px;">璅?撌?(?) ??/td>
            ${fields.map(f => `<td style="padding:4px 6px;text-align:center;font-weight:700;">${stddev(f)}</td>`).join('')}
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
    `;

    rows.forEach((row, i) => {
      const dateStr = row.created_at
        ? new Date(row.created_at * 1000).toLocaleDateString('zh-TW', {month:'2-digit', day:'2-digit'})
        : '??;
      // ?餅扔?亥孛銝嚗?瘜?0 銝??暸?=0嚗Sense ?潘??舀?蝣箇?閮???
      const badContact = (row.attention === 0 && row.meditation === 0);
      const bg = badContact ? '#fff3f3' : (i % 2 === 0 ? '#ffffff' : '#f8faff');
      const nameCell = badContact
        ? `<td style="padding:7px 10px;font-weight:600;color:#c62828;">?? ${row.subject_name || '??}</td>`
        : `<td style="padding:7px 10px;font-weight:600;color:#1a3a6e;">${row.subject_name || '??}</td>`;
      html += `<tr style="background:${bg};" title="${badContact ? '?? 撠釣?擛???嚗?隡潮璆菜閫訾??荔?鞈??⊥?' : ''}">
        ${nameCell}
        <td style="padding:7px 6px;text-align:center;color:#555;">${row.age || '??}</td>
        <td style="padding:7px 6px;text-align:center;color:#555;">${row.sample_count ?? '??}</td>
        <td style="padding:7px 6px;text-align:center;color:#555;font-size:10px;">${dateStr}</td>
        ${fields.map(f => {
          const v = row[f];
          const display = v != null ? v : '??;
          // 撠釣/?暸?=0 銝摰憯??????孵璅?
          const isBadeSense = badContact && (f === 'attention' || f === 'meditation');
          const bg2 = isBadeSense ? '#ffcdd2' : (v != null ? cellBg(f, v) : '#f5f5f5');
          const color2 = isBadeSense ? '#b71c1c' : '#1a3a6e';
          return `<td style="padding:7px 6px;text-align:center;background:${bg2};font-weight:700;color:${color2};">${display}</td>`;
        }).join('')}
      </tr>`;
    });

    html += '</tbody></table></div>';
    bodyEl.innerHTML = html;
  } catch (e) {
    bodyEl.innerHTML = `<div style="text-align:center;color:#c00;padding:30px;">??頛憭望?嚗?{e.message}</div>`;
  }
}

// ??? 憿批?皜嚗? /api/v1/auth/consultants ??DB ?扳??“?董????????????????
let _adminConsultantsCache = [];
async function renderAdminConsultants() {
  const listEl  = document.getElementById('admin-consultant-list');
  const countEl = document.getElementById('admin-consultant-count');
  if (!listEl) return;
  listEl.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:#999;">? 頛銝?..</div>';

  try {
    const r = await apiFetch('/auth/consultants');
    if (!r.ok) {
      let msg = `HTTP ${r.status}`;
      try { const j = await r.json(); if (j && j.detail) msg = j.detail; } catch(e) {}
      throw new Error(msg);
    }
    _adminConsultantsCache = await r.json();
    _filterAdminConsultants();
    if (countEl) countEl.textContent = `??${_adminConsultantsCache.length} 蝑;
  } catch (e) {
    listEl.innerHTML = `<div style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:#c00;">??頛憭望?嚗?{e.message}</div>`;
  }
}

function _filterAdminConsultants() {
  const listEl = document.getElementById('admin-consultant-list');
  const countEl = document.getElementById('admin-consultant-count');
  if (!listEl) return;
  const q = (document.getElementById('admin-consultant-search')?.value || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  const rows = q
    ? _adminConsultantsCache.filter(r =>
        (r.name  || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        (r.phone || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        (r.email || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        (r.org   || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        (r.org_type || '').toLowerCase().includes(q)
      )
    : _adminConsultantsCache;
  if (countEl) countEl.textContent = `??${rows.length} 蝑?{q ? ` / ?券 ${_adminConsultantsCache.length}` : ''}`;
  if (!rows.length) {
    listEl.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:#999;">瘝?蝚血??“??/div>';
    return;
  }

  const roleBadge = (r) => {
    if (r.role === 'admin')  return '<span style="background:#5e35b1;color:white;font-size:10px;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;">ADMIN</span>';
    return '<span style="background:#1976d2;color:white;font-size:10px;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;">憿批?</span>';
  };
  const orgTypeBadge = (t) => {
    if (!t) return '';
    const colors = { '????:'#ff9800', '?渡???:'#4caf50', '隞????:'#00acc1', '撌乩?鈭箏':'#607d8b', '撠?鈭箏':'#9c27b0', '?嗡?':'#9e9e9e' };
    const c = colors[t] || '#9e9e9e';
    return `<span style="background:${c};color:white;font-size:10px;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;">${t}</span>`;
  };
  const activeBadge = (a) =>
    a ? '<span style="color:#4caf50;font-size:11px;">???</span>'
      : '<span style="color:#c00;font-size:11px;">???</span>';

  const me = (typeof _getAuth === 'function') ? _getAuth() : null;
  const myCid = me ? me.consultant_id : null;

  listEl.innerHTML = rows.map(r => {
    const isSelf = r.consultant_id === myCid;
    let actions;
    if (isSelf) {
      actions = `<span style="font-size:11px;color:#888;">嚗撌梧?銝??嚗?/span>`;
    } else {
      const toggleLabel = r.is_active ? '?賂? ?' : '?塚? ?';
      const toggleBg    = r.is_active ? '#ef6c00' : '#4caf50';
      const safeName    = (r.name  || '').replace(/'/g, '');
      const safePhone   = (r.phone || '').replace(/'/g, '');
      actions = `
        <button class="admin-btn" style="background:${toggleBg};color:white;font-size:11px;padding:5px 10px;"
                onclick="toggleConsultantActive(${r.consultant_id}, '${safeName}')">${toggleLabel}</button>
        <button class="admin-btn" style="background:#1565c0;color:white;font-size:11px;padding:5px 10px;"
                onclick="openAdminResetPwdModal(${r.consultant_id}, '${safeName}')">?? ?孵?蝣?/button>
        <button class="admin-btn" style="background:#c62828;color:white;font-size:11px;padding:5px 10px;"
                onclick="deleteConsultant(${r.consultant_id}, '${safeName}', '${safePhone}')">??儭??芷</button>
      `;
    }
    return `
    <div style="background:white;border-radius:12px;padding:12px 14px;margin-bottom:10px;box-shadow:0 1px 4px rgba(0,0,0,0.04);${r.is_active ? '' : 'opacity:0.55;'}">
      <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:8px;margin-bottom:6px;">
        <div style="font-weight:700;color:#1a3a6e;font-size:15px;">
          ${r.name || '(?芸??'} <span style="color:#888;font-weight:400;font-size:11px;">#${r.consultant_id}</span>
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:4px;flex-shrink:0;">
          ${roleBadge(r)}
          ${orgTypeBadge(r.org_type)}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div style="font-size:12px;color:#444;line-height:1.7;">
        ? ${r.phone || '-'}<br>
        ? ${r.email || '<span style="color:#bbb;">(??</span>'}<br>
        ? ${r.org || '<span style="color:#bbb;">(?芸‵?桐?)</span>'}
      </div>
      <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;margin-top:8px;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap;">
        <span style="font-size:11px;color:#888;">${activeBadge(r.is_active)} 繚 撱箇???${r.created_at || '-'}</span>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;flex-shrink:0;">${actions}</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  `;
  }).join('');
}

// ??? ? / ?憿批?撣唾? ??????????????????????????????????????????????????????
async function toggleConsultantActive(cid, name) {
  if (!confirm(`閬???{name}??????嚗n嚗??典?閰脣董???⊥??餃嚗?鞈?靽??舫???嚗)) return;
  try {
    const r = await apiFetch(`/auth/consultants/${cid}/toggle-active`, { method: 'PATCH' });
    const data = await r.json().catch(() => null);
    if (!r.ok) {
      showToast(`??${(data && data.detail) || `HTTP ${r.status}`}`, 'error', 4500);
      return;
    }
    const verb = data && data.action === 'disabled' ? '撌脣??? : '撌脣???;
    showToast(`??${name} ${verb}`, 'ok');
  } catch (e) {
    showToast(`?????憭望?嚗?{e.message}`, 'error');
  }
  await renderAdminConsultants();
}

// ??? 瘞訾??芷憿批?撣唾? ????????????????????????????????????????????????????????
async function deleteConsultant(cid, name, phone) {
  // 蝚砌????箸蝣箄?
  if (!confirm(`?? 蝣箏?閬偶銋?扎“?董??嚗n\n  憪?嚗?{name}\n  ??嚗?{phone}\n  ID嚗?${cid}\n\n?賊?鞈???嚗n  ??閰脤“?遣蝡??葫??????霈??甇詨惇??鞈?靽?嚗n  ??撠??隢?????consultant_id 皜征嚗?????\n  ??憿批??祈澈?? ??瘞訾??芷\n\n甇斗?雿瘜儔??`)) return;
  // 蝚砌???閬?頛詨?????蝯Ⅱ隤?  const typed = prompt(`?粹?炊?迎?隢撓?交迨撣唾???璈?蝣潦?{phone}?Ⅱ隤?歹?`);
  if (typed === null) return;
  if (typed.trim() !== phone) {
    showToast(`??頛詨??璈?銝??湛?撌脣?瘨, 'warn', 4000);
    return;
  }
  try {
    const r = await apiFetch(`/auth/consultants/${cid}?confirm=1`, { method: 'DELETE' });
    const data = await r.json().catch(() => null);
    if (!r.ok) {
      showToast(`???芷憭望?嚗?{(data && data.detail) || `HTTP ${r.status}`}`, 'error', 5000);
      return;
    }
    showToast(`??${name} 撌脫偶銋?亡, 'ok', 4000);
  } catch (e) {
    showToast(`?????憭望?嚗?{e.message}`, 'error');
  }
  await renderAdminConsultants();
}

// ??? ?勗??????嚗? /api/v1/reports/events/sessions ??脣漲 ??????????
let _monitorTimer = null;
let _monitorExpanded = {}; // correlation_id ??bool嚗?????

function _toggleMonitorAutorefresh() {
  const cb = document.getElementById('monitor-autorefresh');
  if (_monitorTimer) { clearInterval(_monitorTimer); _monitorTimer = null; }
  if (cb && cb.checked && _currentAdminTab === 'monitor') {
    _monitorTimer = setInterval(() => { renderAdminMonitor(true); }, 5000);
  }
}

async function renderAdminMonitor(isAutoRefresh) {
  const listEl   = document.getElementById('admin-monitor-list');
  const statusEl = document.getElementById('monitor-status');
  if (!listEl) return;

  const typeFilter   = document.getElementById('monitor-filter-type')?.value || '';
  const onlyFailed   = document.getElementById('monitor-filter-failed')?.checked || false;

  if (!isAutoRefresh) {
    listEl.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:#90a3c2;">?? 頛銝?..</div>';
  }

  try {
    const params = new URLSearchParams();
    params.set('limit', '50');
    if (typeFilter) params.set('report_type', typeFilter);
    if (onlyFailed) params.set('only_failed', '1');
    const r = await apiFetch(`/reports/events/sessions?${params.toString()}`);
    if (!r.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${r.status}`);
    const data = await r.json();
    const sessions = data.sessions || [];

    if (statusEl) statusEl.textContent = `??${sessions.length} 蝑?繚 ?湔??${new Date().toLocaleTimeString('zh-TW')}`;

    if (!sessions.length) {
      listEl.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:#90a3c2;">?桀?瘝???蝝??/div>';
      return;
    }

    listEl.innerHTML = sessions.map(s => _renderMonitorSessionCard(s)).join('');
    // ?蝬?撌脣??? timeline
    Object.keys(_monitorExpanded).forEach(cid => {
      if (_monitorExpanded[cid]) _loadMonitorTimeline(cid);
    });
  } catch (e) {
    if (!isAutoRefresh) {
      listEl.innerHTML = `<div style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:#ff8a80;">??頛憭望?嚗?{e.message}</div>`;
    }
    if (statusEl) statusEl.textContent = `?湔憭望?嚗?{e.message}`;
  }
}

function _renderMonitorSessionCard(s) {
  // ??
  let badge = '';
  let badgeBg = '';
  if (s.is_failed)        { badge = '??憭望?'; badgeBg = '#c62828'; }
  else if (s.is_emailed)  { badge = '?? 撌脣?靽?; badgeBg = '#2e7d32'; }
  else if (s.is_queued)   { badge = '?? 敺祟??; badgeBg = '#f57c00'; }
  else if (s.is_done)     { badge = '??摰?'; badgeBg = '#1565c0'; }
  else                    { badge = `?? ${s.last_phase}`; badgeBg = '#37474f'; }

  // ?脣漲璇?蝡??脣漲嚗?  const progressPct = s.chapter_max ? Math.round((s.chapter_done / Math.max(s.chapter_max, 12)) * 100) : 0;
  const progressColor = s.is_failed ? '#ef5350' : (s.is_done ? '#66bb6a' : '#42a5f5');

  // ?勗?憿?銝剜?
  const typeMap = {
    life_script:  '?犖?行郭??',
    child:        '?咱?行郭??',
    parent_child: '閬芸??勗?',
    marital:      '憭怠氖?勗?',
  };
  const typeLabel = typeMap[s.report_type] || s.report_type;

  // 撌脣???N 蝡?  const chapterInfo = s.is_done ? `12 蝡?典??
                    : (s.chapter_done > 0 ? `蝡? ${s.chapter_done} / 12 摰?` : '撠??蝡?');

  // ??嚗irst_at ~ last_at嚗?  let duration = '';
  if (s.first_at && s.last_at) {
    const ms = new Date(s.last_at).getTime() - new Date(s.first_at).getTime();
    const sec = Math.round(ms / 1000);
    duration = sec < 60 ? `${sec} 蝘 : `${Math.floor(sec / 60)} ??${sec % 60} 蝘;
  }

  const cid = s.correlation_id;
  const expanded = !!_monitorExpanded[cid];

  return `
    <div style="background:#142037;border:1px solid #1f2f4e;border-radius:12px;padding:12px 14px;margin-bottom:10px;">
      <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:flex-start;gap:10px;">
        <div style="flex:1;min-width:0;">
          <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;">
            <span style="background:${badgeBg};color:white;font-size:11px;padding:3px 8px;border-radius:6px;font-weight:700;">${badge}</span>
            <span style="font-weight:700;color:#cfe1ff;font-size:14px;">${s.subject_name || '(?∪???'}</span>
            <span style="color:#7da3d6;font-size:11px;">${typeLabel} 繚 ${s.variant}</span>
          </div>
          <div style="font-size:11px;color:#8aa1c4;margin-top:6px;">
            ${s.subject_email ? `? ${s.subject_email}` : ''}
            ${s.subject_email && s.session_id ? ' 繚 ' : ''}
            ${s.session_id ? `?? session #${s.session_id}` : ''}
          </div>
          <div style="font-size:11px;color:#8aa1c4;margin-top:3px;">
            ${chapterInfo}${duration ? ` 繚 ?? ${duration}` : ''}${s.event_count ? ` 繚 ${s.event_count} 鈭辣` : ''}
          </div>
          ${s.last_error ? `<div style="background:#3a1212;border:1px solid #6a2828;color:#ffb3a8;font-size:11px;padding:6px 8px;border-radius:6px;margin-top:6px;word-break:break-all;">?? ${_escapeHtml(s.last_error)}</div>` : ''}
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:4px;flex-shrink:0;">
          <button onclick="_toggleMonitorTimeline('${cid}')" style="background:#1976d2;color:white;border:none;font-size:11px;padding:5px 10px;border-radius:6px;cursor:pointer;">${expanded ? '?嗅?' : '??蝺?}</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div style="background:#0a1426;border-radius:6px;height:6px;overflow:hidden;margin-top:8px;">
        <div style="width:${progressPct}%;height:100%;background:${progressColor};transition:width 0.3s;"></div>
      </div>
      <div id="monitor-timeline-${cid}" style="display:${expanded ? 'block' : 'none'};margin-top:10px;background:#0a1426;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 10px;font-family:monospace;font-size:10.5px;max-height:240px;overflow-y:auto;"></div>
