// 銝?菜甈∪?斗??葫閰血??async function _cleanupAllTestReports() {
  // ?? 摰?嚗皜? is_test=true ?頂蝯望葫閰血??憪??箝?皜祈??身?潘?
  // 蝯??寞活?芷?祕憪??勗?嚗甇Ｚ炊?芯?鞎餃恥?嗉???  const tests = (_adminReportsCache || []).filter(r => r.is_test);

  if (!tests.length) {
    alert('???桀?瘝??閬???蝟餌絞皜祈岫?勗?嚗????皜祈??身?潘??n\n?仿??芷?孵??勗?嚗??典?????????);
    return;
  }

  // 憿舐內撠◤?芷???株?蝞∠??∠Ⅱ隤?  const listPreview = tests.slice(0, 10).map(r =>
    `  繚 ${r.subject_name || '(?∪?蝔?'}  #${r.report_id}  ${r.report_kind_zh || r.report_kind || '?'}`
  ).join('\n');
  const moreNote = tests.length > 10 ? `\n  ??隞亙??嗡? ${tests.length - 10} 蝑 : '';

  if (!confirm(
    `?喳??芷 ${tests.length} 蝑頂蝯望葫閰血??\n\n${listPreview}${moreNote}\n\n` +
    `?? ?芸?文????皜祈?/ ?喳???/ 皜祈岫璅∪????身?潛?摮文??勗??n` +
    `?祕憪?????其??蔣?踴n\n蝣箏?蝜潛?嚗
  )) return;

  let success = 0, fail = 0;
  for (const r of tests) {
    try {
      const resp = await apiFetch(`/reports/${r.report_id}/delete-test`, { method: 'DELETE' });
      if (resp.ok) {
        success++;
      } else {
        const d = await resp.json().catch(() => ({}));
        fail++;
        console.warn(`[cleanupTest] ?勗? #${r.report_id} ?芷憭望?:`, d.detail || resp.status);
      }
    } catch(e) { fail++; }
  }
  alert(`皜?摰? ?n???芷嚗?{success} 蝑葫閰血?n憭望?嚗?{fail} 蝑);
  renderAdminReports();
}

async function _adminSendReportEmail(reportId) {
  const cached = _adminReportsCache.find(r => r.report_id === reportId);
  if (!cached) {
    alert('?曆??唳迨?勗?');
    return;
  }
  // 瘥活?質?蝞∠??∠Ⅱ隤?靽格?嗡辣 email嚗?閮剖葆?亙歇摮?靽∠拳嚗?  const inputEmail = prompt(
    `撖??#${reportId}嚗?{cached.subject_name || '?芰'}嚗n\n隢Ⅱ隤?靽格?嗡辣 Email嚗,
    cached.subject_email || ''
  );
  if (inputEmail === null) return;          // ??瘨?  if (!inputEmail.trim() || !inputEmail.includes('@')) {
    alert('隢撓?交??? Email ?啣?');
    return;
  }
  cached.subject_email = inputEmail.trim(); // ?湔敹怠?靘?蝥蝙??  const confirmMsg = `蝣箏?撖?策 ${cached.subject_email}嚗n\n?葫??${cached.subject_name || '(?芰)'}\n憿?嚗?{cached.report_kind_zh || cached.report_kind || '?'}\n\n?? 暺Ⅱ摰?隢??? PDF ?汗蝣箄??批捆甇?Ⅱ?;
  if (!confirm(confirmMsg)) return;

  try {
    const r = await apiFetch(`/reports/${reportId}/send-email`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ notify_email: cached.subject_email }),
    });
    if (!r.ok) {
      const err = await r.json().catch(() => ({}));
      alert(`??撖縑憭望?嚗?{err.detail || err.error || r.status}`);
      return;
    }
    const data = await r.json();
    alert(`??撌脣?? ${data.sent_to}嚗ethod=${data.method || '?'}嚗);
    // ?頛?”
    renderAdminReports();
  } catch (e) {
    alert(`??撖縑靘?嚗?{e.message}`);
  }
}

// 敺?Railway 敺垢???隢?憭望??????localStorage
async function _fetchAllRequestsFromBackend() {
  try {
    const r = await apiFetch('/contact-requests');
    if (!r.ok) return null;
    return await r.json();
  } catch (e) {
    return null;
  }
}

// 敺?蝡舀?郊???瘥撐?∠?閰脤＊蝷箝? 憿批?摮 / ?? 憿批?撌脣??/ ?對? ??璈歇?董??
async function _fetchSyncStatus() {
  try {
    const r = await apiFetch('/contact-requests/_sync-status');
    if (!r.ok) return null;
    return await r.json();
  } catch (e) {
    return null;
  }
}

async function renderAdminList() {
  const list = document.getElementById('admin-list');
  if (!list) return;

  list.innerHTML = `<div class="admin-empty">頛銝凌?/div>`;

  // ?芸?敺?Railway 敺垢?選?頝刻?蝵桀?甇伐?嚗仃??? localStorage
  let arr = await _fetchAllRequestsFromBackend();
  const isFromBackend = arr !== null;
  if (!isFromBackend) arr = _loadContactRequests();

  // 銝西???甇亦????其?璅內摮文?嚗?憭望?撠勗蕭??  let syncMap = {};
  let syncSummary = null;
  if (isFromBackend && _currentAdminTab === 'approved') {
    const sync = await _fetchSyncStatus();
    if (sync && sync.items) {
      sync.items.forEach(x => { syncMap[x.id] = x.state; });
      syncSummary = sync;
    }
  }

  // ??撖拇?????菜葫????撌脫?憿批??? ?其?憿舐內霅血? badge
  let consultantPhones = {}; // phone ??consultant
  if (isFromBackend && _currentAdminTab === 'pending') {
    try {
      const cr = await apiFetch('/auth/consultants');
      if (cr.ok) {
        const cs = await cr.json();
        (cs || []).forEach(c => {
          if (c.phone) consultantPhones[c.phone] = c;
        });
      }
    } catch (e) { /* ignore */ }
  }

  document.getElementById('admin-pending-count').textContent  = arr.filter(r => r.status === 'pending').length;
  document.getElementById('admin-approved-count').textContent = arr.filter(r => r.status === 'approved').length;
  document.getElementById('admin-rejected-count').textContent = arr.filter(r => r.status === 'rejected').length;

  const filtered = arr.filter(r => r.status === _currentAdminTab);
  if (filtered.length === 0) {
    const offlineHint = isFromBackend ? '' : '<br><span style="color:#c62828;font-size:12px;">?? 敺垢???憭望?嚗?憿舐內?祆??怠?鞈?</span>';
    list.innerHTML = `<div class="admin-empty">?桀?瘝?${
      _currentAdminTab==='pending'?'敺祟??:_currentAdminTab==='approved'?'撌脫??:'撌脫?蝯?
    }?隢?{offlineHint}</div>`;
    return;
  }

  // 撌脫??????撠?憿批??隢? 憿舐內?郊??? + 皜???
  let cleanupBar = '';
  if (_currentAdminTab === 'approved' && syncSummary && syncSummary.unmatched > 0) {
    const orphans    = syncSummary.orphans || 0;
    const dup        = syncSummary.dup_phone || 0;
    const dupLinked  = syncSummary.dup_linked || 0;
    const linked     = syncSummary.linked || 0;
    const parts      = [];
    if (orphans > 0)   parts.push(`<b style="color:#e65100;">${orphans}</b> 蝑迨??憿批?撌脣嚗);
    if (dup > 0)       parts.push(`<b style="color:#1565c0;">${dup}</b> 蝑?銴???璈歇?董??`);
    if (dupLinked > 0) parts.push(`<b style="color:#7b1fa2;">${dupLinked}</b> 蝑?蝝???“?◤?啁隢?隞??`);
    cleanupBar = `
      <div style="background:#fff8e1;border:1px solid #ffc107;border-radius:10px;padding:10px 14px;margin-bottom:12px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;">
        <div style="font-size:13px;color:#5d4037;line-height:1.6;">
          ?? ??郊嚗? ${linked} 蝑?撠?憿批? 繚 ?? ${parts.join(' 繚 ')}
          <br><span style="font-size:11px;color:#888;">?迨????銴??芸??隞颱?憿批?撣唾?嚗遣霅唳??誑靽???郊??/span>
        </div>
        <button class="admin-btn" style="background:#ef6c00;color:white;font-size:12px;padding:6px 12px;flex-shrink:0;" onclick="purgeOrphanRequests()">?完 銝?菜???(${syncSummary.unmatched})</button>
      </div>`;
  } else if (_currentAdminTab === 'approved' && syncSummary && syncSummary.unmatched === 0 && syncSummary.linked > 0) {
    cleanupBar = `
      <div style="background:#e8f5e9;border:1px solid #81c784;border-radius:10px;padding:8px 14px;margin-bottom:12px;font-size:12px;color:#2e7d32;">
        ????郊嚗?{syncSummary.linked} 蝑歇?詨??券撠???憿批?
      </div>`;
  }

  list.innerHTML = filtered.map(r => {
    const created = new Date(r.createdAt);
    const dt = `${created.getFullYear()}/${String(created.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')}/${String(created.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')} ${String(created.getHours()).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(created.getMinutes()).padStart(2,'0')}`;
    let actions;
    if (r.status === 'pending') {
      const phoneClean = (r.phone || '').replace(/[-\s]/g, '');
      const existingC  = consultantPhones[phoneClean];
      let warn = '';
      if (existingC) {
        warn = `<div class="row" style="margin-top:6px;color:#e65100;font-weight:600;background:#fff3e0;padding:6px 10px;border-radius:8px;font-size:12px;">
          ?? 甇斗?璈歇?舫“??#${existingC.consultant_id} ${existingC.name || ''}
          <br><span style="color:#888;font-weight:400;font-size:11px;">??????身?嗅?蝣潔蒂撖?圈靽?/span>
        </div>`;
      }
      const approveLabel = existingC ? '?? ?詨?銝阡?閮剖?蝣? : '???詨?銝血遣蝡董??;
      actions = warn + `<div class="actions">
           <button class="admin-btn approve" onclick="adminAction('${r.id}','approved')">${approveLabel}</button>
           <button class="admin-btn reject"  onclick="adminAction('${r.id}','rejected')">??</button>
         </div>`;
    } else if (r.status === 'approved') {
      const noteAdmin = (r.note_admin || '');
      const lastSent  = (noteAdmin.match(/\[email sent to [^\]]+\]/g) || []).slice(-1)[0] || '';
      const lastFail  = (noteAdmin.match(/\[email (failed|skipped):[^\]]+\]/g) || []).slice(-1)[0] || '';
      let emailLine;
      if (lastFail && (!lastSent || noteAdmin.lastIndexOf(lastFail) > noteAdmin.lastIndexOf(lastSent))) {
        emailLine = `<div class="row" style="margin-top:8px;color:#c62828;font-weight:600;">?? 撖縑憭望?嚗?{lastFail.replace(/^\[email (failed|skipped):\s*/,'').replace(/\]$/,'')}</div>`;
      } else if (lastSent) {
        emailLine = `<div class="row" style="margin-top:8px;color:#2e7d32;font-weight:600;">??撌脫??繚 撌脣??董? Email</div>`;
      } else {
        emailLine = `<div class="row" style="margin-top:8px;color:#2e7d32;font-weight:600;">??撌脫??/div>`;
      }

      // 憿批?撣唾??郊???      const state = syncMap[r.id];
      let syncLine = '';
      if (state === 'orphan_deleted' || state === 'orphan_null') {
        syncLine = `<div class="row" style="margin-top:6px;color:#e65100;font-weight:600;background:#fff3e0;padding:6px 10px;border-radius:8px;">?? 憿批?撣唾?撌脣??繚 甇斤摮文??唾?蝝??/div>`;
      } else if (state === 'dup_phone') {
        syncLine = `<div class="row" style="margin-top:6px;color:#1565c0;font-weight:600;background:#e3f2fd;padding:6px 10px;border-radius:8px;">?對? ??璈??砍歇?“?董??#${r.consultant_id || '?'} 繚 ?芸遣蝡撣唾? / ?芸?靽?/div>`;
      } else if (state === 'dup_linked') {
        syncLine = `<div class="row" style="margin-top:6px;color:#7b1fa2;font-weight:600;background:#f3e5f5;padding:6px 10px;border-radius:8px;">?? 甇斤?撌脰◤?啁隢?隞?繚 撠?憿批? #${r.consultant_id} 撌脩?湔?隢???/div>`;
      } else if (state === 'linked' && r.consultant_id) {
        syncLine = `<div class="row" style="margin-top:6px;color:#2e7d32;font-size:11px;">?? 撠?憿批? #${r.consultant_id}</div>`;
      }

      const isOrphan     = (state === 'orphan_deleted' || state === 'orphan_null');
      const isDupPhone   = (state === 'dup_phone');
      const isDupLinked  = (state === 'dup_linked');
      const isUnmatched  = isOrphan || isDupPhone || isDupLinked;
      let actionRow;
      if (isUnmatched) {
        const label =
          isOrphan    ? '??儭??芷甇斤?摮文?'
          : isDupPhone ? '??儭??芷甇斤????唾?'
          : '??儭??芷甇斤?????;
        actionRow = `<div class="actions" style="margin-top:8px;">
             <button class="admin-btn" style="background:#c62828;color:white;font-size:12px;" onclick="deleteOrphanRequest('${r.id}','${(r.name||'').replace(/'/g,'')}')">${label}</button>
           </div>`;
      } else if (r.initial_password) {
        actionRow = `<div class="actions" style="margin-top:8px;">
             <button class="admin-btn" style="background:#1976d2;color:white;" onclick="resendWelcomeEmail('${r.id}')">? ?撖迭餈縑</button>
           </div>`;
      } else {
        actionRow = `<div class="row" style="margin-top:4px;color:#888;font-size:11px;">嚗?憪?蝣澆歇鋡思蝙?刻耨?對??⊥??撖?</div>`;
      }
      actions = emailLine + syncLine + actionRow;
    } else {
      actions = `<div class="row" style="margin-top:8px;color:#c62828;font-weight:600;">??撌脫?蝯?/div>`;
    }
    const roleBadge = r.role_label || r.org_type
      ? `<span style="background:#e3f2fd;color:#1565c0;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:8px;font-size:11px;margin-left:6px;">${r.role_label || r.org_type}</span>`
      : '';
    return `<div class="admin-card">
      <div class="name">${r.name}${roleBadge} <span style="font-size:11px;color:#aaa;font-weight:400;">#${r.id}</span></div>
      <div class="meta">${dt}${r.org? ' 繚 '+r.org:''}</div>
      <div class="row">? ${r.phone}</div>
      <div class="row">? ${r.email}</div>
      ${r.ref?  `<div class="row">?? ?刻鈭綽?${r.ref}</div>`:''}
      ${r.note? `<div class="row" style="color:#888;">?? ${r.note}</div>`:''}
      ${actions}
    </div>`;
  }).join('');

  if (cleanupBar) list.innerHTML = cleanupBar + list.innerHTML;
}

// ??? 銝?菜???撠?隞颱?憿批????唾? ????????????????????????????????????????
async function purgeOrphanRequests() {
  const msg = `撠偶銋?斗???閰脤＊蝷箏撌脫???柴?????

  ??摮文??唾?嚗“?歇鋡怠?歹?
  ?????唾?嚗???撌脫?憿批?嚗?撱箸撣唾?嚗?  ???????“??憭??唾?嚗靽???唬?蝑?

?? ?唾?蝝???渡??芷嚗??臬儔??嚗?憿批??祇?摰銝?敶梢??
蝣箏?閬匱蝥?嚗;
  if (!confirm(msg)) return;
  try {
    const r = await apiFetch('/contact-requests/_purge-orphans', { method: 'POST' });
    const data = await r.json().catch(() => null);
    if (!r.ok) {
      showToast(`??皜?憭望?嚗?{(data && data.detail) || `HTTP ${r.status}`}`, 'error');
      return;
    }
    const b = data.breakdown || {};
    const parts = [];
    if (b.orphan_null)    parts.push(`${b.orphan_null} 摮文?(NULL)`);
    if (b.orphan_deleted) parts.push(`${b.orphan_deleted} 摮文?(撌脣)`);
    if (b.dup_phone)      parts.push(`${b.dup_phone} ????`);
    if (b.dup_linked)     parts.push(`${b.dup_linked} ???);
    const detail = parts.length ? `嚗?{parts.join(' + ')}嚗 : '';
    showToast(`??撌脫???${data.deleted_count} 蝑?{detail}`, 'ok', 4500);
  } catch (e) {
    showToast(`?????憭望?嚗?{e.message}`, 'error');
  }
  await renderAdminList();
}

// ??? ?桃??芷摮文??唾? ????????????????????????????????????????????????????????
async function deleteOrphanRequest(reqId, name) {
  if (!confirm(`閬?扎?{name}??#${reqId}) ??摮文??唾???\n嚗???憿批?撣唾?撌脰◤?芷嚗?舀????唾?蝝??`)) return;
  try {
    // ? _purge-orphans ?摩嚗?蝣箄?摰??臬迨????purge
    const r = await apiFetch('/contact-requests/_purge-orphans', { method: 'POST' });
    const data = await r.json().catch(() => null);
    if (!r.ok) {
      showToast(`???芷憭望?嚗?{(data && data.detail) || `HTTP ${r.status}`}`, 'error');
      return;
    }
    if ((data.deleted_ids || []).includes(reqId)) {
      showToast(`??撌脣?文迨?隢, 'ok');
    } else {
      showToast(`?? 甇斤?撌脖??摮文?嚗?賢??◤?乩犖?孵?鈭?`, 'warn');
    }
  } catch (e) {
    showToast(`?????憭望?嚗?{e.message}`, 'error');
  }
  await renderAdminList();
}

async function adminAction(id, status) {
  // 1) ?? Railway 敺垢嚗?迤????嚗?  let backendOk = false;
  let backendData = null;
  try {
    const resp = await apiFetch(`/contact-requests/${id}/${status}`, { method: 'POST' });
    if (resp.ok) {
      backendOk = true;
      backendData = await resp.json();
    } else {
      console.warn('admin action backend', resp.status);
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn('admin action backend offline', e);
  }

  // 2) ?郊?湔?祆? localStorage嚗??蝡舀?鈭??函瘜?雿?
  const arr = _loadContactRequests();
  const i = arr.findIndex(r => r.id === id);
  if (i >= 0) {
    arr[i].status = status;
    arr[i].handledAt = new Date().toISOString();
    if (backendData && backendData.initial_password) {
      arr[i].initial_password = backendData.initial_password;
    }
    _saveContactRequests(arr);
  }

  // 3) ?內蝞∠???  const targetName = (i >= 0 ? arr[i].name : (backendData && backendData.name) || id);
  if (status === 'approved') {
    let msg = `撌脫??${targetName} ?隢;
    const targetEmail = (i >= 0 ? arr[i].email : (backendData && backendData.email) || '');
    const es = backendData && backendData.email_status;
    if (es && es.ok) {
      msg += `\n\n??撣唾? + ??撖Ⅳ撌脣??嚗n${targetEmail}`;
      if (backendData.initial_password) {
        msg += `\n\n嚗恣??遢?剁???撖Ⅳ嚗?{backendData.initial_password}`;
      }
    } else if (es && !es.ok) {
      msg += `\n\n?? 撖縑憭望?嚗?{es.error || '?芰?航炊'}`;
      if (backendData.initial_password) {
        msg += `\n\n??撖Ⅳ嚗?{backendData.initial_password}\n隢??誑?嗡??孵???唾?鈭綽?銝西 Railway 閮剖? GMAIL_USER / GMAIL_APP_PASSWORD 敺??啣??;
      }
    } else if (backendData && backendData.initial_password) {
      msg += `\n\n??撖Ⅳ嚗?{backendData.initial_password}\n嚗???隞?Email ??唾?鈭綽?`;
    } else {
      msg += `\n?身撖Ⅳ撠???唾?鈭?Email`;
    }
    if (!backendOk) msg += `\n\n?? 敺垢???憭望?嚗甈∪??祆??湔嚗??唳??賣???嚗;
    alert(msg);
  } else if (!backendOk) {
    alert('?? 敺垢???憭望?嚗甈∪??祆??湔嚗??唳??賣???嚗?);
  }

  await renderAdminList();
}

async function resendWelcomeEmail(id) {
  if (!confirm('?撖迭餈縑蝯行迨?唾?鈭綽?')) return;
  try {
    const resp = await apiFetch(`/contact-requests/${id}/resend-welcome-email`, { method: 'POST' });
    const data = await resp.json().catch(() => null);
    if (!resp.ok) {
      alert(`??撖仃??${(data && data.detail) || `HTTP ${resp.status}`}`);
      return;
    }
    const es = data && data.email_status;
    const to = data && data.request && data.request.email;
    if (es && es.ok) {
      alert(`??撌脤??啣?? ${to}`);
    } else {
      alert(`?? 撖縑隞仃??${(es && es.error) || '?芰?航炊'}\n\n嚗???Railway 閮剖? GMAIL_USER / GMAIL_APP_PASSWORD嚗?瑼Ｘ?唾?鈭?Email ?臬甇?Ⅱ嚗);
    }
  } catch (e) {
    alert(`?????憭望?嚗?{e.message}`);
  }
  await renderAdminList();
}

// 敹怠?嚗??甈?render ??re-fetch
let _cachedRules = null;

function _defaultShareRules() {
  const basePct    = { franchise:25, direct:20, agent:18, project:15, staff:5 };
  const rules = {};
  _PRODUCTS.forEach(p => {
    rules[p.key] = {
      mode: 'pct',
      pct_byRole:    Object.fromEntries(_ROLES.map(r => [r.key, basePct[r.key]])),
      amount_byRole: Object.fromEntries(_ROLES.map(r => [r.key, Math.round(p.price * basePct[r.key] / 100)])),
    };
  });
  return rules;
}

function _mergeDefaults(parsed) {
  const defaults = _defaultShareRules();
  Object.keys(defaults).forEach(k => {
    if (!parsed[k]) { parsed[k] = defaults[k]; return; }
    ['pct_byRole','amount_byRole'].forEach(sub => {
      if (!parsed[k][sub]) parsed[k][sub] = defaults[k][sub];
      _ROLES.forEach(r => {
        if (typeof parsed[k][sub][r.key] !== 'number')
          parsed[k][sub][r.key] = defaults[k][sub][r.key];
      });
    });
    // ?詨捆?撘?byRole ??pct_byRole嚗?    if (parsed[k].byRole && !parsed[k].pct_byRole) {
      parsed[k].pct_byRole = parsed[k].byRole;
      delete parsed[k].byRole;
    }
    if (!parsed[k].mode) parsed[k].mode = 'pct';
  });
  return parsed;
}

async function _loadShareRulesAsync() {
  try {
    const r = await apiFetch('/share-rules');
    if (r.ok) {
      const data = await r.json();
      if (data.ok && data.rules) {
        _cachedRules = _mergeDefaults(data.rules);
        return _cachedRules;
      }
    }
  } catch(e) { /* fallback */ }
  // localStorage fallback
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem('eeg_share_rules_v2');
    if (raw) { _cachedRules = _mergeDefaults(JSON.parse(raw)); return _cachedRules; }
  } catch(e) {}
  _cachedRules = _defaultShareRules();
  return _cachedRules;
}

function _loadShareRules() {
  return _cachedRules || _defaultShareRules();
}

async function resetShareRules() {
  if (!confirm('蝣箏?閬?敺拚?閮剖?瞏方???嚗?)) return;
  _cachedRules = _defaultShareRules();
  await _persistRules(_cachedRules);
  renderShareRulesEditor();
}

async function _persistRules(rules) {
  try {
    const token = _getAuthToken();
    if (token) {
      await apiFetch('/share-rules', {
        method: 'PUT',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ rules }),
      });
    }
  } catch(e) {}
  try { localStorage.setItem('eeg_share_rules_v2', JSON.stringify(rules)); } catch(e) {}
}

function _getAuthToken() {
  try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('eeg_auth') || '{}').token || null; } catch(e) { return null; }
}

async function saveShareRules() {
  const rules = _loadShareRules();
  _PRODUCTS.forEach(p => {
    const modeBtn = document.querySelector(`.rule-mode-toggle[data-prod="${p.key}"] button.active`);
    if (modeBtn) rules[p.key].mode = modeBtn.dataset.mode;
    ['pct','amount'].forEach(kind => {
      _ROLES.forEach(r => {
        const inp = document.querySelector(`input[data-prod="${p.key}"][data-role="${r.key}"][data-kind="${kind}"]`);
        if (inp) {
          const v = parseFloat(inp.value);
          rules[p.key][`${kind}_byRole`][r.key] = isNaN(v) ? 0 : v;
        }
      });
    });
  });
  _cachedRules = rules;
  await _persistRules(rules);
  alert('???膜閬?撌脣摮蒂?郊?喃撩?');
  renderEarningsOverview();
}

function renderShareRulesEditor() {
  const rules = _loadShareRules();
  const wrap = document.getElementById('admin-rules-table');
  if (!wrap) return;
  wrap.innerHTML = _PRODUCTS.map(p => {
    const r = rules[p.key] || _defaultShareRules()[p.key];
    const mode = r.mode || 'pct';
    const modeToggle = `<div class="rule-mode-toggle" data-prod="${p.key}">
      <button data-mode="pct"    class="${mode==='pct'?'active':''}"    onclick="toggleRuleMode('${p.key}','pct')">% 瘥?</button>
      <button data-mode="amount" class="${mode==='amount'?'active':''}" onclick="toggleRuleMode('${p.key}','amount')">?箏???</button>
    </div>`;
    // ?亙??? rolesOnly ?嚗憿舐內?拍閫??雿?    const applicableRoles = p.rolesOnly
      ? _ROLES.filter(role => p.rolesOnly.includes(role.key))
      : _ROLES;
    const rows = applicableRoles.map(role => {
      const pctVal    = (r.pct_byRole    || {})[role.key] ?? 0;
      const amtVal    = (r.amount_byRole || {})[role.key] ?? 0;
      const isPct = mode === 'pct';
      return `<div class="rule-row">
        <span class="rule-role">${role.label}</span>
        <input type="number" class="rule-input" min="0" step="${isPct?'0.5':'100'}"
               data-prod="${p.key}" data-role="${role.key}" data-kind="${isPct?'pct':'amount'}"
               value="${isPct ? pctVal : amtVal}">
        <span class="rule-suffix">${isPct ? '%' : 'NT$'}</span>
      </div>`;
    }).join('');
    return `<div class="rule-card">
      <div class="rule-title">${p.upgrade?'漎?':'??'} ${p.label}
        <span style="font-size:11px;color:#888;font-weight:400;margin-left:auto;">?桀 NT$${p.price.toLocaleString()}</span>
      </div>
      ${modeToggle}
      ${rows}
    </div>`;
  }).join('');
}

function toggleRuleMode(prodKey, mode) {
  const rules = _loadShareRules();
  if (!rules[prodKey]) return;
  rules[prodKey].mode = mode;
  _cachedRules = rules;
  renderShareRulesEditor();
}

// ??? ?膜蝮質汗嚗?敺垢 /api/v1/earnings ???祕隞狡鞈? ??????????????????????
let _earningsMonth = (() => {
  const d = new Date();
  return `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')}`;
})();

function _earningsMonthAdj(delta) {
  const [y, m] = _earningsMonth.split('-').map(Number);
  const d = new Date(y, m - 1 + delta, 1);
  _earningsMonth = `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')}`;
  renderEarningsOverview();
}

function _updateEarningsMonthLabel() {
  const el = document.getElementById('admin-earnings-month');
  if (el) el.textContent = _earningsMonth;
}

async function renderEarningsOverview() {
  const list    = document.getElementById('admin-earnings-list');
  const totalEl = document.getElementById('admin-total-earnings');
  const byRoleEl = document.getElementById('admin-earnings-by-role');
  if (!list) return;

  _updateEarningsMonthLabel();
  list.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align:center;padding:24px;color:#aaa;">?? 頛銝?..</div>';
  if (totalEl) totalEl.textContent = 'NT$--';

  try {
    const r = await apiFetch(`/earnings?month=${_earningsMonth}`);
    if (!r.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${r.status}`);
    const data = await r.json();

    if (totalEl) totalEl.textContent = 'NT$' + (data.total_share||0).toLocaleString();

    // ???脣蝮?    const byRole = {};
    _ROLES.forEach(r => byRole[r.key] = 0);
    (data.by_consultant || []).forEach(c => {
      if (byRole[c.role] != null) byRole[c.role] += c.total_share;
    });
    if (byRoleEl) byRoleEl.innerHTML = _ROLES.map(r =>
      `<span style="background:rgba(255,255,255,0.1);border-radius:8px;padding:4px 10px;">
        ${r.label}嚗?b style="color:#90caf9;">NT$${(byRole[r.key]||0).toLocaleString()}</b>
      </span>`
    ).join('');

    if (!(data.items || []).length) {
      list.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:#aaa;">?祆?撠撌脖?甈曄???/div>';
      return;
    }

    // 靘“??蝯?    const groups = {};
    const groupOrder = [];
    (data.items || []).forEach(o => {
      const key = o.consultant_name || '(?芰)';
      if (!groups[key]) { groups[key] = { role: o.role, items: [], total: 0 }; groupOrder.push(key); }
      groups[key].items.push(o);
      groups[key].total += o.share || 0;
    });

    list.innerHTML = groupOrder.map((cname, gi) => {
      const g = groups[cname];
      const roleLabel = (_ROLES.find(r => r.key === g.role) || {}).label || g.role;
      const roleColor = { franchise:'#ff9800', direct:'#4caf50', agent:'#00acc1', project:'#9c27b0', staff:'#607d8b' }[g.role] || '#9e9e9e';
      const rows = g.items.map(o => {
        const prod = _PRODUCTS.find(p => p.key === o.report_type) || { label: o.report_type, upgrade: false };
        const paidDate = o.paid_at ? new Date(o.paid_at * 1000).toLocaleDateString('zh-TW') : '';
        return `<div class="earn-detail-row">
          <div style="flex:1;min-width:0;">
            <div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:600;color:#1a237e;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;">${o.subject_name||'(?芰)'}</div>
            <div style="font-size:11px;color:#888;margin-top:1px;">${prod.label}${prod.upgrade?' 漎?':''}?${paidDate}</div>
            <div style="font-size:10px;color:#aaa;">${o.rule_text}</div>
          </div>
          <div style="text-align:right;flex-shrink:0;padding-left:8px;">
            <div style="font-size:15px;font-weight:800;color:#2e7d32;">+NT$${(o.share||0).toLocaleString()}</div>
            <div style="font-size:10px;color:#aaa;">?桀 NT$${(o.amount||0).toLocaleString()}</div>
          </div>
        </div>`;
      }).join('');

      return `<div class="earn-group-card">
        <div class="earn-group-header" onclick="_toggleEarnGroup(${gi})">
          <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;flex:1;min-width:0;">
            <span style="font-size:15px;font-weight:700;color:#1a237e;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;">? ${cname}</span>
            <span style="font-size:11px;font-weight:600;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:6px;background:${roleColor}22;color:${roleColor};flex-shrink:0;">${roleLabel}</span>
            <span style="font-size:11px;color:#888;flex-shrink:0;">${g.items.length} 蝑?/span>
          </div>
          <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;flex-shrink:0;">
            <span style="font-size:16px;font-weight:800;color:#2e7d32;">NT$${g.total.toLocaleString()}</span>
            <button class="earn-toggle-btn" id="earn-toggle-${gi}" onclick="event.stopPropagation();_toggleEarnGroup(${gi})">??/button>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="earn-detail-list" id="earn-detail-${gi}">${rows}</div>
      </div>`;
    }).join('');
  } catch(e) {
    list.innerHTML = `<div style="text-align:center;padding:24px;color:#c00;">??頛憭望?嚗?{e.message}</div>`;
  }
}

function _toggleEarnGroup(gi) {
  const detail = document.getElementById(`earn-detail-${gi}`);
  const btn    = document.getElementById(`earn-toggle-${gi}`);
  if (!detail) return;
  const hidden = detail.style.display === 'none';
  detail.style.display = hidden ? '' : 'none';
  if (btn) btn.textContent = hidden ? '?? : '+';
}

// 隞狡??敺?蝯曹?頝唾??摩嚗芋??+ ?祕隞狡?梁嚗?let _payNavTimer = null;
function _onPaymentPaid(qrScreenId) {
  clearInterval(_pollTimer);
  clearInterval(_countdownTimer);
  // 皜??靽? timer嚗甇Ｗ?甈∟孛?澆???
  if (_payNavTimer) { clearTimeout(_payNavTimer); _payNavTimer = null; }

  const isUpgrade = (qrScreenId || '').startsWith('screen-qr-up-');
  const targetScreen = isUpgrade ? 'screen-upgrade-done' : 'screen-connect';

  // 蝡憿舐內隞狡???恍
  showPaymentResult("paid", qrScreenId);

  // 靽?嚗? 蝘?撘瑕頝唾?嚗甇?showPaymentResult ?折 setTimeout 憭望?嚗?  _payNavTimer = setTimeout(() => {
    _payNavTimer = null;
    _forceCloseAllOverlays();
    goto(targetScreen);
  }, 2000);
}

async function simulatePaid(qrScreenId) {
  // ??蝡?澆蝯曹??摩 ??閬?蝡?箇嚗?蝑?API
  _onPaymentPaid(qrScreenId);

  // API ?澆?刻??臬銵?fire-and-forget嚗? await嚗?  if (_currentOrderId) {
    fetch(`${API_BASE}/payments/simulate-paid/${_currentOrderId}`, { method: "POST" })
      .catch(() => { /* 敺垢?芸???敹賜 */ });
  }
}

const _t0 = document.getElementById('screen-title'); if(_t0) _t0.textContent = screens[0].label;

// Keyboard navigation
document.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
  if (e.key === 'ArrowRight' || e.key === 'ArrowDown') navNext();
  if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft' || e.key === 'ArrowUp') navPrev();
});

// ?? Auto-fit phone to viewport ??????????????????????????????????
function fitPhone() {
  // Android WebView 皛輻?璅∪?嚗SS 撌脰???JS 銝?蝮格
  if (document.documentElement.classList.contains('android-webapp')) return;

  const wrap = document.querySelector('.phone-wrap');
  const PHONE_W = 406;  // 390px + 8px border ? 2
  const PHONE_H = 860;  // 844px + 8px border ? 2
  const PAD_TOP  = 0;   // ?垢?內?歇蝘駁
  const PAD_SIDE = 80;  // room for left dots + right buttons

  const availW = window.innerWidth  - PAD_SIDE;
  const availH = window.innerHeight - PAD_TOP;

  const scale = Math.min(availW / PHONE_W, availH / PHONE_H, 1.0);
  wrap.style.zoom = scale.toFixed(4);
}

window.addEventListener('resize', fitPhone);
fitPhone();
</script>

<!-- ??????????????????Toast ?摰孵 ??????????????????-->
<div id="toast-container" class="toast-container" aria-live="polite"></div>

<script>
// ????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????
// Toast嚗陛???舀?蝷綽?top center嚗??憭梧?
// ????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????
function showToast(message, type = 'ok', durationMs = 3500) {
  const cont = document.getElementById('toast-container');
  if (!cont) { console.log('[toast]', message); return; }
  const t = document.createElement('div');
  t.className = `toast ${type}`;
  t.textContent = message;
  cont.appendChild(t);
  setTimeout(() => {
    t.classList.add('fade-out');
    setTimeout(() => t.remove(), 320);
  }, durationMs);
}

// ????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????
// 蝞∠??⊿?閮剝“??蝣?Modal
// ????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????
let _arpTargetCid  = null;
let _arpTargetName = '';

function openAdminResetPwdModal(cid, name) {
  _arpTargetCid  = cid;
  _arpTargetName = name;
  const m = document.getElementById('admin-reset-pwd-modal');
  if (!m) return;
  document.getElementById('arp-sub').textContent = `?箝?{name}?身摰??亙?蝣嬋;
  ['arp-new','arp-new2'].forEach(id => {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if (el) { el.value = ''; el.type = 'password'; }
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('#admin-reset-pwd-modal .pwd-toggle').forEach(b => {
    b.classList.remove('is-visible');
    b.textContent = '??';
  });
  _showArpError('');
  m.classList.add('show');
  m.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');
  setTimeout(() => document.getElementById('arp-new')?.focus(), 50);
}

function closeAdminResetPwdModal() {
  const m = document.getElementById('admin-reset-pwd-modal');
  if (!m) return;
  m.classList.remove('show');
  m.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
  _arpTargetCid  = null;
  _arpTargetName = '';
}

function _showArpError(msg) {
  const el = document.getElementById('arp-error');
  if (!el) return;
  if (!msg) { el.classList.remove('show'); el.textContent = ''; return; }
  el.textContent = msg;
  el.classList.add('show');
}

async function submitAdminResetPassword() {
  const newPw  = (document.getElementById('arp-new')?.value  || '').trim();
  const newPw2 = (document.getElementById('arp-new2')?.value || '').trim();
  if (!newPw)            return _showArpError('隢撓?交撖Ⅳ');
  if (newPw.length < 6)  return _showArpError('撖Ⅳ?喳? 6 蝣?);
  if (newPw !== newPw2)  return _showArpError('?拇活頛詨??蝣潔?銝??);

  const btn = document.getElementById('arp-submit-btn');
  if (btn) { btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = '?身銝凌?; }

  try {
    const r = await apiFetch(`/auth/consultants/${_arpTargetCid}/reset-password`, {
      method: 'PATCH',
      body: JSON.stringify({ new_password: newPw }),
    });
    const data = await r.json().catch(() => null);
    if (!r.ok) {
      _showArpError((data && data.detail) || `HTTP ${r.status}`);
      return;
    }
    closeAdminResetPwdModal();
    showToast(`??撌脤?閮准?{_arpTargetName}??撖Ⅳ`, 'ok');
  } catch (e) {
    _showArpError('???憭望?嚗? + e.message);
  } finally {
    if (btn) { btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = '蝣箄??身'; }
  }
}

// ????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????
// 靽格撖Ⅳ Modal嚗???/ ?? / ?漱
// ????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????
let _cpForceMode = false;  // 擐活?餃撘瑕璅∪?嚗?敺?隤芥????蝷箸???
function openChangePasswordModal({ force = false, hint = '' } = {}) {
  const m = document.getElementById('change-pwd-modal');
  if (!m) return;
  _cpForceMode = !!force;

  const subEl = document.getElementById('change-pwd-sub');
  if (subEl) {
    subEl.textContent = hint || (force
      ? '?? 擐活?餃隢??喃耨?孵?蝣潘??踹?隞犖雿輻?函???撖Ⅳ?餃'
      : '?箔?霅瑟?董???剁?隢身摰?犖撖Ⅳ'
    );
  }

  const cancelBtn = document.getElementById('cp-cancel-btn');
  if (cancelBtn) cancelBtn.textContent = force ? '蝔??牧嚗?撱箄降嚗? : '??';

  ['cp-old','cp-new','cp-new2'].forEach(id => {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if (el) { el.value = ''; el.type = 'password'; }
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('#change-pwd-modal .pwd-toggle').forEach(b => {
    b.classList.remove('is-visible');
    b.textContent = '??';
    b.setAttribute('aria-label','憿舐內撖Ⅳ');
  });
  _showCpError('');

  m.classList.add('show');
  m.setAttribute('aria-hidden','false');
  setTimeout(() => document.getElementById('cp-old')?.focus(), 50);
}

function closeChangePasswordModal() {
  if (_cpForceMode) {
    if (!confirm('?函??雿輻??撖Ⅳ嚗遣霅啁??颱耨?嫘n蝣箏?閬?敺??孵?嚗?)) return;
    showToast('??嚗隞蝙?典?憪?蝣潘?隢?敹怨?身摰???靽格撖Ⅳ????, 'warn', 5000);
  }
  const m = document.getElementById('change-pwd-modal');
  if (!m) return;
  m.classList.remove('show');
  m.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
  _cpForceMode = false;
}

function _showCpError(msg) {
  const el = document.getElementById('change-pwd-error');
  if (!el) return;
  if (!msg) { el.classList.remove('show'); el.textContent = ''; return; }
  el.textContent = msg;
  el.classList.add('show');
}

async function submitChangePassword() {
  const oldP  = (document.getElementById('cp-old')?.value  || '').trim();
  const newP  = (document.getElementById('cp-new')?.value  || '').trim();
  const newP2 = (document.getElementById('cp-new2')?.value || '').trim();

  if (!oldP)              return _showCpError('隢撓?亥?撖Ⅳ');
  if (!newP || !newP2)    return _showCpError('隢撓?亙甈⊥撖Ⅳ');
  if (newP.length < 6)    return _showCpError('?啣?蝣潸撠?6 蝣?);
  if (newP !== newP2)     return _showCpError('?拇活頛詨?撖Ⅳ銝???);
  if (newP === oldP)      return _showCpError('?啣?蝣潔??航???蝣潛??);

  const submitBtn = document.getElementById('cp-submit-btn');
  if (submitBtn) { submitBtn.disabled = true; submitBtn.textContent = '靽格銝凌?; }

  try {
    const r = await apiFetch('/auth/change-password', {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify({ old_password: oldP, new_password: newP }),
    });
    if (!r.ok) {
      const err = await r.json().catch(() => ({ detail: `HTTP ${r.status}` }));
      _showCpError(err.detail || '靽格憭望?');
      return;
    }
    _cpForceMode = false;
    closeChangePasswordModal();
    showToast('??撖Ⅳ撌脫?堆?銝活?餃隢蝙?冽撖Ⅳ', 'ok', 4000);
    // ???cache ??must_change_password 皜?
    try {
      const cached = _getAuth();
      if (cached) { cached.must_change_password = false; _saveAuth(cached); }
    } catch (e) {}
  } catch (e) {
    _showCpError('???憭望?嚗? + e.message);
  } finally {
    if (submitBtn) { submitBtn.disabled = false; submitBtn.textContent = '靽格撖Ⅳ'; }
  }
}

// ?誨????prompt() ??openChangePassword
window.openChangePassword = function() { openChangePasswordModal({ force: false }); };

// ????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????
// 閮剖????鞈? Modal
// ????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????
function _showSettingsInfo(title, bodyHtml, subText) {
  const m = document.getElementById('settings-info-modal');
  if (!m) return;
  document.getElementById('settings-info-title').textContent = title || '';
  const sub = document.getElementById('settings-info-sub');
  if (subText) { sub.textContent = subText; sub.style.display = 'block'; }
  else         { sub.textContent = '';      sub.style.display = 'none'; }
  document.getElementById('settings-info-body').innerHTML = bodyHtml || '';
  m.classList.add('show');
  m.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');
}

function closeSettingsInfoModal() {
  const m = document.getElementById('settings-info-modal');
  if (!m) return;
  m.classList.remove('show');
  m.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
}

// ? ?犖鞈?
async function openProfileModal() {
  const user = _currentUser || _getAuth();
  if (!user) { showToast('隢??餃', 'warn'); return; }
  // ?岫?瑟??啗???  let me = user;
  try {
    const r = await apiFetch('/auth/me');
    if (r.ok) me = await r.json();
  } catch (e) {}

  const rowStyle = 'padding:10px 0;border-bottom:1px solid #eef0f4;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;';
  const labelStyle = 'color:#888;font-size:12px;';
  const valStyle = 'color:#1a1a2e;font-weight:600;font-size:13px;text-align:right;max-width:60%;word-break:break-all;';
  const roleLabel = me.role === 'admin' ? '?? 蝞∠??? : '憿批?';
  const orgLine = me.org ? `<div style="${rowStyle}"><span style="${labelStyle}">?撅砍雿?/span><span style="${valStyle}">${(me.org_type ? me.org_type + ' 繚 ' : '') + me.org}</span></div>` : '';
  const html = `
    <div style="${rowStyle}"><span style="${labelStyle}">憪?</span><span style="${valStyle}">${me.name || '??}</span></div>
    <div style="${rowStyle}"><span style="${labelStyle}">頨思遢</span><span style="${valStyle}">${roleLabel}</span></div>
    <div style="${rowStyle}"><span style="${labelStyle}">??</span><span style="${valStyle}">${me.phone || '??}</span></div>
    <div style="${rowStyle}"><span style="${labelStyle}">Email</span><span style="${valStyle}">${me.email || '??}</span></div>
    ${orgLine}
    <div style="margin-top:14px;padding:10px 12px;background:#f0f4ff;border-radius:8px;font-size:12px;color:#555;line-height:1.6;">
      ? 憒?靽格憪??mail ??撅砍雿?隢蝯∠頂蝯梁恣???    </div>`;
  _showSettingsInfo('? ?犖鞈?', html);
}

// ?屁 隞狡閮?嚗??祆? _historyCache + sessionStorage 蝪⊥?敶嚗?async function openPaymentsModal() {
  _showSettingsInfo('?屁 隞狡閮?', '<div style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:#bbb;font-size:13px;">頛銝凌?/div>');

  let payments = [];
  try {
    const r = await apiFetch('/payments/my?limit=50');
    if (r.ok) {
      const data = await r.json();
      payments = data.payments || [];
    }
  } catch (e) {}

  if (payments.length === 0) {
    document.getElementById('settings-info-body').innerHTML = `
      <div style="text-align:center;padding:30px 12px;color:#bbb;">
        <div style="font-size:48px;margin-bottom:10px;">?</div>
        <div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:600;color:#666;">撠隞狡閮?</div>
        <div style="font-size:12px;margin-top:6px;line-height:1.5;">摰?擐??行郭瑼Ｘ葫隞狡敺?<br>閮???＊蝷箏?ㄐ</div>
      </div>`;
    return;
  }

  const _statusLabel = (s) => ({
    paid:    '<span style="color:#2e7d32;font-weight:600;">??撌脖?甈?/span>',
    pending: '<span style="color:#e65100;font-weight:600;">??敺?甈?/span>',
    expired: '<span style="color:#999;">??撌脤暹?</span>',
    failed:  '<span style="color:#c62828;font-weight:600;">??憭望?</span>',
    refunded:'<span style="color:#6a1b9a;font-weight:600;">?抬? 撌脤甈?/span>',
  }[s] || `<span style="color:#888;">${s}</span>`);

  const _reportLabel = (rt) => {
    const m = {
      life_trial:'鈭箇??擃???, life_full:'鈭箇??摰??, life_vip:'鈭箇??VIP??,
      child_trial:'?咱憭抵釵擃???, child_full:'?咱憭抵釵摰??, child_vip:'?咱憭抵釵VIP??,
      test_1:'NT$1?皜祈岫',
    };
    return m[rt] || rt;
  };

  const totalPaid = payments.filter(p => p.status === 'paid').reduce((s, p) => s + p.amount, 0);
  let listHtml = payments.map(p => `
    <div style="padding:12px;background:#f8f9fc;border-radius:10px;margin-bottom:8px;">
      <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:flex-start;">
        <div>
          <div style="font-weight:600;color:#1a1a2e;font-size:13px;">${p.subject_name || '??}</div>
          <div style="font-size:12px;color:#666;margin-top:2px;">${_reportLabel(p.report_type)}</div>
          ${p.invoice_no ? `<div style="font-size:11px;color:#888;margin-top:2px;">?潛巨嚗?{p.invoice_no}</div>` : ''}
          <div style="font-size:11px;color:#aaa;margin-top:2px;">${_formatTs(p.created_at)}</div>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align:right;min-width:80px;">
          <div style="font-weight:700;color:#1a1a2e;font-size:14px;">NT$${p.amount.toLocaleString()}</div>
          <div style="margin-top:4px;">${_statusLabel(p.status)}</div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>`).join('');

  document.getElementById('settings-info-body').innerHTML = `
    <div style="padding:10px 12px;background:#e8f5e9;border-radius:8px;font-size:13px;color:#2e7d32;margin-bottom:14px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;">
      <span>撌脖?甈曉?閮?/span>
      <b>NT$${totalPaid.toLocaleString()}</b>
    </div>
    ${listHtml}`;
}

// ? 撌脤?撠身??function openDevicesModal() {
  let devices = [];
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem('paired_devices');
    if (raw) devices = JSON.parse(raw) || [];
  } catch (e) {}

  // 敺?? BLE ?????皜穿???window._btDevice ????脣
  try {
    if (window._btDevice && window._btDevice.name) {
      const exists = devices.find(d => d.name === window._btDevice.name);
      if (!exists) devices.push({ name: window._btDevice.name, type: 'BLE', last_used: Date.now() });
    }
  } catch (e) {}

  if (devices.length === 0) {
    _showSettingsInfo('? 撌脤?撠身??,
      `<div style="text-align:center;padding:24px 12px;color:#bbb;">
         <div style="font-size:48px;margin-bottom:10px;">?</div>
         <div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:600;color:#666;">撠??隞颱?閮剖?</div>
         <div style="font-size:12px;margin-top:6px;line-height:1.6;color:#888;">隢??憪炎皜研?蝔葉暺?<br>???行郭閮剖??脰?????</div>
       </div>`);
    return;
  }

  let html = '';
  devices.forEach(d => {
    const last = d.last_used ? new Date(d.last_used).toLocaleString('zh-TW', { hour12: false }) : '??;
    html += `
      <div style="padding:12px;background:#f8f9fc;border-radius:10px;margin-bottom:10px;">
        <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;">
          <div>
            <div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:600;color:#1a1a2e;">? ${d.name}</div>
            <div style="font-size:11px;color:#888;margin-top:2px;">${d.type || 'BLE'} 繚 銝活雿輻 ${last}</div>
          </div>
          <span style="background:#e8f5e9;color:#2e7d32;padding:3px 10px;border-radius:10px;font-size:11px;font-weight:600;">撌脤?撠?/span>
        </div>
      </div>`;
  });
  html += `<div style="margin-top:8px;padding:10px 12px;background:#fff8e1;border-radius:8px;font-size:12px;color:#666;line-height:1.6;">?? ?亥?閫???嚗??單?璈頂蝯晞身摰???????雿?/div>`;
  _showSettingsInfo('? 撌脤?撠身??, html);
}

// ?? ?勗?摰??嚗ocalStorage ??嚗?function _getNotifPref() {
  try {
    const v = localStorage.getItem('notify_report_done');
    return v === null ? true : v === '1';
  } catch (e) { return true; }
}
function _setNotifPref(on) {
  try { localStorage.setItem('notify_report_done', on ? '1' : '0'); } catch (e) {}
}
function toggleReportNotification() {
  const cur = _getNotifPref();
  const next = !cur;
  _setNotifPref(next);
  const el = document.getElementById('settings-notify-state');
  if (el) { el.textContent = next ? '??' : '??'; el.style.color = next ? '#2D3561' : '#888'; }
  showToast(next ? '??撌脤?????' : '撌脤?????', 'ok', 2500);
}

// ?餃摰?敺?炎?交?虫?雿輻??撖Ⅳ嚗?店撘瑕敶?
function _maybePromptInitialPwdChange() {
  const cached = (typeof _getAuth === 'function') ? _getAuth() : null;
  if (cached && cached.must_change_password) {
    setTimeout(() => openChangePasswordModal({ force: true }), 400);
  }
}

// ??? ?憿舐內 & ?芸??湔瑼Ｘ ??????????????????????????????????????????????
// 敺垢 /app 蝡舫?撌脰身 no-cache header嚗?甈⊿???App ?賣?????HTML??// ?ㄐ?蕭??撅歹???/api/v1/app/version 瘥????憿舐內?刻??賭????Ⅱ隤?(async function showAppVersion() {
  try {
    const tag = document.createElement('div');
    tag.id = 'app-version-tag';
    tag.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;right:6px;bottom:6px;z-index:9999;background:rgba(0,0,0,0.45);color:#fff;font-size:10px;padding:3px 8px;border-radius:10px;font-family:monospace;pointer-events:none;';
    tag.textContent = 'v -.-.-';
    document.body.appendChild(tag);

    const r = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/app/version`, { cache: 'no-store' });
    if (r.ok) {
      const v = await r.json();
      tag.textContent = `v${v.html_version || '?'}`;
      window._appVersion = v;
    }
  } catch (e) { /* 瘝雯頝臭?銝蔣?蹂蝙??*/ }
})();

// ????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????// ?????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????
// ?? ??隤文?勗?嚗? GCS 摮文? PDF ?遣 DB 蝝??
// ?????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????
let _gcsOrphanCache = [];

async function _openRestoreModal() {
  const modal = document.getElementById('restore-report-modal');
  if (!modal) return;
  modal.style.display = 'flex';
  modal.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';
  document.getElementById('restore-result').textContent = '';

  const sel = document.getElementById('restore-gcs-url');
  sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">??頛 GCS 摮文? PDF??/option>';
  _gcsOrphanCache = [];

  try {
    // ????GCS ?券瑼? + DB ?券?勗???pdf_url
    const [gcsR, dbR] = await Promise.all([
      apiFetch('/reports/gcs-list?limit=500'),
      apiFetch('/reports/list'),
    ]);
    if (!gcsR.ok) throw new Error('GCS ?”頛憭望?');
    if (!dbR.ok)  throw new Error('DB ?勗??”頛憭望?');

    const gcsData  = await gcsR.json();
    const dbData   = await dbR.json();

    const dbUrls = new Set((dbData.reports || []).map(r => r.pdf_url).filter(Boolean));
    const allFiles = gcsData.files || [];

    // 摮文? = GCS ?B ??    _gcsOrphanCache = allFiles.filter(f => f.url && !dbUrls.has(f.url));

    if (!_gcsOrphanCache.length) {
      sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">??瘝?摮文? PDF嚗???GCS 瑼???撠? DB 蝝??</option>';
      return;
    }

    sel.innerHTML = `<option value="">???豢?閬??? PDF嚗 ${_gcsOrphanCache.length} ?迨????/option>` +
      _gcsOrphanCache.map((f, i) =>
        `<option value="${i}">${f.name || f.url.split('/').pop()} (${f.size_mb ? f.size_mb.toFixed(1)+'MB' : '?'})</option>`
      ).join('');
  } catch(e) {
    sel.innerHTML = `<option value="">??頛憭望?嚗?{e.message}</option>`;
  }
}

function _onRestoreUrlChange() {
  const sel = document.getElementById('restore-gcs-url');
  const idx = parseInt(sel.value, 10);
  if (isNaN(idx)) return;
  const f = _gcsOrphanCache[idx];
  if (!f) return;

  // 敺??皜砍?皜祈????澆??虜??????犖?行郭?勗?_xxx.pdf??
  const fname = (f.name || f.url.split('/').pop() || '').replace(/\.pdf$/i, '');
  const parts = fname.split('_');
  const guessName = parts[0] || '';
  document.getElementById('restore-subject-name').value = guessName;

  // 敺??皜砍????  let kindGuess = '';
  if (fname.includes('?咱') || fname.includes('child')) kindGuess = 'child';
  else if (fname.includes('?犖') || fname.includes('life')) kindGuess = 'life_script';
  else if (fname.includes('憭怠氖') || fname.includes('marital')) kindGuess = 'marital';
  else if (fname.includes('閬芸?') || fname.includes('parent')) kindGuess = 'parent_child';
  document.getElementById('restore-report-kind').value = kindGuess;

  document.getElementById('restore-url-hint').textContent = `URL嚗?{f.url}`;
}

async function _doRestoreReport() {
  const sel  = document.getElementById('restore-gcs-url');
  const idx  = parseInt(sel.value, 10);
  const name = document.getElementById('restore-subject-name').value.trim();
  const email = document.getElementById('restore-notify-email').value.trim();
  const kind  = document.getElementById('restore-report-kind').value;
  const resultEl = document.getElementById('restore-result');

  if (isNaN(idx)) { resultEl.textContent = '??隢??GCS 瑼?'; return; }
  const f = _gcsOrphanCache[idx];
  if (!f || !f.url) { resultEl.textContent = '???曆??圈?? GCS 瑼?'; return; }
  if (!name) { resultEl.textContent = '??隢‵?亙?皜祈???; return; }

  resultEl.textContent = '??撱箇?銝凌?;
  try {
    const resp = await apiFetch('/reports/restore-from-gcs', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        pdf_url: f.url,
        subject_name: name,
        notify_email: email || null,
        report_kind: kind || null,
      }),
    });
    const data = await resp.json().catch(() => ({}));
    if (!resp.ok) {
      resultEl.textContent = `??憭望?嚗?{data.detail || resp.status}`;
      return;
    }
    resultEl.innerHTML = `??撌脣遣蝡??#${data.report_id}嚗?{name}嚗?隢??啗??亙??銵冽?;
    // 敺迨??銵函宏?文歇??????    _gcsOrphanCache.splice(idx, 1);
    // ?湔銝??詨
    sel.remove(sel.selectedIndex);
    sel.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin',
      `<option value="">??蝜潛????游?嚗 ${_gcsOrphanCache.length} ?迨????/option>`);
    sel.selectedIndex = 0;
    document.getElementById('restore-subject-name').value = '';
    document.getElementById('restore-url-hint').textContent = '';
    renderAdminReports();
  } catch(e) {
    resultEl.textContent = `??靘?嚗?{e.message}`;
  }
}

// ?? 隞狡?葫嚗???Modal ???詨恥?????湔?脫??蝔?
// ???????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????
let _redetectCache = [];

// ?? ???勗?敹恍 Modal ????????????????????????????????????????????????????????
function openRelationModal() {
  const modal = document.getElementById('relation-modal');
  if (!modal) return;
  modal.style.display = 'flex';
  modal.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';
}
function closeRelationModal() {
  const modal = document.getElementById('relation-modal');
  if (modal) modal.style.display = 'none';
}

async function openPaidRedetectModal() {
  const modal = document.getElementById('paid-redetect-modal');
  if (!modal) return;
  modal.style.display = 'flex';
  modal.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';  // 皜 _forceCloseAllOverlays() ????pointer-events:none
  _redetectCache = [];
  document.getElementById('redetect-list').innerHTML =
    '<div style="text-align:center;padding:24px;color:#999;">?? 頛撌脖?甈暹摰?瑼Ｘ葫???/div>';
  document.getElementById('redetect-count').textContent = '頛銝凌?;
  try {
    const r = await apiFetch('/payments/admin/paid-not-detected?days=180');
    if (!r.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${r.status}`);
    const data = await r.json();
    _redetectCache = data.orders || [];
    document.getElementById('redetect-count').textContent =
      `??${_redetectCache.length} 蝑??葫`;
    _filterRedetectList();
  } catch (e) {
    document.getElementById('redetect-list').innerHTML =
      `<div style="text-align:center;padding:24px;color:#c00;">??頛憭望?嚗?{e.message}</div>`;
    document.getElementById('redetect-count').textContent = '頛憭望?';
  }
}

function closePaidRedetectModal() {
  const modal = document.getElementById('paid-redetect-modal');
  if (modal) modal.style.display = 'none';
}

function _filterRedetectList() {
  const listEl = document.getElementById('redetect-list');
  if (!listEl) return;
  const q = (document.getElementById('redetect-search')?.value || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  const rows = q
    ? _redetectCache.filter(o =>
        (o.subject_name || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        (o.subject_email || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        (o.report_type || '').toLowerCase().includes(q))
    : _redetectCache;

  if (!rows.length) {
    listEl.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align:center;padding:24px;color:#999;">??瘝?敺?皜祉?摰Ｘ</div>';
    return;
  }

  const REPORT_ZH = {
    life_script: '?犖?行郭??', child: '?咱?行郭憭抵釵',
    marital: '憭怠氖???勗?', parent_child: '閬芸????勗?',
  };
  const esc = v => String(v ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, c =>
    ({ '&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;' }[c]));

  listEl.innerHTML = rows.map((o, i) => {
    const kindZh  = REPORT_ZH[o.report_type] || (o.report_type ? o.report_type : '嚗?詨??');
    const daysAgo = o.days_since_paid != null ? `${o.days_since_paid} 憭拙?隞狡` : '?芣?隞狡蝝??;
    const reason  = o.blocked_reason || '';
    const meta    = [o.subject_age ? `${o.subject_age}甇深 : '', o.subject_gender || ''].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
    const srcBadge = o.source === 'subject'
      ? '<span style="font-size:9px;background:#e3f2fd;color:#1565c0;padding:1px 5px;border-radius:4px;font-weight:600;">?葫??/span>'
      : '<span style="font-size:9px;background:#fff3e0;color:#e65100;padding:1px 5px;border-radius:4px;font-weight:600;">隞狡蝝??/span>';
    return `
    <div style="background:#fffaf7;border:1px solid #ffe0b2;border-radius:12px;padding:12px 14px;margin-bottom:8px;">
      <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;">
        <div style="flex:1;min-width:0;">
          <div style="font-size:15px;font-weight:700;color:#bf360c;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap;">
            ? ${esc(o.subject_name || '(?芰)')}
            ${meta ? `<span style="font-size:11px;font-weight:400;color:#888;">${esc(meta)}</span>` : ''}
            ${srcBadge}
          </div>
          <div style="font-size:11px;color:#666;margin-top:3px;">
            ?? ${esc(kindZh)}
            ${o.subject_email ? ` 繚 ? ${esc(o.subject_email)}` : ''}
          </div>
          <div style="font-size:10px;color:#e65100;margin-top:2px;font-weight:600;">
            ??${esc(daysAgo)} 繚 ${esc(reason)}
          </div>
        </div>
        <button onclick="_startRedetectFromOrder(${i})"
          style="background:#e65100;color:white;border:none;padding:8px 14px;border-radius:10px;font-size:13px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;white-space:nowrap;flex-shrink:0;">
          ?? ???葫
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>`;
  }).join('');
}

function _startRedetectFromOrder(idx) {
  const rows = _redetectCache;
  const q = (document.getElementById('redetect-search')?.value || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  const filtered = q
    ? rows.filter(o =>
        (o.subject_name || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        (o.subject_email || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        (o.report_type || '').toLowerCase().includes(q))
    : rows;
  const o = filtered[idx];
  if (!o) return;
  // 敺?description ?冽 variant
  let variant = 'full';
  const desc = (o.description || o.report_variant || '').toLowerCase();
  if (desc.includes('trial') || desc.includes('擃?')) variant = 'trial';
  else if (desc.includes('vip'))                        variant = 'vip';
  else if (desc.includes('full') || desc.includes('摰')) variant = 'full';
  _launchRedetect(
    o.subject_name, o.subject_email, o.report_type, variant,
    o.subject_age, o.subject_gender, o.subject_id
  );
}

function _startRedetectManual() {
  const name = (document.getElementById('redetect-manual-name')?.value || '').trim();
  if (!name) { alert('隢撓?亙恥?嗅???); return; }
  const type = document.getElementById('redetect-manual-type')?.value || 'life_script';
  _launchRedetect(name, '', type, 'full', null, '', null);
}

function _launchRedetect(subjectName, subjectEmail, reportType, variant, subjectAge, subjectGender, subjectId) {
  // 憛怠?典?霈嚗?甇?虜隞狡敺?瘚?摰銝??  _currentSubjectName   = subjectName || '?葫??;
  _currentSubjectEmail  = subjectEmail || '';
  _currentSubjectAge    = (subjectAge != null && subjectAge !== '') ? parseInt(subjectAge, 10) : null;
  _currentSubjectGender = subjectGender || '';
  _currentSubjectId     = subjectId || null;
  _currentReportType    = reportType || 'life_script';
  _currentReportVariant = variant || 'full';
  _currentReportSubType = reportType || 'life_script';
  _eegTestMode          = false;   // 蝣箔?韏唳迤撘?蝔?摮?DB + 閫貊?勗???嚗?
  closePaidRedetectModal();

  // 憿舐內蝣箄??內
  const REPORT_ZH = {
    life_script:'?犖?行郭??', child:'?咱?行郭憭抵釵',
    marital:'憭怠氖???勗?', parent_child:'閬芸????勗?',
  };
  const kindZh = REPORT_ZH[reportType] || reportType;
  const confirmed = confirm(
    `?? 隞狡?葫蝣箄?\n\n` +
    `摰Ｘ嚗?{_currentSubjectName}\n` +
    `?勗?嚗?{kindZh}嚗?{variant}嚗n\n` +
    `蝣箄?敺??湔?脣?行郭?⊿??恍嚗n` +
    `?⊿?摰?敺頂蝯梯???亥??澈銝衣???n\n` +
    `隢Ⅱ隤瘜Ｗ?撌脤?璈蒂??典恥?園銝?暺Ⅱ摰
  );
  if (!confirmed) return;

  // ?湔頝喳????恍嚗?隞狡??敺?楝敺?
  goto('screen-connect');
}
